On synthetic structural-discrimination benchmarks, CondPSE separates graph structures that 1-WL-bounded message passing cannot: it raises CSL accuracy from 42.9% to 97.3% and EXP accuracy from 68.3% to 99.9% relative to GPSE, and ablations show that the polynomial filter bank accounts for most of this gain. On real molecular property prediction, the picture is more limited. With a hybrid local-message-passing/global-attention backbone, CondPSE performs comparably to GPSE without surpassing it, and a ZINC backbone sweep shows no consistent ordering between the two encoders. We report these results and discuss why strong synthetic structural discrimination does not, on its own, yield a downstream advantage for frozen learned PSE encoders, including the role of downstream integration and possible mismatch between structural pretraining targets and molecular property labels.

</details>


### [136] [Rethinking CD: A Reproducibility Study and Extension on the Ineffectiveness of Contrastive Decoding at Mitigating Object Hallucinations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25196)
*Arnav Bendre,Guneesh Gupta,Kavish Grover,Chayan Aggarwal,Shreyansh Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了"对比解码在MLLM中无法缓解物体幻觉"的研究，验证了CD导致判别数据集单向分布偏移、自适应似然约束将采样退化为贪心搜索等主张，并通过多项实验表明CD的改进是虚假的，不能真正增强视觉基础以减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑对比解码在缓解多模态大模型物体幻觉方面的表面性能提升，探究其是否真正改善了视觉基础，而非仅仅是评估指标上的虚假增益。

Method: 复现并扩展前人工作，通过分析判别/生成数据集上的logit分布、提出代理方法对比CD技术、逐层分析幻觉信号的传播机制等实验进行验证。

Result: 验证了CD在判别数据集上导致单向输出分布偏移、APC将采样退化为贪心搜索等主张；实验表明CD带来的改进是虚假的，不能持续转化为更强的视觉基础以减少幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前对比解码策略在缓解MLLM幻觉方面的有效性受到挑战，需要开发更可靠的幻觉缓解方法。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) has been proposed as a training-free strategy for mitigating object hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), with reported gains on benchmarks such as POPE. However, recent work has questioned whether these gains reflect genuine improvements in visual grounding. In this study, we reproduce and extend the findings of "The Mirage of Performance Gains: Why Contrastive Decoding Fails to Mitigate Object Hallucinations in MLLMs." Specifically, we test the claim that CD induces a unidirectional output distribution shift in discriminative datasets and examine its generalizability across datasets. We also verify that the adaptive plausibility constraint (APC) reduces sampling to greedy search on both discriminative and generative benchmarks. Beyond reproduction, we rigorously study the effects of CD across generative and discriminative datasets. We conduct several experiments that provide additional insights: we analyze the logit distributions induced by different CD strategies on generative datasets, propose a proxy method and compare its performance against CD techniques, and investigate how hallucination signals propagate through each layer of the expert and amateur models. Experimental results across MME, POPE, and CHAIR using LLaVA and Qwen validate the original claims and show that the apparent improvements from CD are often spurious and do not consistently translate into stronger visual grounding for reducing hallucinations. These findings challenge the effectiveness of current contrastive decoding strategies and motivate the development of more reliable approaches for mitigating hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [137] [Algorithmic Separation between Constant-Depth and Logarithmic-Depth Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25200)
*Yunwei Ren,Zihao Wang,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了深度为对数的网络与深度为常数的网络之间存在算法上的分离：前者可通过分层坐标下降高效学习一类具有分层傅里叶谱的布尔函数，而后者在特定子类上必然产生常数级的L²逼近误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度分离结果多集中于逼近能力层面，而算法层面的分离主要限于两层与三层网络的比较，缺乏对更深网络结构之间算法能力的理论刻画。

Method: 识别了一类具有分层傅里叶谱结构的布尔函数；证明对数深度网络可通过分层坐标下降自适应地分层重构频谱实现高效学习；构造了其子类，证明任何常数深度、多项式宽度的网络（激活函数足够正则、谱范数受控）在该子类上必然产生常数L²误差。

Result: 首次在算法层面证明了常数深度网络与对数深度网络之间的分离性。

Conclusion: 深度不仅在逼近能力上重要，在算法可学习性上也具有根本性的优势，对数深度网络能够学习常数深度网络无法有效学习的问题。

Abstract: Despite the empirical advantages of deep networks over shallow ones, theoretical depth separations largely concern approximation power, while algorithmic results are mostly limited to comparisons between two- and three-layer networks. In this work, we prove the first algorithmic separation between constant-depth and logarithmic-depth networks.
  Specifically, we identify a class of Boolean functions with hierarchically structured Fourier spectra that logarithmic-depth networks can learn efficiently using layerwise coordinate descent by reconstructing the spectra hierarchically and adaptively. We also exhibit a subclass for which every constant-depth, polynomial-width network with sufficiently regular activations and controlled spectral norms must incur constant $L^2$ approximation error under the uniform distribution over the hypercube.

</details>


### [138] [A Unified Algorithmic Framework for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning in Tabular MDPs with Shifted Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25207)
*Zheshun Wu,Renjie Zheng,Jinhang Zuo,Zenglin Xu,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了表格型MDP中的混合强化学习问题，提出MIN-UCB-VI和MAX-LCB-VI两种算法，利用细粒度偏差信息在一般转移漂移下有效利用离线数据，并提供了理论和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据可能来自转移动态发生漂移的过时环境，简单整合历史数据效果不佳，因此需要一种能在转移偏移下有效利用离线数据的混合强化学习框架。

Method: 提出统一算法框架，包含MIN-UCB-VI（遗憾最小化）和MAX-LCB-VI（最优策略识别）两种算法，利用细粒度偏差信息处理一般性转移漂移。

Result: 提供了实例依赖和实例无关的遗憾与次优间隙上界，建立了匹配的下界以证明最优性，并通过大量实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 所提框架在转移漂移下能有效利用离线数据，在理论上达到最优，实验上表现良好。

Abstract: This paper investigates a hybrid reinforcement learning setting in tabular Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), where an agent aims to learn an optimal policy by combining online interactions with a target environment and offline data from a source environment. A central challenge is that offline data may be collected from outdated environments with shifted transition dynamics, making naive integration of historical data ineffective. To address this, we propose a unified algorithmic framework featuring two algorithms: MIN-UCB-VI for regret minimization and MAX-LCB-VI for best policy identification. Both algorithms leverage fine-grained bias information to more effectively exploit offline data under general transition shifts. We provide theoretical guarantees for our framework, including both instance-dependent and independent upper bounds on regret and sub-optimality gap. Furthermore, we establish matching lower bounds to demonstrate the optimality of our approach and validate our theoretical findings through extensive experiments.

</details>


### [139] [Neurai-VN Benchmark: Standardized Machine Learning Models for Multimodal Digital Phenotyping in Mental Health Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25232)
*Quoc-Cuong Pham,Hoang-Thuy-Duong Vu,Thi-Thanh-Huong Ha,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Neurai-VN数据集构建了数字表型分析的可重复基准，针对四种精神健康分类任务评估了线性、树模型和神经网络三种基线模型，在100名越南成年人两周的高分辨率多模态数据上，跨验证折的平均F1分数达到0.56-0.71。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字表型分析在精神健康监测中潜力巨大，但由于数据集异构、预处理流程不一致，进展难以评估，亟需可重复的标准化基准。

Method: 基于Neurai-VN数据集（100名越南成年人，两周，高分辨率多模态被动感知+主动评估数据），定义四个二分类任务，采用标准化的受试者级交叉验证，评估线性模型、树模型和神经网络基线模型在不同预设特征配置下的表现。

Result: 受试者级平均F1分数：健康对照vs抑郁/临床均为0.71，健康对照vs焦虑为0.69，抑郁vs焦虑为0.56。

Conclusion: 该基准为未来多模态数字表型分析的精神健康分类研究提供了可重复的基线结果。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping (DP) using smartphones and wearable devices has shown considerable potential for mental health monitoring. However, progress remains difficult to evaluate due to heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent preprocessing pipelines. In this study, we present a reproducible benchmark built upon the Neurai-VN dataset, a high-resolution, multimodal dataset comprising passive sensing and active assessment from wearable and smartphone devices, collected from 100 Vietnamese adults over two weeks. The benchmark defines four clinically relevant binary classification tasks evaluated using standardized subject-wise cross-validation. Representative linear, tree-based, and neural baseline models are evaluated across predefined feature configurations. Mean subject-level F1 scores across five cross-validation folds reached 0.71 for Healthy Control vs. Depression and Healthy Control vs. Clinical, while Healthy Control vs. Anxiety and Depression vs. Anxiety achieved 0.69 and 0.56, respectively. These benchmark results provide reproducible baselines for future research on multimodal DP for mental health classification tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Single-Episode Optimization: Sliding-Window Aware Generative Auto-Bidding for Long-Term Advertising Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25233)
*Binglin Wu,Chuan Yue,Yingyi Zhang,Xianneng Li,Ruyue Deng,Weiru Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWAG-Bid提出了一种分层框架，通过滑动窗口感知的生成式自动出价方法，解决稀疏价值广告主在跨日耦合窗口下的效率约束问题，在AliExpress的A/B测试中取得了竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动出价方法将每天视为独立回合，但许多广告主产生的价值过于稀疏，导致每日效率比统计上不可靠，影响广告主留存。平台使用滑动窗口（W=7天）评估效率，造成跨回合耦合：每天的出价决策影响最多W个重叠窗口，因此需要预测未来市场条件来设定每日目标。

Method: 提出SWAG-Bid分层框架：1）规划层使用Masked Trajectory Model预测市场并生成候选计划，通过Multi-Window Model Predictive Control Sampling (MWMS) 及指数置信度衰减对所有重叠窗口评分；2）控制层通过Per-Step Gated Adaptive Layer Normalization (PSG-AdaLN) 状态自适应门控，结合Return-to-Go和Cost-to-Go通道传递预算和约束信息。

Result: 在AuctionNet-Sparse数据集和AliExpress在线A/B测试中，SWAG-Bid在滑动窗口评估下实现了有竞争力的约束满足和价值获取。

Conclusion: 所提出的层次化生成式自动出价框架有效解决了滑动窗口评估下的跨回合耦合问题，尤其适用于价值稀疏的广告主场景。

Abstract: Auto-bidding systems optimize bids to maximize value under efficiency constraints such as Cost-Per-Action (CPA). Existing methods treat each day as an independent episode. However, many advertisers produce value so sparsely that per-day efficiency ratios become statistically unreliable, undermining advertiser retention. Platforms therefore evaluate window-level efficiency over sliding windows of $W{=}7$ days, ensuring fair evaluation and long-term advertising effectiveness. This creates cross-episode coupling: each day's bidding decisions affect up to $W$ overlapping windows, so setting daily targets requires anticipating future market conditions. We propose SWAG-Bid (Sliding-Window Aware Generative Auto-Bidding), a hierarchical framework decomposing this challenge into episode-level planning and step-level execution. The planner uses a Masked Trajectory Model to forecast markets and generate candidate plans, scored across all overlapping windows by Multi-Window Model Predictive Control Sampling (MWMS) with exponential confidence decay. The controller adjusts reliance on this guidance through a state-adaptive gate, Per-Step Gated Adaptive Layer Normalization (PSG-AdaLN), complemented by Return-to-Go and Cost-to-Go channels carrying budget and constraint information. Experiments on AuctionNet-Sparse and online A/B tests on AliExpress show that SWAG-Bid achieves competitive constraint satisfaction and value acquisition under sliding-window evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [Bridging Compute- and Data-Optimal Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25271)
*Tian Qin,Kimia Hamidieh,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Compute-Data (CD)缩放定律，通过引入令牌有效性函数η统一了计算最优缩放和数据最优缩放，揭示了多轮重复和释义等数据扩展策略中令牌有效性的非恒定特性，证明经典计算最优分配在多数实际场景中并非最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算最优缩放定律假设无限的新鲜预训练数据供应，但现实中高质量数据增长已落后于算力增长，需要一个统一框架来桥接数据无限扩展与数据受限两种场景。

Method: 引入令牌有效性函数η量化派生令牌（多轮重复/释义）相对于新鲜令牌的价值，在14M-600M参数规模模型上使用Dolma-3语料拟合η，并基于此划分计算受限、数据受限和模型受限三种训练区间。

Result: 令牌有效性非恒定，取决于模型大小、令牌-参数比和派生数据量，且随语料扩展趋于饱和；用计算替代数据的效果呈边际递减；经典计算最优分配在多数实际场景中次优。

Conclusion: CD缩放定律将训练分为三种操作区间，表明经典计算最优策略在数据受限条件下不成立，为实际大模型训练提供了更准确的资源分配指导。

Abstract: Classical compute-optimal scaling laws assume an unbounded supply of fresh pretraining data, yet pretraining is increasingly entering a regime in which compute grows faster than the availability of high-quality data. We propose Compute-Data (CD) scaling laws, a unified framework that bridges compute-optimal scaling, where data scales freely with compute, and data-optimal scaling, where the corpus is fixed while compute can grow without bound. CD scaling extends classical scaling laws by introducing a token-effectiveness function, $η$, which quantifies the value of a derived token-produced, for example, through multi-epoch repetition or paraphrasing-relative to a fresh token, ranging from a perfect substitute to having no value. We fit $η$ for two data-expansion strategies, multi-epoch repetition and paraphrasing, across model sizes from 14M to 600M parameters using the Dolma-3 corpus. We find that token effectiveness is far from constant: it depends jointly on model size, the tokens-per-parameter ratio, and the amount of derived data, and it saturates as the corpus is expanded. The functional form of $η$ implies diminishing returns when substituting compute for data as either model size or data availability increases. It also partitions training into three operational regimes---compute-bound, data-bound, and model-bound---and shows that classical compute-optimal allocation is suboptimal across most practically relevant settings.

</details>


### [142] [HeAD-CP: Heterophily-Aware Diffused Conformal Prediction Sets for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25273)
*Phan Binh Nguyen Lam,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出DAPS在异配图上因均匀低通扩散导致性能恶化，提出基于无标签局部同配性估计的HeAD-CP系列节点级扩散变体，在十项基准测试中全面优于或持平APS，并在异配图上显著超越DAPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图感知共形预测基线DAPS采用均匀扩散系数，隐含同配性假设，在异配图上会扩大预测集尺寸（相对APS最多增加10.6%），亟需设计适应不同局部同配性水平的节点级扩散方法。

Method: 提出HeAD-CP系列方法：利用GNN softmax导出的无标签局部同配性估计来确定节点级扩散系数，包含三种变体——signed-γ（极端异配）、edge-compatibility（中等异配）、DAPS-baseline-with-correction（中高同配），均保持边际覆盖率保证。

Result: 在10个基准数据集上，HeAD-CP系列全面不高于APS（而DAPS在6个数据集上超过APS）；后验oracle在8/10数据集上显著优于DAPS（p<0.01），异配图增益最大（Texas上10.3%）；在同配数据集上DAPS优势不显著（CiteSeer上p=0.23）。

Conclusion: HeAD-CP系列有效解决了DAPS在异配图上的缺陷，但设计一个校准的无标签选择器以逼近后验oracle仍是主要待解决问题。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free uncertainty quantification, and its extension to graphs is an active research direction. Diffused Adaptive Prediction Sets (DAPS) is a widely used graph-aware diffusion baseline, propagating Adaptive Prediction Sets (APS) non-conformity scores along edges with a uniform coefficient $λ$. We identify a fundamental shortcoming of this design: the uniform low-pass diffusion presupposes graph homophily and proves detrimental on heterophilic graphs, enlarging the mean prediction-set size by up to 10.6% relative to plain APS. To mitigate this, we propose HeAD-CP, a family of node-wise diffusion variants whose coefficients are determined by a label-free local-homophily estimate derived from the GNN softmax. Three variants, namely signed-$γ$, edge-compatibility, and a DAPS-baseline-with-correction, are most effective at extreme heterophily, intermediate heterophily, and moderate-to-high homophily, respectively, and all preserve the marginal coverage guarantee. On ten benchmarks, the HeAD-CP family stays at or below plain APS on every dataset, while DAPS exceeds APS on six. The post-hoc oracle over the family improves over DAPS on 8/10 datasets at $p<0.01$ (paired Wilcoxon), with the largest gains on heterophilic graphs (10.3% on Texas); on the two homophilic datasets where DAPS still wins (CiteSeer, PubMed), it retains a marginal advantage of at most 0.002, statistically insignificant on CiteSeer ($p=0.23$). Designing a calibrated label-free selector that approaches this oracle is the main outstanding empirical question.

</details>


### [143] [When Does Deep Representation Learning Help Single-Cell Clustering? A Sensitivity-Aware Diagnostic Benchmark for Biomedical AI Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25288)
*Nguyen Thanh Phong,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Ha Thu,Tran An Ky,Tran Hoang Thong,Le Pham Thuy Hien,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过基准测试九个聚类流程在十个真实scRNA-seq数据集上的表现，发现对比自编码器平均ARI最高（0.7872），但并未显著优于强基线；不同数据集呈现三种可复现的聚类模式，Sobol分析表明学习率和潜在维度是主要方差贡献因素；最终提供的是一个面向数据集特性和计算成本的决策框架，而非通用 superiority 主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决scRNA-seq分析中实践者面临的 recurring 工程决策问题：额外的深度表示阶段是否值得其计算和调优成本，还是经典PCA流程已经足够。

Method: 在10个真实数据集（90-5,685个细胞，19,046-41,480个基因，4-11种细胞类型）上对9种聚类流程进行诊断性基准测试，并在7个数据集上进行部分scVI V2专项比较。流程整合了Optuna超参数搜索、重复运行鲁棒性评估、Friedman/Wilcoxon-Holm/TOST统计检验以及Sobol全阶敏感性分析。

Result: 对比自编码器达到最高平均ARI（0.7872），但Holm校正检验未建立对强基线的显著优势。识别出三种可复现的聚类模式：概率VAE变体在最小数据集上有帮助，深度自编码器在具有多批次或多类型结构的中等规模数据上胜出，经典PCA在线性投影已捕获主导变异时仍有竞争力。Sobol指数显示学习率（S_T=0.70）和潜在维度（S_T=0.56）是主要方差贡献者。

Conclusion: 提供一个面向数据集特性和计算成本的决策框架，而非提出通用的 superiority 主张，以支持可持续的医疗AI管线设计。

Abstract: Single-cell ribonucleic acid sequencing (scRNA-seq) is a foundational technology for precision-medicine workflows that contribute to United Nations Sustainable Development Goal 3 on Good Health and Well-being, and unsupervised clustering is the analytical step that turns raw expression matrices into interpretable cell populations. Practitioners therefore face a recurring engineering decision: is an additional deep representation stage worth its compute and tuning cost, or do classical principal component analysis (PCA) pipelines already suffice? We address this question with a diagnostic benchmark of nine clustering pipelines on ten real datasets (90-5,685 cells, 19,046-41,480 genes, 4-11 cell types), augmented by a partial scVI V2 specialized comparison on seven datasets. The protocol integrates Optuna hyperparameter search, repeated-run robustness, Friedman/Wilcoxon-Holm/TOST testing, and Sobol total-order sensitivity analysis. The contrastive autoencoder achieved the highest mean Adjusted Rand Index (0.7872), but Holm-corrected tests did not establish dominance over the strongest baselines. Per-dataset analysis reveals three reproducible regimes: probabilistic variational autoencoder (VAE) variants help on the smallest datasets, deep autoencoders win on mid-scale data with multi-batch or many-type structure, and classical PCA pipelines remain competitive when linear projection already captures the dominant variation. Sobol indices identify learning rate ($S_T=0.70$) and latent dimensionality ($S_T=0.56$) as the dominant variance contributors, indicating where limited tuning budgets should be allocated. The contribution is therefore a dataset-aware and compute-conscious decision framework for biomedical AI pipelines supporting sustainable healthcare analytics, rather than a universal superiority claim.

</details>


### [144] [AMRD: Adaptive Multi-Teacher Relational Distillation for Lightweight Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25289)
*Yuqi Li,Yi-Cheng Lin,Xianglong Wang,Kuo Yang,Xiaoqin Feng,Yixuan Wang,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师关系蒸馏（AMRD），通过单类SVM分配每批次教师权重和关系蒸馏损失，在边缘设备上实现高效语音情感识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在边缘设备上计算成本过高，现有知识蒸馏面临教师可靠性波动和忽略样本间关系结构的问题。

Method: 1) 对每个教师的logit相似度矩阵使用单类SVM分配批次级权重；2) 引入关系蒸馏损失对齐师生相似度矩阵。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和CREMA-D上，AMRD在多数设置下优于单教师蒸馏基线，消融实验证实两个组件具有互补增益。

Conclusion: AMRD有效解决了多教师蒸馏中的教师可靠性差异和结构信息丢失问题，提升了边缘设备SER性能。

Abstract: On-device speech emotion recognition (SER) is critical for real-time applications, yet large self-supervised models that excel at SER are too costly for edge devices. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation can compress them into a lightweight student, but two challenges remain: teacher reliability varies across batches, and logit-level distillation ignores inter-sample relational structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-teacher Relational Distillation (AMRD) to address both. A one-class SVM on each teacher's logit similarity matrix assigns per-batch weights favoring more coherent teachers. A relational distillation loss aligns teacher and student similarity matrices, capturing structure that logit matching misses. On IEMOCAP and CREMA-D datasets across four student architectures, AMRD outperforms single-teacher distillation baselines in most settings, and ablations confirm both components yield complementary gains.

</details>


### [145] [Breaking the Periodicity Assumption: Robust Tensorial Multi-View Clustering via Graph-Spectral Low-Rank Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25295)
*Jintian Ji,Xingsu Li,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有t-SVD张量多视图聚类方法对样本排列顺序的敏感性缺陷，指出其性能优势部分源于隐式的"周期性假设"而非真正的高阶结构建模，并提出基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的数据驱动图谱低秩张量学习框架来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的t-SVD多视图聚类框架广泛采用FFT对样本模式施加频域低秩约束，但作者发现该设计依赖于样本排列顺序隐含的"周期性假设"。当样本按类别排序时产生人工局部连续性，有利于FFT低秩正则化；一旦随机打乱顺序，性能严重下降。这种对类别顺序的敏感性违背了聚类任务应有的置换不变性，意味着部分性能源于特权样本排列而非真正的高阶结构建模。

Method: 1) 系统分析了样本排列顺序对t-SVD TMC方法性能影响的底层代数和谱机制；2) 提出基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架，用数据驱动的图谱基替换沿样本模式的固定傅里叶基，从而捕获流形内在结构而不依赖特定样本排序；3) 开发了锚点变体以高效处理大规模数据集。

Result: 在各种基准数据集上的大量实验验证了发现的问题，并证明所提方法相比现有最先进的TMC方法具有竞争性或更优的性能。

Conclusion: t-SVD TMC方法存在被忽视的样本排列顺序敏感性缺陷，基于GFT的图谱低秩张量学习框架能有效消除该依赖，更真实地建模高阶关联结构，实现置换不变的多视图聚类。

Abstract: Tensorial multi-view clustering (TMC) has achieved strong performance due to its ability to capture high-order correlations across multiple views. Most existing t-SVD-based TMC frameworks apply the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) along the sample mode to impose frequency-domain low-rank constraints. However, we reveal that this widely adopted design critically relies on an implicit ``periodicity assumption'' induced by the sample arrangement. When samples are ordered by class, neighboring indices tend to be semantically similar, creating artificial local continuity along the sample mode and a favorable spectral structure for FFT-based low-rank regularization. Once this ordering is removed by random permutation, existing t-SVD-based TMC methods suffer severe performance degradation. This strong sensitivity to class ordering conflicts with the permutation-invariant nature of clustering and indicates that part of the reported performance may be attributed to a privileged sample arrangement rather than genuine high-order structure modeling. In this paper, we systematically investigate this phenomenon and its underlying algebraic and spectral mechanisms. To address this fundamental flaw, we further propose a graph-spectral low-rank tensor learning framework based on the Graph Fourier Transform (GFT), which replaces the fixed Fourier basis along the sample mode with a data-driven graph spectral basis, thereby capturing the intrinsic manifold structure without relying on a particular sample ordering. Moreover, we develop an anchor-based variant to address large-scale datasets efficiently. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks validate our findings and demonstrate the competitive or superior performance of the proposed methods compared with state-of-the-art TMC approaches.

</details>


### [146] [Zhinv: Real-time hub-height wind field reconstruction using only local sparse observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25298)
*Zongwei Zhang,Chin Chun Ooi,Lianlei Lin,Sheng Gao,Tiantian He,Yew Soon Ong,Junkai Wang,Hangyi Yu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hanqing Zhao,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Zhinv框架，能从稀疏、离散的不规则风观测数据直接端到端重建高分辨率网格风场，在多个地区实验中将误差降低约66%，支持直接风资源评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例风电并网需要细粒度的区域风场信息，但实际获得的往往是稀疏、离散、不规则分布的局部观测数据，难以满足风电调控、资源评估和低空环境感知等任务的需求。

Method: 提出Zhinv，一个端到端重建框架，直接将稀疏不规则观测数据编织成轮毂高度处的细网格风场。

Result: 在东北中国、欧洲和东南亚地区的实验中，Zhinv能从稀疏观测中准确、鲁棒、高效地重建细网格风场，相比Kriging方法降低约66%的误差。

Conclusion: Zhinv使风电中心能够绕过NWP和复杂同化过程，直接利用本地可用数据进行实时风资源评估。

Abstract: The high proportion of wind power connected to the grid places higher demands on fine-grained knowledge of regional wind fields. Since the wind information directly obtainable in actual operations is mostly sparse, discrete, and irregularly distributed local observations, it is difficult to directly meet the needs of tasks such as wind power regulation, wind resource assessment, and low-altitude environmental perception of continuous regional wind fields. Therefore, we propose Zhinv, an end-to-end reconstruction framework that directly weaves sparse and irregular observations into a fine-grid wind field at hub-height. Experiments in Northeast China, Europe, and Southeast Asia demonstrate that Zhinv can accurately, robustly, and efficiently reconstruct fine-grid wind fields from sparse observations, reducing the error by about 66% compared with Kriging. With local wind-power observations as input, Zhinv enables wind power centers to bypass NWP and complex assimilation processes, supporting direct and real-time wind resource assessment from locally available data.

</details>


### [147] [Retraction-Free Optimization over the Stiefel Manifold for the LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25299)
*Yuan Zhang,Jiang Hu,Zhijian Lai,Lin Lin,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免收缩、免惩罚参数的直接着陆算法用于Stiefel流形优化，具有最佳已知迭代复杂度，并将其应用于大语言模型的LoRA微调（Manifold-LoRA），通过几何加速实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Stiefel流形优化方法要么使用收缩算子（需昂贵正交化），要么使用着陆方法（需仔细选择步长和调惩罚参数），缺乏高效且免调参的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种免收缩且免惩罚参数的直接着陆算法；利用二次罚函数的强凸-like性质和Stiefel流形的近端光滑性建立全局收敛保证；将LoRA微调重构成流形优化问题，设计Manifold-LoRA框架并搭配精心设计的步长策略。

Result: 在常步长和递减步长下均达到最佳已知迭代复杂度；在基准数据集上的数值实验展示了高效性和强下游性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在理论上具有最优复杂度保证，在实践中通过Manifold-LoRA实现了对大语言模型的高效几何加速微调。

Abstract: Optimization over the Stiefel manifold plays a significant role in various machine learning tasks. Existing methods either use the retraction operators, requiring costly orthonormalization for large-scale matrices, or employ landing methods that rely on careful step size selection and penalty parameter tuning. To address these challenges, we propose a retraction-free and penalty parameter-free algorithm that directly lands on the manifold. By leveraging the strongly-convex-like property of the quadratic penalty function and the proximal smoothness of the Stiefel manifold, we establish global convergence guarantees with the best-known iteration complexities under both constant and diminishing step sizes. Then, we reformulate the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning problem for large language models as a manifold optimization problem, introducing Manifold-LoRA for geometry-accelerated adaptation. This approach employs the proposed landing technique and a carefully designed step size strategy to accelerate the training process. Numerical experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the efficiency and strong downstream performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [148] [Guiding Posterior Exploration with Optimizer-Derived Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25312)
*Moritz Schlager,Emanuel Sommer,Thomas Möllenhoff,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用AdamW等自适应优化器在热启动阶段计算出的曲率估计（二阶几何信息）来预调节贝叶斯神经网络的采样过程，在不增加额外计算成本的前提下，显著减少或消除采样预热阶段，提升数值稳定性，并保持或改善预测性能与不确定性量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络中基于采样的不确定性量化方法虽理论上严谨，但受限于高维、多模态后验分布的高计算成本。现有的贝叶斯深度集成（热启动策略）虽有效，但采样预热阶段仍耗时。本文旨在利用热启动阶段已有优化器的副产品——曲率信息，以零额外成本加速采样。

Method: 提出一种基于优化器导出几何结构的预调节采样策略：在AdamW等自适应优化器的热启动过程中，利用其累积的梯度二阶矩（曲率估计）构造预调节矩阵，指导后续MCMC采样步长和方向，减少或消除采样预热阶段。

Result: 该方法在不增加额外计算成本的前提下，显著减少或消除了冗长的采样预热阶段，提升了数值稳定性，并在多个数据集和网络架构上一致地保持或改善了预测性能与不确定性量化质量。

Conclusion: 优化器导出的几何信息可作为低成本且高效的预调节器，用于加速贝叶斯神经网络的后验采样，为在保持不确定性量化质量的同时降低计算开销提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Sampling-based methods offer a principled approach to uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks. Their practical use, however, is often challenged by the computational cost of exploring high-dimensional and multimodal posterior distributions. To overcome these difficulties, Bayesian Deep Ensembles, i.e., warmstarting the sampling from several optimized solutions, have proven to be an effective strategy. In this paper, we demonstrate that curvature estimates computed during the warmstart as a byproduct in adaptive optimizers such as AdamW can inform the sampling phase at negligible additional cost. Specifically, our proposed preconditioned sampling strategy based on optimizer-derived geometries can substantially reduce or even eliminate the need for a lengthy sampling burn-in phase and leads to greater numerical stability. This approach consistently maintains or improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification without any additional computational costs. We confirm the consistency of our findings across various datasets and network architectures.

</details>


### [149] [Explainable AI for Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction Using Simulated Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25348)
*Md Zahid Hasan Ontor,Md Al Amin,Anik Dev Nath,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用联邦学习结合VotingClassifier（随机森林、AdaBoost、XGBoost）预测慢性肾病，通过GridSearchCV优化客户端模型，并引入可解释AI技术，最终全局模型平均准确率达99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病早期检测对预防严重并发症至关重要，但传统集中式机器学习面临数据隐私问题。本研究旨在探索联邦学习框架下，能否在保护数据隐私的同时实现高精度CKD预测，并增强模型的可解释性。

Method: 联邦学习框架中集成VotingClassifier，客户端使用Random Forest、AdaBoost、XGBoost三种基础模型并通过GridSearchCV优化，全局服务器集成各客户端模型进行预测；同时引入XAI技术解释预测机制。

Result: 全局模型平均准确率达到99%，表明该联邦学习框架在CKD预测上性能优异。

Conclusion: 可解释的联邦学习模型在支持早期CKD诊断方面具有巨大潜力，能够推动数据驱动的医疗解决方案发展，在保护隐私的同时实现高精度预测。

Abstract: Chronic Kidney Disease (CKD), characterized by the gradual loss of kidney function, remains a significant public health challenge. Early detection is crucial for preventing severe complications and enhancing patient outcomes. In this study, Federated Learning (FL) with a VotingClassifier was used to predict CKD using a clinical dataset, where Random Forest, AdaBoost, and XGBoost were utilized to compare and identify the best-fitting model for the global server. Additionally, GridSearchCV was applied to optimize the models' performance on the client's side. To enhance model transparency and trustworthiness, explainable AI (XAI) techniques were incorporated to interpret the prediction mechanisms. The global model's average accuracy was 99%, highlighting the potential of interpretable FL models in supporting early CKD diagnosis and advancing data-driven healthcare solutions.

</details>


### [150] [Raven: High-Recall Sequence Modeling with Sparse Memory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25357)
*Arshia Afzal,Aviv Bick,Eric P. Xing,Volkan Cevher,Albert Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Raven是一种线性时间序列模型，通过维护固定数量的记忆槽位并采用学习得到的、依赖于输入的动态路由机制，在每一步只衰减和更新选中的子集，从而在密集状态更新（如SSM）和稀疏窗口写入（如SWA）之间取得平衡，显著提升长上下文召回能力，并在召回密集型基准测试中达到或超越现有线性时间基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性时间序列模型在长上下文召回方面存在两难困境：基于状态的模型（如SSM和线性Transformer）采用密集写入方式，每到来一个token就更新整个状态，导致干扰和特定历史token难以恢复；而滑动窗口注意力（SWA）采用稀疏写入，仅存储固定窗口内的显式token表示，一旦相关token被移出窗口则召回能力骤降。作者希望设计一种能兼顾两者优点、克服各自缺点的模型。

Method: Raven维护一组固定的记忆槽位，在每个时间步，通过学习得到的、输入依赖的路由机制，只选择并更新一个子集，同时衰减其余槽位。这种方法在写入密度上介于SSM的全局密集更新和SWA的固定窗口稀疏写入之间，实现了选择性写入。

Result: 在召回密集型基准测试中，Raven达到或超越了现有线性时间基线模型的性能；在SSM和SWA性能显著下降的长上下文召回任务上，Raven表现强劲；能够外推到训练长度的16倍；在混合架构中也有类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: Raven通过可学习的输入依赖路由机制，有效缓解了SWA的基于位置的覆盖和硬驱逐问题，同时减少了SSM密集状态更新带来的干扰，从而更好地保持长距离内容。该模型在长上下文召回方面具有显著优势，且具有良好的外推能力。

Abstract: Long-context recall in linear-time sequence models highlights a tradeoff in how they write to memory. State-based linear models, such as state-space models (SSMs) and linear Transformers, write densely, updating the entire state for each newly arrived token, which leads to interference and makes specific past tokens hard to recover. Sliding-window attention (SWA) exhibits the opposite behavior: it writes sparsely by storing explicit token representations, but only within a fixed window, so recall drops once the relevant token is evicted. Interpolating between these models, we introduce Raven, a linear-time sequence model that maintains a fixed set of memory slots and, at each step, decays and updates only a selected subset via learned, input-dependent routing. This lets Raven mitigate SWA's position-based overwriting and hard eviction while reducing interference from dense state updates in SSMs, thereby preserving long-range content much more effectively. Across recall-intensive benchmarks, Raven is competitive with or outperforms prior linear-time baselines, achieving strong long-context recall where both SWA and SSMs sharply degrade. It remains effective when extrapolating to context lengths as large as 16x its training length, with similar gains in hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [151] [Rethinking Likelihood distributions: Student's t Likelihood Boosts Bayesian Neural Network Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25376)
*Pei-Hsuan Hsia,Lars H. Heyen,Arvid Weyrauch,Markus Goetz,Achim Streit,Sebastian Krumscheid,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝叶斯神经网络中变分推断的似然分布假设，发现学生t分布相比高斯分布能提供更好的预测性能，且不受数据分布和网络架构影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出BNN中常见的高斯分布假设可能存在模型误指定风险，但以往工作主要关注先验分布的替代选择，而似然分布的分布选择效应尚待探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在标准多层感知机（MLP）上，对比多种似然分布假设（如偏态分布、重尾分布）在人工和真实数据集上的回归任务表现，系统评估不同分布假设（包括高斯和学生t分布）的预测性能。

Result: 学生t分布在不同数据分布和MLP架构（深度和宽度）下均优于高斯似然分布，在某些情况下还能缩短训练时间，且易于实现。

Conclusion: 学生t分布是BNN变分推断中比高斯分布更优的似然分布选择，具有更好的预测鲁棒性和计算效率。

Abstract: In Bayesian neural networks (BNNs), variational inference is a widely adopted framework for modeling uncertainty in a distributional way, with the evidence lower bound (ELBO) serving as the standard objective function. Several distributions contribute to the ELBO loss, such as the prior, approximated posterior, and likelihood distribution. Typically, these distributions are all approximated by a Gaussian distribution, since it is easy to compute, allows for reparameterized gradients, and provides a closed-form loss for training. However, several works have highlighted that this assumption may not generally hold, posing the risk of model misspecification. Alternative distributions have been proposed for the prior specifically, while the effect of distribution choice on the likelihood distribution remains unexplored. In this work, our aim is to close this gap by investigating whether alternative assumptions for the likelihood distribution can outperform the commonly used Gaussian. We compare several likelihood distribution assumptions, such as skewed or heavy-tailed, across regression tasks on both artificial and real-world datasets using standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Our findings demonstrate that Student's t yields better predictive performance than a Gaussian likelihood distribution, independent of the data distribution and MLP architecture (depth and width). In some cases, Student's t can also lead to shorter training times, while still being easy to implement.

</details>


### [152] [Learned, Relied Upon, or Necessary? Separating Checkpoint Dependence from Task-Level Value in Sheaf GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25387)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在层图神经网络中学到的限制映射（restriction maps）常被当作模型发现了有用边几何的证据，但这种推断不成立。作者提出两种估计量（checkpoint reliance和protocol-relative replacement），通过任务零定理、精确框架模型和实验证明：学到的映射可以支配已拟合的计算，但并不构成不可或缺的边几何。因此，关于学到传输的结论应同时结合检查点干预和匹配重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作错误地将层图神经网络中学到的限制映射视为模型发现了有用边几何的证据，但参数移动和后验消融等方法无法区分映射是否在模型适应后仍然不可或缺。本文旨在澄清这一混淆，提出严格的因果估计框架来分离两个不同的问题：固定检查点下映射是否重要 vs. 映射在重训练后是否仍不可替代。

Method: 提出两种估计量：Checkpoint reliance（对固定预测器的映射进行干预）和 Protocol-relative replacement（重新训练匹配的模型族，移除映射容量、边变异性或持久边分配）。通过任务零定理证明标签只能识别被传输的分类器方向，留下d²-d个不可见的自由度。构建精确框架模型给出依赖变为不可替代任务值的边界。在NSD、DNSD、DSNN等真实图数据集上进行标签仅训练和审计实验。

Result: 所有五个DNSD基准在固定检查点下均表现出依赖（checkpoint reliance）。重训练后，去除边分配或使用共享映射的控制方法在四个基准上恢复了完整性能；Roman-Empire数据集上仍有0.0675的持续重采样分配优势和0.0391的参数匹配共享映射优势。

Conclusion: 学到的限制映射可以支配已拟合的计算，但不一定构成不可或缺的边几何。研究者应将检查点干预与匹配重训练配对，才能可靠地声称学到了传输/边几何。

Abstract: Learned restriction maps in sheaf graph neural networks are often treated as proof that the model has discovered useful edge geometry. That conclusion does not follow from parameter movement or from a post-hoc ablation: both can show how one checkpoint is organized while leaving open whether learned transport still helps after the rest of the model adapts. We separate these claims with two estimands. Checkpoint reliance intervenes on the maps of a fixed predictor; protocol-relative replacement retrains matched families that remove map capacity, edge variation, or persistent edge assignment. A task-null theorem shows why the claims can diverge: labels identify only the transported classifier directions, leaving $d^2-d$ invisible degrees of freedom in every full $d\times d$ map. An exact frame model then gives the boundary at which reliance becomes unreplaced task value. Label-only training realizes the predicted separation, while audits of public NSD, DNSD, and Directed Sheaf Neural Network (DSNN) implementations recover both replaceable and unreplaced transport regimes on real graphs. All five DNSD benchmarks exhibit fixed-checkpoint reliance. After retraining, assignment-breaking or shared-map controls recover Full performance on four; Roman-Empire retains a $.0675$ advantage over continually resampled assignment and a $.0391$ advantage over a parameter-matched shared map across ten official splits. Thus, a learned map can govern a fitted computation without constituting indispensable edge geometry. Claims of learned transport should pair checkpoint interventions with matched retraining.

</details>


### [153] [TWICE: Two-Clock, Two-Window Learning for Long-Horizon Conversion Prediction in Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25404)
*Kaiyuan Li,Kun Wang,Zhongbo Wang,Teng Sha,Ming Yan,Yanhua Cheng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWICE框架将长时程转化率预测分解为目标窗口转化概率和分组延迟累积分布函数，利用点击时钟和转化时钟的双重监督信号，通过在到达条件似然中引入固定点击时预测的CVR质量作为队列曝光量，解决了延迟反馈下的两时钟、两窗口学习问题。在公开基准和工业数据集上有效，并在快手广告系统中上线后提升了收益和转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告中，长时程转化预测面临延迟反馈带来的"两时钟、两窗口"问题：短基础观测窗口内的点击时钟提供及时但部分监督，而长目标转化窗口内的转化时钟揭示长尾延迟但存在历史点击队列的混合权重问题。现有方法难以有效融合这两个时钟的互补信息。

Method: TWICE将长时程点击后转化率（CVR）分解为目标窗口转化概率和分组经过延迟累积分布函数（CDF）。点击时钟记录通过当前状态似然训练目标窗口CVR头；新到达的转化在转化时钟上训练延迟模型。为解决队列混合问题，TWICE在到达条件似然中使用固定点击时预测的CVR质量（pCVR）作为队列曝光量，从而考虑不同队列流量和转化倾向的差异。训练好的CDF可为所有截止到目标窗口的时延生成单调预测，推理时无需历史查找或卷积操作。

Result: 在公开基准和工业广告数据集上验证了TWICE的有效性。在快手广告系统的在线A/B测试中，TWICE使预期收入提升2.486%，实际收入提升1.858%，转化数提升2.061%。该方法随后在全流量部署。

Conclusion: TWICE通过分解建模和双重时钟监督，有效解决了长时程转化预测中的延迟反馈与队列混合问题，生成了自包含的聚合记录，支持任意目标时窗的单调预测，并在实际工业系统中取得了显著的收益提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon conversion prediction under delayed feedback creates a two-clock, two-window learning problem in online advertising. A short base observation window releases recent clicks on the click clock before their outcomes mature, whereas conversions continue to arrive on the conversion clock throughout a longer target conversion window. The click clock provides timely but partially observed status supervision. The conversion clock reveals long-tail delays, but the delay composition within an arrival-time slice is weighted by historical click cohorts with different traffic volumes and target-window conversion rates.
  We present TWICE, a framework that factorizes long-horizon post-click conversion rate (CVR) into a target-window conversion probability and a grouped elapsed-delay cumulative distribution function (CDF). The two clocks provide complementary supervision. Click-clock records train the target-window CVR head through a current-status likelihood over the base observation window. Newly arrived conversions train the delay model on the conversion clock. To account for the cohort mixture, TWICE uses fixed click-time predicted CVR (pCVR) mass as cohort exposure in an arrival-conditioned likelihood. This accounts for differences in cohort traffic and conversion propensity. The resulting aggregate records are self-contained. A single learned CDF produces monotone predictions for all requested horizons up to the target conversion window. Serving requires neither historical lookup nor convolution. Experiments on a public benchmark and an industrial advertising dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of TWICE. In an online A/B test in Kwai's advertising system, TWICE increased expected revenue, revenue, and conversions by 2.486%, 1.858%, and 2.061%, respectively. It was subsequently deployed to full traffic.

</details>


### [154] [SPARC Segmentation to Prediction via Affine Regression and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25413)
*Shivani,Subhayan Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对B2B电商中组织买家异质性采购行为导致SMOTE过采样失效的问题，本文提出了一个生产部署的倾向性建模框架，用DiCE生成合成数据替代SMOTE，并结合PyPARC分类器输出可解释的校准概率。在两年真实数据（1:9不平衡比）上，该框架在0.8阈值下达到93.1%的精确率，比SMOTE基线提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B2B电商中组织实体的采购行为具有异质性（多模态采购周期），导致SMOTE假设的类别内特征同质性被违反，线性插值生成的少数类样本结构上无效，无法代表真实购买行为。

Method: 1) 用Diverse Counterfactual Explanations (DiCE)生成具有更高分布保真度的少数类合成数据，替代SMOTE；2) 采用PyPARC分段仿射分类框架生成校准的倾向得分，实现可解释的风险分层。

Result: 在1:9不平衡比的两年B2B电商平台数据上，所提框架在0.8决策阈值下达到93.1%精确率，相比SMOTE基线（83.9%）提升9.2个百分点；在0.7阈值下（66.04%）提升26.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架在多个操作点上均优于SMOTE基线，能够支撑高精度营销活动，显著提升客户激活率和投资回报率。

Abstract: Transaction propensity prediction in B2B e commerce presents unique challenges distinct from B2C contexts, primarily due to the heterogeneous procurement behaviors of organizational entities, which violate SMOTE's implicit assumption of within class feature homogeneity. Specifically, B2B buyers exhibit multi modal procurement cycles that render linear interpolation between minority class samples structurally invalid, producing synthetic data that does not represent real purchasing behavior. This paper introduces a production deployed propensity modeling framework designed to address these complexities through two primary contributions. First, we replace conventional SMOTE based augmentation with a synthetic data generation approach leveraging Diverse Counterfactual Explanations (DiCE). This method produces minority class samples with superior distributional fidelity compared to SMOTE, as validated through quantitative proximity analysis and UMAP cluster visualization. Second, we adapt the PyPARC piecewise affine classification framework to generate calibrated propensity probabilities, facilitating the interpretable segmentation of customers into actionable risk tiers. Evaluated on two years of longitudinal data from a large scale B2B e commerce platform with a 1 to 9 class imbalance ratio, the proposed architecture achieves 93.1% precision at a decision threshold of 0.8, a 9.2 percentage point improvement over SMOTE based baselines at the same threshold (83.9%), and a 26.1 point improvement over SMOTE at threshold 0.7 (66.04%), demonstrating consistent superiority across operating points. These results demonstrate the framework's efficacy in enabling high precision marketing campaigns with significant improvements in customer activation and return on investment.

</details>


### [155] [PIcsC: Partitioning-Induced Covariate Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25441)
*Behraj Khan,Behroz Mirza,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fisher信息的划分诱导协变量偏移校正框架PIcsC，通过Fisher信息矩阵近似划分散度作为正则项，统一处理集中式（批处理/交叉验证）和分布式（联邦学习）场景下的数据分布不匹配问题，在40+数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据划分导致的协变量偏移会偏差交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习中的模型选择与参数估计，现有方法缺乏统一的校正框架。

Method: 利用Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）近似划分散度，将计算得到的统计量作为正则项加入优化目标；引入条件自适应机制，结合FIM偏移与KL散度检测显著分布偏移，仅在必要时激活正则化。

Result: 在40+数据集上持续改进；碎片化批处理和折设置下性能退化分别降低20%+和25%+；七个联邦学习基准上超越FedAvg、FedProx和SCAFFOLD 3-5个百分点。

Conclusion: Fisher信息为缓解集中式和分布式学习中的划分诱导协变量偏移提供了有效且统一的机制。

Abstract: Covariate shift across training-data partitions biases model selection and parameter estimation in cross-validation, lifelong learning, and federated learning. We propose \textit{Partition-Induced Covariate-shift Correction} (\texttt{PIcsC}), a Fisher information-based regularization framework that mitigates distribution mismatch between data partitions and a reference distribution. \texttt{PIcsC} approximates partition divergence using the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) and incorporates the resulting statistic as a regularizer during optimization. The same formulation applies to both centrally partitioned datasets (batches or cross-validation folds) and inherently distributed data (federated clients or decentralized nodes), requiring only partition-local gradient statistics rather than raw data. We further introduce a conditional adaptation mechanism that combines FIM shift with KL divergence to detect significant distribution shifts and activates regularization only when necessary. Experiments on more than 40 datasets demonstrate consistent improvements under both natural and synthetic covariate shift. On fragmented batch and fold settings, \texttt{PIcsC} reduces fragmentation-induced performance degradation by more than 20\% and 25\%, respectively. On seven federated learning benchmarks, it consistently outperforms FedAvg, FedProx, and SCAFFOLD by 3 -5 percentage points without requiring client-specific personalization. These results demonstrate that Fisher information provides an effective and unified mechanism for mitigating partition-induced covariate shift across both centralized and distributed learning.

</details>


### [156] [Tools Are Not Islands: Set-Level Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents via Query-Conditioned Hyperedge Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25718)
*Xinyi Hong,Pinjun Dong,Xinyang Yu,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYSET，一种基于超边预测的集合级工具检索方法，通过构建工具共调用超图并学习基数相关的工具兼容性，在ToolBench上显著优于现有基线方法，且支持零样本/少样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索方法要么独立评分每个工具，要么顺序构建工具集合，未能整体评估候选集合的联合效用，导致检索结果无法充分反映工具间的协同作用。

Method: HYSET将工具检索建模为查询条件化的超边预测问题：构建工具共调用超图，以工具集合为评分单元；通过基数相关的交互函数捕获大小依赖的工具兼容性；设计为无需修改下游智能体的预选择模块。

Result: 在ToolBench上，HYSET在工具检索性能和端到端任务成功率上持续优于现有基线方法，并支持零样本/少样本迁移到未见过的工具/类别和新领域。

Conclusion: HYSET通过集合级建模有效提升了工具检索的联合效用评估能力，且作为即插即用模块具有良好的泛化性，为LLM智能体的工具检索提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on invoking external tools to complete real-world tasks. Tool retrieval, which selects a small task-relevant subset from a library of thousands of tools before the agent acts, has therefore become a critical component of LLM agent pipelines. However, existing retrievers either score each tool in isolation or assemble the tool set sequentially, so the joint utility of a candidate set is never evaluated as a whole. In this paper, we propose HYSET, short for HYperedge-based SEt-level Tool retrieval. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we formulate tool retrieval as query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, under which the tool set itself becomes the unit of scoring and most existing retrieval paradigms reduce to restricted instances; (ii) we capture size-dependent tool compatibility through cardinality-specific interactions; and (iii) we design HYSET as a pre-selection module requiring no modification to the downstream agent. Experiments on ToolBench demonstrate that HYSET consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both tool retrieval performance and end-to-end task success. Beyond the in-domain setting, HYSET further supports zero-shot/few-shot transfer, generalizing to held-out tools/categories and unseen domains with minimal supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Bits and Memories: Measuring Verbatim Extraction Across LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25451)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量化对语言模型逐字记忆提取风险的影响，发现量化会选择性遗忘记忆内容（遗忘速度超过能力下降速度），但不足以作为隐私防御手段，大模型在4比特量化下仍能复现大部分记忆序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作几乎全部使用成员推断攻击来度量量化后的隐私风险，但这与大多数人真正担心的风险（模型逐字复现训练数据）不匹配。作者认为应该直接测量提取攻击而非成员推断。

Method: 使用Pythia模型及其已知记忆的公开序列，在五个精度级别（全精度到4比特）和三种模型规模下追踪逐字提取，同时衡量通用能力（困惑度）。采用两种不相关的量化算法和两种评估语料库。

Result: 量化是一种选择性遗忘机制：逐字记忆的下降速度快于能力下降；但这种选择性不足以使量化成为隐私防御手段。在最大模型中，4比特量化仍能复现大部分记忆序列，且记忆数据存活的比率随模型规模增长。

Conclusion: 压缩不应被视为移除记忆化训练数据的方法，提取攻击（而非成员推断）才是从业者应该关注的指标。

Abstract: Language models are almost always quantized before they are deployed, and a growing line of work asks whether quantization also lowers their privacy risk. That work measures privacy almost entirely with membership inference. We think this is the wrong thing to measure for the risk that most people actually worry about, namely a model reproducing its training data word for word, and we measure that directly. Using the Pythia models and the public set of sequences each of them is known to have memorized, we track verbatim extraction across five precision levels, from full precision down to four bits, and across three model sizes, while measuring general capability (perplexity) at every point. We find two things. Quantization is a selective forgetter: verbatim memorization falls off faster than capability at every precision and every model size we tried, and this holds under two unrelated quantization algorithms and two evaluation corpora. But the selectivity is not enough to make quantization a privacy defense, which cuts against the optimistic reading of earlier membership-inference results. At the largest model we study, four-bit quantization still reproduces most of the memorized sequences while giving up only a few percent of capability, and the fraction of memorized data that survives quantization grows with model size. We conclude that compression should not be treated as a way to remove memorized training data, and that extraction, not membership inference, is the number practitioners should be watching. All code, sampled evaluation data, and per-configuration results are released.

</details>


### [158] [Emergent Latent-State Computation under Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25459)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Lulu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在多元随机波动率设定下研究序列模型（如Transformer）如何内部表征隐式的随机动力学，发现模型采用两阶段计算：隐层编码关于下一时刻潜在波动率状态的信息，输出头将其映射为平方收益预测。Transformer中潜在状态的可解码性出现在特定架构阶段，长期期模式下可简化为显式的潜在状态滤波器（线性投影+L2归一化）。部分MSE训练退化源于读出层失配而非表征失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性研究大多聚焦于语言模型和确定性任务，对于序列模型如何在噪声、部分可观测条件下内部表征隐式随机动力学知之甚少。作者希望在一个受控的随机波动率设定下填补这一空白。

Method: 在多元随机波动率环境中，模型仅观测收益序列，真实潜在波动率状态已知但隐藏于模型。跨架构（包括Transformer）、损失函数和输出头进行实验，分析隐层表征对潜在状态的可解码性，以及输出头的映射作用。特别分析了Transformer中不同架构阶段的可解码性模式，以及长期期下的简化滤波器结构。

Result: 1) 隐层表征编码了关于下一时刻潜在波动率状态的信息，输出头将其映射为平方收益预测；2) Transformer中潜在状态可解码性出现在特定架构阶段，位置依赖于波动率周期；3) 长期期模式下计算简化为"线性投影+L2归一化"的显式潜在状态滤波器；4) 噪声MSE训练下的部分性能退化源于读出层失配而非表征本身失败。

Conclusion: 随机波动率模型可作为机械可解释性在噪声隐式动力学和部分可观测条件下的有用基准测试工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks. Much less is known about how sequence models internally represent latent stochastic dynamics under noisy, partially observed observations. We study this question in a controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting, where models observe only returns while the ground-truth latent volatility state is known to the researcher. This setting provides a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under partial observability: the latent state is hidden from the model but directly available for evaluation. Across architectures, losses, and output heads, we find evidence for a two-stage computation. Hidden representations encode substantial information about the next latent volatility state, and the output head maps this representation to squared return forecasts. Furthermore, in Transformers, latent-state decodability emerges at identifiable architectural stages whose location depends on the volatility period. In long-cycle regimes, this computation simplifies into an explicit latent-state filter consisting of a learned linear projection followed by $\ell^2$ normalization. Output-head replacement further shows that part of the degradation under noisy MSE training arises from readout misalignment rather than representation failure. These results suggest that stochastic volatility models provide a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under noisy latent dynamics and partial observability.

</details>


### [159] [Data-Dependent Regret and Polyak Corrections for Constrained Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25480)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对约束在线凸优化问题，对结合在线梯度下降与Polyak可行步的方法进行了更紧的依赖数据的 regret 分析，通过引入累积梯度范数 G_T 和 Polyak 校正项 P_T，改进了标准 O(√T) 界，并提出了自适应步长方法 AdaOGD-PFS，实验提升 38-43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用需要在每一轮满足凸约束的同时最小化 regret，现有 OGD-Polyak 方法虽高效但标准分析忽略了数据依赖信息，导致 regret 界不够紧。

Method: 将标准 worst-case 分析中省略的两个量纳入：一是用观测累积梯度范数 G_T = Σ||∇f_t(x_t)||² 替换梯度上界 G_f²T；二是识别非负的 Polyak 校正项 P_T，衡量可行投影导致的累积平方位移并以负号进入 regret 界。在此基础上提出自适应步长 AdaOGD-PFS。

Result: 得到非负的改进量 Δ_T = (η/2)(G_f²T - G_T) + P_T/(2η)；AdaOGD-PFS 达到 O(√G_T) regret 同时保持每轮可行；球约束和半空间约束实验提升 38-43%，梯度与 Polyak 校正均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 数据依赖分析能得到更紧的 regret 界，Polyak 校正项不仅保证可行性和稳定性，还能从理论上有益于 regret 上界。

Abstract: Constrained online convex optimization requires minimizing regret against adversarial convex costs while satisfying a convex constraint at every round, as needed in safety-critical applications. A computationally efficient method combines online gradient descent with a Polyak feasibility step, using one constraint evaluation and one subgradient per round. Although this method achieves O(sqrt(T)) regret with per-round feasibility, we derive a tighter, data-dependent analysis by retaining two quantities omitted by the standard worst-case argument. First, we replace the gradient envelope G_f^2 T with the observed accumulation G_T = sum_t ||grad f_t(x_t)||^2. Second, we identify a nonnegative Polyak correction P_T that measures the cumulative squared displacement caused by feasibility projections and enters the regret bound with a negative sign. The resulting improvement, Delta_T = (eta/2)(G_f^2 T - G_T) + P_T/(2 eta), is always nonnegative. We further propose AdaOGD-PFS, an adaptive-step-size method that achieves O(sqrt(G_T)) regret while preserving per-round feasibility. Experiments on ball- and halfspace-constrained problems improve the regret bound by 38 to 43 percent, with both data-dependent gradients and Polyak corrections contributing substantially.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Speedups for Stochastic Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25492)
*Bin Luo,Chengchang Liu,Jonathan Allcock,Shengyu Zhang,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾梯度噪声的随机优化问题，提出了量子均值估计器（在低维场景下查询复杂度优于经典最优方法）和基于此的量子归一化随机梯度下降法（QNSGD）与量子投影随机梯度下降法（QPSGD），在非凸和凸问题上均实现了比经典下限更优的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典随机优化方法在处理重尾梯度噪声时存在查询复杂度下限瓶颈，而量子计算有望突破这一限制，特别是在低维场景下实现加速。

Method: 1) 提出一种针对多元重尾随机变量的量子均值估计器，在低维场景下查询复杂度优于经典最优估计器；2) 应用广义多层蒙特卡罗技术构造无偏量子均值估计器；3) 基于上述估计器提出QNSGD（非凸优化）和QPSGD（凸优化）方法。

Result: 非凸问题：QNSGD达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt d\,\varepsilon^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}})$查询复杂度；凸问题：QPSGD达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt d\,\varepsilon^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+\varepsilon^{-2})$期望查询复杂度。两者在低维场景下均优于经典下限。

Conclusion: 量子方法能在重尾梯度噪声的随机优化中实现加速，低维场景下量子估计器最优（对数因子内），且维度依赖的下界表明低维场景下维度影响不可避免。

Abstract: We study stochastic optimization with heavy-tailed gradient noise. We first propose a novel quantum mean estimator for multivariate heavy-tailed random variables that achieves lower query complexity than optimal classical estimators in the low-dimensional regime. We further develop an unbiased quantum mean estimator by applying a generalized multi-level Monte Carlo technique. We prove quantum lower bounds showing that, when the dimension $d$ of the random vector is small and can be viewed as a constant, our quantum estimators are optimal up to logarithmic factors. We further derive stronger dimension-dependent lower bounds for tail index $p>4/3$, showing that a nontrivial dependence on the dimension is unavoidable in the low-dimensional regime. Based on these estimators, we propose a quantum normalized stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QNSGD}$), which finds an $ε$-stationary point using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}}\big)$ queries to the quantum stochastic gradient oracle. For a convex objective function, we propose a quantum projected stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QPSGD}$), which computes a solution with $ε$-optimal solution using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+ε^{-2}\big)$ queries in expectation. These sharper bounds improve upon the classical lower bounds $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}}\big)$ for nonconvex problems and $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}\big)$ for convex problems in the low-dimensional regimes $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}$ and $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{2-p}{p-1}}$, respectively.

</details>


### [161] [Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection via Cumulative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25502)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将训练时防御后门攻击的策略形式化为核心集选择问题，利用有毒样本不确定性低且数量少的特点，通过累积熵筛选良性样本，并在每轮训练中遗忘所选样本以增强分离性，从而构建无后门模型，且对自然性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时防御方法通过从中毒训练数据中隔离良性子集来学习无后门模型，但这类方法在防御效果和自然性能保持方面存在不足，需要更有效的选择策略。

Method: 1) 将防御形式化为反后门核心集选择问题；2) 利用有毒样本预测不确定性低且数量少的特性；3) 采用累积熵作为选择标准追踪学习动态；4) 每轮训练中遗忘已选样本以增强良性与有毒样本的可分离性。

Result: 该方法能持续缓解后门攻击，同时对自然性能的影响可忽略不计，优于先前在自然精度上妥协或无法抵御特定攻击的防御方法。

Conclusion: 基于累积熵的核心集选择结合遗忘机制，是一种极其有效的训练时防御策略，能在保持自然性能的同时稳定抵御后门攻击。

Abstract: Recent training-time defenses against neural backdoors isolate a benign subset from poisoned training data, to learn a backdoor-free model from it. In this paper, we formulate this defense strategy as a coreset selection problem, giving rise to so-called "Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection." Since poisonous samples have (a) lower prediction uncertainty and are (b) less frequent than benign samples, coreset selection naturally focuses more on samples associated with benign functionality than the backdoor functionality. We use the Cumulative Entropy as selection criterion to further facilitate this effect. The metric tracks the learning dynamics of training samples and allowing us to select benign samples with high informativeness for the coreset. Additionally, we unlearn the chosen samples in each epoch to facilitate the separability between benign and poisonous samples. Together, this yields an exceptionally effective training-time defense that constructs a benign coreset to train a backdoor-free model. Unlike prior defenses that compromise natural accuracy and fail against certain attacks, our method mitigates backdooring attacks consistently with a negligible impact on natural performance.

</details>


### [162] [AMPBench-MT: A Homology-Controlled Benchmark for Antimicrobial Peptide Potency, Spectrum, and Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25518)
*Ziheng Zhou,Huiyu Luo,Xiaohu Zhu,Nan Wang,Xuebiao Qin,Chaoyan Zhang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMPBench-MT基准，通过整合AMP识别、物种特异性效力、溶血毒性、选择性和谱系活性等多维度检测指标，证明高二进制识别性能不能可靠预测检测终点行为，呼吁AMP评估从识别排行榜转向终点感知的证据审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMP和肽基准仅覆盖二进制识别、多标签注释、检测回归或通用肽模型比较，缺乏在同一序列同源性控制协议下联合评估AMP识别、物种条件效力、抗菌谱、安全性替代终点及跨端点行为的统一框架。

Method: 构建AMPBench-MT基准，保留数据来源并标准化规范肽记录，组织为二进制识别、物种条件pMIC回归、以及终点特异性效力和安全性读出的多层次评估任务。通过161个终点特异性模型评估，系统比较二进制性能与检测终点行为之间的关系。

Result: 高二进制AMP识别性能不能可靠指示检测终点行为；冻结蛋白质语言模型嵌入在pMIC回归中误差最低，图模型和经典回归器与之接近；谱标签显示PR导向指标在负样本稀缺时具有误导性；低毒性、HC50溶血和选择性指标信号虽小但更具检测终点相关性。

Conclusion: AMP评估应超越识别排行榜，转向终点感知的证据审计范式，AMPBench-MT为这一转变提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Computational AMP discovery is often evaluated through AMP/non-AMP recognition, yet follow-up decisions depend on assay-derived evidence such as target-species potency, hemolysis, toxicity, and selectivity. Existing AMP and peptide benchmarks cover binary recognition, multilabel annotation, assay regression, or broader peptide-model comparison, but they do not jointly place AMP recognition, species-conditioned potency, spectrum, safety-facing proxy endpoints, and cross-endpoint behavior within one sequence-homology-controlled protocol. To address this problem, we introduce AMPBench-MT, a provenance-preserving benchmark that standardizes canonical peptide records and organizes them into binary recognition, species-conditioned pMIC regression, and endpoint-specific potency and safety-facing readouts. Across 161 endpoint-specific model evaluations, high binary performance does not reliably indicate assay-endpoint behavior. Frozen protein-language-model embeddings form the leading pMIC error cluster, while graph and classical regressors remain close. Spectrum labels further reveal that PR-oriented metrics can be misleading under scarce observed negatives, whereas low-toxicity, HC50 hemolysis, and selectivity expose smaller but more assay-facing signals. AMPBench-MT shows that AMP evaluation should move beyond recognition leaderboards toward endpoint-aware evidence auditing. Our proposed benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT.

</details>


### [163] [Multi-Scale Structural Features for Continual, Comprehensible Visual Recognition in a Developmental Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25531)
*Zeki Doruk Erden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示，并将其集成到基于发育学习的无梯度学习框架中，用于持续学习二维形状识别。该方法通过局部变异和选择学习离散拓扑模型，无需重放缓冲区或预定义任务边界，在类增量MNIST基准上显著提高了识别准确率，匹配或超越了基于重放和正则化的基线方法，同时保留了持续学习能力：旧知识不被覆盖，表示具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法难以实现持续学习、重用先验知识并暴露可理解的内部结构。先前基于发育学习的梯度自由框架虽能通过局部变异和选择学习离散拓扑模型，具备内在的持续学习保证，但其在视觉输入上的扩展依赖于表达能力有限的特征表示，限制了识别精度。

Method: 1) 提出一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示，编码跨尺度的形状结构，捕获边缘和轮廓特征及其空间关系；2) 将该表示集成到网络精炼学习过程中；3) 改进学习动态和用于从学习模型中预测的读出机制；4) 以类增量MNIST作为受控的可解释基准进行测试。

Result: 新方法显著提高了较先前表示的准确率，匹配或超越了同等存储条件下的重放和正则化基线，且不存储任何历史数据。关键区别在于保留能力：基线方法会在刚学完的类别循环内放弃大部分知识然后重新学习，而本方法不会。

Conclusion: 该系统能够逐样本整合信息，同时可证明地保持对先前输入的正确响应，实现了真正的持续学习。该方法的意义不在于单纯提升准确率，而在于学习方式本身——无需重放、无需任务边界，知识持续累积而不发生灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Contemporary machine learning struggles to learn continually, reuse prior knowledge, and expose a comprehensible internal structure. A recently proposed developmental, gradient-free learning framework addresses these limitations by learning a discrete, topological model of its inputs through local variation and selection, yielding an inherent continual-learning guarantee: new observations refine existing structure without overwriting past knowledge, and without replay buffers or predefined task boundaries. Its extension to visual inputs demonstrated this principle on shape recognition, but relied on a feature representation of limited expressivity that capped recognition accuracy. We introduce a new visual feature representation that encodes shape structure across multiple scales, capturing edge and contour features together with their spatial relations, and integrate it with the network-refinement learning process; we further improve the learning dynamics and the read-out used to predict from the learned model. The study targets two-dimensional shape, with class-incremental MNIST as a controlled, interpretable benchmark in which continual-learning behavior can be measured directly. Our approach substantially increases accuracy over the prior representation, matching or exceeding replay- and regularisation-based baselines at comparable storage while storing no past data, and preserves the framework's defining behavior: earlier-learned classes are retained as new ones are introduced, with no destructive adaptation, and the learned representations remain human-interpretable. What separates the methods is retention: the baselines surrender most of a just-trained class within its own cycle and relearn it afterwards, which ours does not. The significance lies in the manner of learning. The system integrates information one sample at a time while provably preserving its responses to...

</details>


### [164] [Mind the Missing Split: Resolving Feature Heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25538)
*Mohammad Tajabadi,Dominik Heider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在群体学习（Swarm Learning）中，针对随机森林模型因各站点特征空间部分重叠导致推理时出现缺失分裂的问题，设计了多种确定性和概率性推理策略，无需限制训练时只使用特征交集，实验证明这些方法在多个数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中不同站点的测量手段、协议和协变量不同，导致数据集间特征空间仅部分重叠。传统横向群体学习假设各站点特征集相同，但现实场景中特征异质性普遍存在。对于随机森林模型，全局树的某些分裂节点可能在本地站点缺失，使得推理无法正常进行，迫使组织放弃局部特有特征，造成信息浪费。

Method: 提出多种确定性和概率性的推理阶段策略，处理群体学习中随机森林模型因特征部分重叠而产生的缺失分裂问题。这些策略不需要在训练阶段限制使用特征交集，而是在推理时通过不同方式处理缺失分裂。

Result: 在九个数据集上进行评估，所提方法在多种场景下均优于仅使用特征交集的基线方法以及本地独立训练的模型。

Conclusion: 提出的推理时策略有效解决了群体学习中随机森林在特征部分重叠场景下的推理可行性问题，既保留了各站点的特有特征信息，又提升了全局模型的性能。

Abstract: Swarm Learning is a decentralized collaborative learning mechanism that allows multiple organizations to train a shared model without central coordination or direct data sharing. In typical horizontal Swarm Learning, datasets across sites are usually assumed to share the same feature set. However, in real-world applications, sites often have partially overlapping features because measurements, protocols, and available covariates differ across sites. This feature heterogeneity creates a practical issue for machine learning algorithms such as Random Forests. Specifically, when decision trees are pooled into a global Random Forest, inference at a given site can become ill-defined if a traversal encounters a split on a feature that is not available locally, often forcing organizations to discard site-specific variables upfront. In this paper, we address feature heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests under partially overlapping feature spaces. We propose several deterministic and probabilistic inference-time strategies that resolve such missing splits without restricting training to the intersection of features. We evaluate the methods on nine datasets and demonstrate that they outperform both the intersection baseline and locally trained models across a broad range of scenarios.

</details>


### [165] [OrthKD: Extracting Generalized Clinical Knowledge from Heterogeneous Teachers for Lightweight Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25545)
*Yi Xu,Cheng Chen,Mufan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrthKD选择性信任蒸馏框架，从强CNN教师迁移完整监督、从弱ViT教师仅蒸馏特征并施加正交约束，使5.4M参数的MobileNetV3学生在DR筛查中达到0.885 QWK（EyePACS），并将零样本Messidor-2性能从0.507提升至0.728 QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师知识蒸馏假设所有教师提供同等可信的监督，但在DR筛查场景中，强CNN教师（EfficientNet-B3, 0.876 QWK）与弱Transformer教师（Swin-Base, 0.830 QWK）能力不均，弱教师的logits会误导学生模型。

Method: OrthKD框架：对强CNN教师进行完整的logits+特征蒸馏；对弱ViT教师仅进行特征蒸馏（避免不可靠logits干扰）；施加正交约束使两个教师特定的学生投影相互正交，鼓励互补而非冗余的证据。

Result: 在132,049张视网膜图像上，5.4M参数的MobileNetV3学生达到0.885 QWK（EyePACS），零样本Messidor-2从0.507提升至0.728 QWK，同时取得优异的转诊AUC和校准性能。

Conclusion: 选择性蒸馏异构教师可以在资源受限设备上实现实用的DR筛查，验证了根据教师能力差异化分配蒸馏信任的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying diabetic retinopathy (DR) screening models in primary care requires edge-efficient systems that remain accurate, safe, and reliable under domain shift. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation (KD) is a natural compression strategy, but existing approaches largely assume that all teachers provide equally trustworthy supervision. In our setting, this assumption fails: a strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3, 0.876 QWK) and a weaker Transformer teacher (Swin-Base, 0.830 QWK) are complementary, yet the Transformer's logits can still mislead the student. We therefore propose OrthKD, a selective-trust distillation framework that transfers full supervision from the strong CNN, uses feature-only distillation from the weak ViT, and enforces orthogonality between teacher-specific student projections to encourage complementary rather than redundant evidence. This design preserves local lesion precision, injects global structural context, and improves robustness to distribution shift. On 132,049 retinal images, a 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student reaches 0.885 QWK on EyePACS and improves zero-shot Messidor-2 performance from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, while also achieving strong referral AUC and calibration. These results show that selectively distilling heterogeneous teachers can enable practical DR screening on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [166] [Physics-Informed Broad Learning System: An Efficient Backpropagation-Free Framework for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25608)
*Pinki Khatun,M. Sajid,Abhinav Jha,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的宽学习系统（PI-BLS），通过将控制微分算子及初边值条件嵌入线性输出层优化问题，利用伪逆求解最小二乘解，替代了传统PINN中耗时的非线性梯度训练，在保持物理约束的同时将学习过程简化为单步线性优化，在多个前向PDE基准上实现了更快的训练速度和更少的参数，性能与PINN相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN依赖于计算昂贵的梯度优化和深层架构，导致训练速度慢、计算成本高且可扩展性有限。为了解决这些问题，作者希望提出一种更高效、无需迭代反向传播的物理信息学习框架。

Method: 提出PI-BLS框架，将控制微分算子及初边值条件直接嵌入线性输出层的优化问题中，将非线性梯度训练替换为通过伪逆求解的确定性最小二乘解，从而将整个学习过程简化为单步线性优化阶段。

Result: 在多个代表性前向PDE基准测试上，PI-BLS在减少训练时间和模型参数的同时，取得了与传统PINN具有竞争力且往往更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: PI-BLS为求解PDE提供了一种高效的物理信息学习范式，消除了迭代反向传播，同时保留了底层物理约束，是首个基于宽神经网络的物理信息学习框架。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding governing physical laws into deep neural networks. However, their reliance on computationally expensive gradient-based optimization and deep architectures often results in slow training, high computational cost, and limited scalability. In this work, we propose a novel physics-informed broad learning system (PI-BLS), the first physics-informed learning framework based on broad RdNNs. The proposed formulation embeds the governing differential operator and the associated initial and boundary constraints directly into a linear output-layer optimization problem, thereby replacing nonlinear gradient-based training with a deterministic least-squares solution obtained via the pseudoinverse. Consequently, the entire learning process is reduced to a single linear optimization stage while preserving the underlying physical constraints. As a result, PI-BLS offers an efficient learning paradigm for a physics-informed learning framework for solving PDEs that eliminates iterative backpropagation while preserving the underlying physical constraints. Experimental results on representative forward PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PI-BLS achieves competitive and often superior performance with reduced training time and model parameters compared with conventional PINNs.

</details>


### [167] [Contrastive Representation Learning of Longitudinal Disease Trajectories on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25609)
*Bastian Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比图神经网络的框架，通过将纵向临床数据建模为时间图，利用结构感知随机游走进行对比学习，生成保留时间上下文和轨迹拓扑的表征，用于患者疾病进展模式的聚类分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从纵向临床数据中理解疾病轨迹仍然具有挑战性，主要因为复杂的时间动态和异质性患者队列，亟需一种能有效捕捉时间结构和轨迹拓扑的表征学习方法。

Method: 将多元疾病轨迹建模为时间图（节点为患者观测，边捕获时间连续性和结构相似性），采用对比图神经网络，通过结构感知随机游走引导对比学习，生成保留时间上下文和轨迹拓扑的嵌入表征。

Result: 生成的表征能够实现具有相似疾病进展模式患者的鲁棒聚类，并揭示纵向数据中的潜在结构。

Conclusion: 该对比表征学习框架有效解决了纵向临床数据中疾病轨迹建模的难点，为后续患者分层和疾病进展分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Understanding disease trajectories from longitudinal clinical data remains challenging due to complex temporal dynamics and heterogeneous patient cohorts. Here, we present a contrastive representation learning framework that models multivariate disease trajectories as temporal graphs and learns representations using contrastive graph neural networks. Nodes represent patient observations over time, while edges capture temporal continuity and structural similarity between trajectories. Structure-aware random walks guide contrastive learning to generate embeddings that preserve temporal context and trajectory topology. The resulting representations enable robust clustering of patients with similar disease progression patterns and reveal latent structure in longitudinal data.

</details>


### [168] [MemSFT: Mitigating Alignment Tax with an External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25614)
*Jiarui Wang,Xiang Shi,Jiaqi Cao,Rubin Wei,Xiquan Wang,Hao Sun,Jingzhi Wang,Zhiqi Yang,Qipeng Guo,Bowen Zhou,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MemSFT通过可插拔参数化记忆模块解耦领域专长与骨干参数更新，在保持通用能力的同时提升领域性能，有效缓解了对齐税问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在领域微调时会遭遇对齐税（alignment tax），即领域微调导致灾难性遗忘，严重损害通用任务性能。现有方法难以同时兼顾领域专业化与通用能力保持。

Method: 提出MemSFT框架：1) 训练一个可插拔的参数化记忆模块，模仿非参数化检索器在领域数据上的行为；2) 记忆模块可跨不同规模的LLM重用；3) 在解码时，学习一个路由器动态融合记忆模块与骨干模型的输出分布，实现选择性调用领域知识。

Result: 在生物学、地球科学、法学三个领域，使用Qwen3-8B到Qwen3-235B-A22B模型评估，MemSFT持续提升领域性能，通用性能几乎无退化；而全参数SFT在通用任务上出现严重遗忘。

Conclusion: MemSFT提供了一条在参数层面解耦通用模型能力与领域特定知识的实用路径，使LLM获得新领域能力而不牺牲通用能力。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains often incurs an alignment tax, as fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks can cause catastrophic forgetting and substantially degrade performance on general tasks. We propose MemSFT, which mitigates the alignment tax by decoupling domain specialization from backbone parameter updates through a plug-and-play parametric memory. The memory is trained to imitate the behavior of a non-parametric retriever operating over domain data, thereby memorizing knowledge and patterns that would otherwise be accessed through retrieval. Once trained on a specific domain, the memory can be reused across LLMs of different sizes. During generation, a learned router dynamically fuses the output distributions of the memory and backbone at each decoding step, allowing domain expertise to be invoked selectively. Across biology, geoscience, and law, evaluations with models ranging from Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-235B-A22B show that MemSFT consistently improves domain performance with negligible degradation in general performance, whereas full SFT suffers severe forgetting on general tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate a practical path to decoupling general model capabilities from domain-specific knowledge at the parameter level, thereby equipping LLMs with new specialized capabilities without compromising their general capabilities.

</details>


### [169] [Using Data-Derived Priors to Guide CNN Architecture Design for NIR Chemometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了近红外光谱数据的属性（如熵、内在秩、小波能量支撑分数等）是否能为一维CNN架构设计提供经验先验。通过25个回归任务发现，卷积感受野与光谱特征存在明确关联，基于这些关系生成的启发式规则在性能上可与贝叶斯超参数优化相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外化学计量学中常用的CNN架构设计规则较为通用，但不同光谱数据集在采样、平滑度、冗余性和样本量上差异显著。作者希望探究这些数据集属性是否能为CNN设计提供经验性的先验知识，从而减少对昂贵超参数优化的依赖。

Method: 在25个NIR回归任务上，计算数据集大小、光谱长度与间距、熵、内在秩、自相关和小波尺度结构等描述符。设计了两种可解释的一维CNN骨架（最小单卷积模型和扩展浅层模型），使用五折交叉验证的贝叶斯超参数优化进行调优。从近最优试验中提取关系转换为热启动启发式规则，通过直接评估和留一数据集验证进行测试。

Result: 最小CNN中，卷积核分数随光谱熵和内在秩增加而减小，随小波能量支撑分数增加而增大；学习率随训练集大小增加而降低。直接启发式和LODO启发式的中位测试RMSE比分别为0.953和1.017，与HPO相当。扩展CNN表现出类似但可迁移性较弱的结构。联合预处理和CNN超参数优化在19/25任务上优于标准化光谱HPO。

Conclusion: 光谱描述符可以为CNN设计提供实用的先验知识，在针对性调优之前引导浅层NIR模型走向合理的超参数区域，降低对昂贵HPO的依赖。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) for near-infrared (NIR) chemometrics are often designed using generic architectural rules, although spectral datasets differ in sampling, smoothness, redundancy, and sample size. We tested whether these properties can provide empirical priors for CNN design. Across 25 NIR regression tasks, we computed descriptors of dataset size, spectral length and spacing, entropy, intrinsic rank, autocorrelation, and wavelet-scale structure. Two interpretable 1D-CNN scaffolds (a minimal single-convolution model and an extended shallow model with optional branching, dilation, etc) were optimized using five-fold cross-validated Bayesian hyperparameter optimization (HPO). Relationships extracted from near-optimal trials were converted into warm-start heuristics and evaluated directly and through leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) validation. The clearest relationships involved convolutional receptive fields. In the minimal CNN, the preferred kernel fraction decreased with spectral entropy and intrinsic rank, increased with the wavelet energy-support fraction, and the learning rate tended to decrease with training-set size. Direct and LODO heuristics were competitive with HPO, with median test-RMSE ratios of 0.953 and 1.017, respectively. The extended CNN showed similar but less transferable structure across branch usage, dilation, dropout, filter counts, and receptive-field choices. Ten stochastic refits showed seed sensitivity comparable to that of HPO-selected configurations. In a separate experiment, joint preprocessing and CNN HPO outperformed standardized-spectra HPO in 19 of 25 tasks, although gains were dataset-dependent. These results show that spectral descriptors can provide practical CNN design priors, guiding shallow NIR models toward plausible hyperparameter regions before target-specific tuning

</details>


### [170] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文反卷积（CD），一种两阶段需求感知估计器，通过核调制带状算子将促销驱动的瞬态冲击与平滑结构基线分离，并利用分层部分池化实现目录级部署。在M5（30,490 SKU）和Favorita（2,845 SKU）数据集上的严格样本外评估显示：CD虽非期望成本最小化器，但能降低安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，并在预测可靠性（横截面误差离散度）方面全面优于11个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习需求预测虽优化了统计精度，却留下过度的运营波动性，导致安全库存膨胀和牛鞭效应加剧。现有方法未能有效分离促销驱动的瞬态冲击与结构性基线需求，且缺乏可扩展至目录级部署的解决方案。

Method: CD采用两阶段估计器：1）核调制带状算子将需求分解为瞬态促销冲击和平滑结构基线；2）分层部分池化实现无需逐SKU训练的目录级部署。算子由数据驱动而非先验设定，在促销响应为脉冲式的场景下退化为恒等映射。

Result: 在30,490个M5 SKU和2,845个Favorita SKU上：CD在横截面误差离散度和预测失误率上全面最优（失误SKU比例0.8% vs 基线9.9-20.6%）；仅当持有成本超过缺货成本的约20%时才能降低总成本；否则主要贡献运营稳定性和库存资本节约。

Conclusion: CD的精度贡献在于可靠性而非集中趋势；方差比率和标准差安全库存应作为诊断工具而非优化目标；学习到的需求算子虽非正态，但CD的紧凑参数化核能以可解释方式匹配其运营表现。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [171] [DynaBridge: Dynamic Summary-Guided Cross-Task Multimodal Fusion for DASS-Structured Mental Health Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25679)
*Shiyu Teng,Haichen Yu,Jiaqing Liu,Hao Sun,Yu Song,Shurong Chai,Ruibo Hou,Lanfen Lin,Yen-Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaBridge是一个动态摘要引导的跨任务多模态框架，通过结合声学、视觉和文本线索、冻结LLM生成的DASS感知摘要以及置信度感知的精细化策略，在抑郁症、焦虑症和压力评估中实现了优于基线方法的表现，验证了融合多模态线索、语义摘要和DASS-21心理测量结构的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态行为分析模型在评估抑郁、焦虑和压力时，通常采用通用的融合方法，忽略了DASS-21问卷标签的心理测量结构（即风险标签来源于有序症状条目通过固定映射），导致模型未能充分利用这一结构信息。

Method: DynaBridge包含三个关键组件：(1) 对跨会话的声学、视觉和文本线索进行编码，并使用冻结LLM生成DASS感知摘要作为参与者级别的语义证据；(2) 预测有序条目分布，从条目级软分数重建抑郁、焦虑和压力风险证据，并将其与直接多模态风险预测融合；(3) 采用置信度感知的精细化策略，保守地整合高置信度语义线索。

Result: 在AdoDAS官方验证集上，DynaBridge在D/A/S风险预测上达到0.5012的平均F1分数，在DASS-21条目预测上达到0.3216的平均QWK，优于官方基线和代表性多模态方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将多模态线索、语义摘要和DASS-21心理测量结构有效桥接对于提升心理健康评估性能具有重要价值。

Abstract: Multimodal behavioral analysis offers a scalable approach to assessing depression, anxiety, and stress, yet generic fusion models often ignore the psychometric structure of questionnaire labels.
