In DASS-21, risk labels are derived from ordered symptom items through fixed item-to-subscale mappings. We propose \textbf{DynaBridge}, a dynamic summary-guided cross-task multimodal framework for DASS-structured mental health assessment. DynaBridge encodes acoustic, visual, and textual cues across multiple sessions and augments them with frozen-LLM-generated DASS-aware summaries as participant-level semantic evidence. It predicts ordinal item distributions, reconstructs depression, anxiety, and stress risk evidence from item-level soft scores, and fuses this evidence with direct multimodal risk predictions. A confidence-aware refinement strategy further incorporates high-confidence semantic cues conservatively. On the official AdoDAS validation split, DynaBridge outperforms the official baseline and representative multimodal methods, achieving 0.5012 mean F1 for D/A/S risk prediction and 0.3216 mean QWK for DASS-21 item prediction. These results show the value of bridging multimodal cues, semantic summaries, and DASS-21 psychometric structure.

</details>


### [172] [Rashomon Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25680)
*Moisés Santos,Peter van der Putten,Bernhard Pfahringer,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon对齐（RA）度量，从几何角度评估两个模型在整个数据空间中的功能相似性，而非仅依赖数据分布。实验证明几何对齐与分布对齐提供互补视角，可用于模型选择、集成构建和可解释性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能相似性度量仅基于真实数据分布评估模型输出差异，生态效度局限于数据覆盖的输入空间区域，缺乏对整个数据空间中决策边界对齐情况的全面评估。

Method: 提出Rashomon对齐（RA）框架，引入几何视角的功能相似性度量，通过从实例空间均匀采样数据计算几何Rashomon对齐（GRA），在90多个数据集上进行实验分析。

Result: 几何对齐与分布对齐提供不同且互补的模型相似性视角；在模型对齐与预测准确性出现分歧的关键场景中，RA展现了独特价值。

Conclusion: RA可应用于模型选择、集成构建以及增强机器学习模型和算法的可解释性，是一种更全面的功能相似性评估方法。

Abstract: We propose Rashomon Alignment (RA), a new measure to assess functional similarity between two models. Existing functional similarity measures are distributional, quantifying differences between outputs of models applied to real-world data. However, these measures can be regarded as ecologically valid only for regions in the input space represented by the available data. We introduce a geometrical perspective on functional model similarity, which estimates it across the entire data space, offering a comprehensive view of decision boundary alignment independent of any specific data distribution. We also propose geometric Rashomon Alignment as a measure of geometrical similarity, which is computed using data uniformly sampled from the instance space. We perform an experimental analysis on more than 90 datasets, examining critical cases where model alignment diverges from predictive accuracy. Our results show that geometrical and distributional alignment provide different and complementary perspectives on the similarity between models and algorithms. RA can be used for multiple purposes, including model selection, ensemble construction, and enhanced interpretability of machine learning models and algorithms.

</details>


### [173] [From Deterministic to Generative Deep Learning for Urban Air Quality Reconstruction from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25687)
*Abhishek A. Sabnis,Mihai Mitrea,Lya Lugon,Karine Sartelet,Marc Bocquet,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Shupeng Zhu,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的生成式框架，利用深度学习从稀疏观测数据重构巴黎地区四种空气污染物（NO2、O3、PM2.5、PM10）的全场分布，在模拟数据和真实观测上均取得良好效果，并通过数据增强实现跨时段泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染的全场重构对于污染暴露评估和公共卫生决策至关重要，但由于污染物间复杂相互作用、天气模式难以预测以及监测站点覆盖有限，这一任务极具挑战性。现有方法难以同时处理多污染物耦合和稀疏观测问题。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的生成式框架进行多污染物重构，在模拟全场仿真数据上训练，使用来自巴黎9-28个监测站的真实观测数据评估。引入数据增强方法，使模型无需重新训练即可迁移到真实观测数据并泛化至训练期外。

Result: 尽管存在噪声观测和强空间变异性，模型在模拟验证数据上取得了高结构相似性，在真实观测数据上通过功率谱分析生成了逼真的空间模式。数据增强使模型能够泛化至训练时段之外的真实场景。

Conclusion: 该研究凸显了机器学习模型在空气污染重构任务中实现可靠实际部署的潜力，为稀疏监测场景下的污染暴露评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Full-field reconstruction of air pollution is essential for evaluating pollution exposure and supporting public health decision-making. However, the complex interactions among pollutants, hard-to-predict weather patterns, and limited monitoring station coverage make this a complex task. We apply deep learning techniques to provide fast and accurate reconstructions from sparse observations of four key pollutants: NO2, O3, PM2.5 and PM10. Models are trained on full-field simulation data and evaluated on real-world observations collected from 9 to 28 monitoring stations in the city of Paris. We introduce a diffusion-based generative framework for multi-pollutant reconstruction and benchmark its performance against deterministic deep learning models. Despite noisy observations and strong spatial variability, the models achieve high structural similarity on simulated validation data and produce realistic spatial patterns on real-world observations, as indicated by power-spectrum analysis. We introduce data augmentation methods that enable transfer to real-world observations without retraining, allowing the models to generalise beyond the training period. These findings highlight the potential of ML models for reliable real-world deployment in air pollution reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Optimization with Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25719)
*Ezgi Oztekin,Figen Oztoprak,S. Ilker Birbil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束学习（DCL）框架，在约束函数未知且无法查询时，通过局部代理模型和信任域方法求解约束优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化问题中约束函数常未知或难以显式表达，现有离线全局学习方法计算成本高，需要更高效的数据驱动框架。

Method: 每轮迭代利用邻近数据学习局部代理约束函数，在数据支持的信任域内求解子问题，使用简单局部模型和较小优化子问题。

Result: 在合成测试问题和两个文献案例中验证了DCL的性能，可实现与全局模型相当的求解质量。

Conclusion: 局部代理模型在优化过程中自适应数据分布，能以更简单的模型和更小的子问题达到全局模型的求解效果。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL), a data-driven framework for constrained optimization when constraint functions are unknown and cannot be queried during optimization. At each iteration, the method learns a local surrogate from nearby data and solves a subproblem within a data-supported trust region. Compared with offline global constraint learning, the approach uses local surrogates that adapt to the data distribution during optimization and can achieve solution quality comparable to that of global models while using simpler local models and smaller optimization subproblems. We demonstrate the performance of DCL on a synthetic test problem and two case studies from the literature.

</details>


### [175] [Detecting CSAM Text-to-Image LoRAs From Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25750)
*David Demitri Africa,Cate Heine,Nadine Staes-Polet,Kimberly Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过分析LoRA微调权重的左奇异向量（u₁）来检测有害内容（如儿童性虐待材料），无需依赖元数据或生成输出，即可识别LoRA的训练目标，且该方法对权重噪声、缩放和精度变化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA微调模型审核依赖于不可靠的元数据或可能本身违法/不可接受的内容生成，亟需一种无需推理即可直接从权重中检测有害LoRA的方法。

Method: 提取LoRA权重更新矩阵的top-1左奇异向量（u₁）作为指纹，通过该向量的特征识别LoRA的训练内容，并在不同基模型上验证其泛化性。

Result: u₁能够有效识别LoRA的训练数据（以年龄作为CSAM的良性代理），在不同基模型间泛化，能区分有害与良性内容，并对权重噪声、缩放和精度降低等干扰保持稳健。

Conclusion: LoRA权重本身包含可用于检测有害微调的安全信号，有望在不生成有害输出的前提下实现模型审核。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning has made it cheap and easy to customize open-weight image generation models for specific tasks, including the production of child sexual abuse material (CSAM). Existing moderation relies on metadata or generated outputs, but metadata can be deceptive and generating outputs may itself be unacceptable or illegal. We show that a safer signal lives in the weights. The top-left singular vectors of a LoRA's updates form a compact, inference-free fingerprint ($u_1$) of its strongest learned change. Using human-subject age as a benign proxy for CSAM, we find that $u_1$ identifies what a LoRA was trained on, generalizes across base models, and abstains on unrelated benign content. The signal is robust to additive weight noise, rescaling, and precision reduction. These results indicate that harmful LoRAs could be screened directly from their weights without relying on metadata or generating harmful outputs.

</details>


### [176] [An Embarrassingly Simple Rule-based Visiting Circulation Approach to Trip Destination Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25751)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Yong-Han Chen,En-Chao Liu,Hao-Yun Keng,Cheng-Te Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，用于IEEE Big Data Cup 2022中的行程目的地预测任务。该任务的核心挑战在于目标大都市区的目的地完全未知。RVC模型通过利用起源信息和个体的重访行为直接预测目的地，无需从四个训练区域学习。实验结果表明，RVC在离线评估和排行榜提交中均显著优于监督学习方法和启发式方法，最终获得比赛第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决IEEE Big Data Cup 2022中行程目的地预测的独特挑战：目标大都市区的目的地信息在训练阶段完全不可见，传统的监督学习方法在此场景下难以适用。

Method: 提出Rule-based Visiting Circulation (RVC)模型，一种基于规则的访问循环方法，直接利用出发地信息和个体的重访行为模式来确定目标大都市区中的目的地，无需从四个训练区域进行学习。

Result: 在离线评估和比赛排行榜提交上，RVC模型均显著优于监督学习方法和其它启发式方法，最终在比赛中获得第二名。

Conclusion: 重访行为以及出发地与目的地之间的关系在个体出行中起关键作用；基于规则的简单方法在目的地完全未知的跨区域预测任务中可超越复杂监督学习方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Rule-based Visiting Circulation (RVC) model in tackling the challenge in the IEEE Big Data Cup 2022: Trip Destination Prediction. Given trips containing travel information, personal attributes, origin zones, and their features in the training metropolitan areas, the task is to predict the destination of every trip in a targeted metropolitan area whose destinations are not given at all at the training stage. We highlight the challenges in this destination prediction task -- having no knowledge of the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area. We provide insights from the datasets, in which revisiting behaviors and the relationships between origins and destinations play a crucial role in individuals' trips. Hence, we design a simple but comprehensive method, rule-based visiting circulation, which directly utilizes the origin information and individuals' trip behaviors to determine the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area, i.e., requiring no learning from the four training areas. Experimental results on both offline evaluation and leaderboard submission consistently exhibit the proposed RVC can significantly outperform supervised learning methods and other heuristics. The RVC method eventually brings us to second place in the competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [177] [SpectONet: A Physics-Guided Spectral Deep Operator Network for Euler-Bernoulli Beam Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25790)
*Shivani Saini,Ramesh Kumar Vats,Arup Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的谱深度算子网络SpectONet，用于求解欧拉-伯努利梁振动问题。该框架将DeepONet的算子学习能力与物理约束和切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托传感器布局相结合，采用非均匀谱传感器位置（边界附近更密集），有效改善了边界敏感结构响应的有限维表示。在三个合成EBB振动问题和真实桥梁振动数据集上，相比Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet等基线模型，SpectONet在合成问题上至少提升64%，在真实问题上至少提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DeepONet框架通常采用均匀分布的传感器，难以有效捕捉结构响应中的边界敏感特征，且缺乏物理一致性约束，导致预测精度和泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出SpectONet框架，包含三个关键技术：(1) 将DeepONet的算子学习能力与物理信息约束相结合；(2) 采用切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托(CGL)非均匀谱传感器布局，在边界区域集中更多采样点；(3) 将EBB控制方程及初始/边界条件纳入训练目标函数，促进物理一致性和泛化能力。

Result: 在三个合成EBB振动问题和真实桥梁振动数据集上，SpectONet在所有评估指标上均优于Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet等基线模型。合成问题预测误差至少降低64%，真实问题至少降低37%。

Conclusion: SpectONet提供了一种精确、计算高效且物理一致的算子学习框架，适用于结构振动分析，特别是在处理边界敏感的结构响应方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel physics-guided spectral deep operator network, termed SpectONet, for solving Euler-Bernoulli beam (EBB) vibration problems. The proposed framework integrates the operator-learning capability of DeepONet with physics-informed constraints and Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto (CGL) sensor placement. Unlike conventional DeepONet frameworks, which commonly employ uniformly distributed sensors, SpectONet uses nonuniform spectral sensor locations with a higher concentration of points near the domain boundaries. This sampling strategy improves the finite-dimensional representation of boundary-sensitive structural responses while requiring only a limited number of branch-network inputs. The governing beam equation, together with the associated initial and boundary conditions, incorporated into the training objective to promote physically consistent and generalizable predictions. Numerical experiments on three synthetic EBB vibration problems and a real-world bridge vibration dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Comparisons with strong baselines such as, Vanilla DeepONet, PI-DeepONet, PINN, and CNN-UNet show that SpectONet consistently achieves lower prediction errors across all considered evaluation metrics. In particular, SpectONet achieves at least \(64\%\) improvement over the considered baseline models across the three synthetic problems and at least \(37\%\) for the real-world problems. These results demonstrate that SpectONet provides an accurate, computationally efficient, and physically consistent operator-learning framework for structural vibration analysis.

</details>


### [178] [Prototype Adaptation for Zero-Shot sEMG Movement Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25826)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于原型网络的新方法（CPI和SAP），通过线性插值假设在嵌入空间实现组合动作的零样本学习，仅用基本动作训练即可识别未见过的组合动作，在多个数据集上相比现有方法准确率提升超过20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sEMG假肢控制研究主要关注基本动作识别，但日常活动需要组合动作。为所有可能组合收集训练数据耗时且需重新训练模型，因此需要一种无需为组合动作收集数据即可识别它们的方法。

Method: 提出Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI)和Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP)两种方法，基于嵌入空间中组合动作的线性插值假设，在原型网络中实现零样本学习。通过分析信号空间和嵌入空间中组合运动的几何特性来验证假设。

Result: 在NearLab、NinaPro DB3及新采集的BasCom数据集上，SAP方法在组合动作识别中相比现有零样本学习方法准确率提升超过20%，并在在线推理用户实验中保持优势。

Conclusion: 仅用基本动作训练即可有效识别未见过的组合动作，显著降低了数据采集成本，为假肢控制的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables the control of prostheses, allowing upper-limb amputees to re-gain some hand function. Most current research focuses on recognizing basic movements for prosthesis control. However, in most daily activities, such as opening a door, combined movements are essential. However, collecting training data for all possible combined movements is time-consuming and requires re-training of the model for any new combination. We propose two novel recognition approaches, Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) and Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP), that enable zero-shot learning of combined, novel and unseen movements in Prototype Networks after training only with basic movements. Our methods rest on a linear interpolation assumption in the embedding space, which we study by inspecting the geometry of combined motions in signal and embedding space. In experiments on the NearLab and NinaPro DB3 data sets as well as our newly recorded BasCom dataset, our proposed SAP outperforms prior zero-shot learning methods with accuracy improvements on combined movements of more than 20%. This advantage is maintained in online inference experiments in a user study.

</details>


### [179] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一种混合预测框架，结合直接模型（主预测）和递归动作条件模型（约束修正），用于ICU时序预测。在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上，DRIFT相比动作条件TFT在MAP预测上略有改善（0.673%），且在治疗序列改变场景下表现更优，但对治疗变化的敏感性略高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU时序预测方法中，直接预测模型难以有效利用治疗动作信息，而自回归模型易累积误差。需要一种既能利用动作信息又能避免误差累积的预测方法。

Method: DRIFT混合框架：直接模型生成主要预测，递归的动作条件模型提供受约束的修正。具体是将直接预测与动作条件递归校正相结合。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV上，DRIFT相比TFT-action在MAP上降低0.673% MAE；在eICU-CRD上取得最低误差。在治疗序列变更的子集分析中，DRIFT在8小时和24小时预测点上误差更低。DRIFT对治疗变化的敏感性略高于TFT-action（误差增加0.21-0.26 mmHg）。

Conclusion: DRIFT提供了一种有效结合直接预测与递归修正的混合方案，在整体精度上略有提升，尤其在治疗序列变化场景下优势更明显，但代价是对治疗变化的敏感性略高。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [180] [A2TTA: Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation for Evolving Traffic Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25875)
*Du Yin,Xiachong Lin,Yue Tan,Jinliang Deng,Estrid He,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对交通传感器网络动态演化（拓扑扩展和多尺度时间偏移）问题，提出A2TTA框架，通过将拓扑引起的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题，并分离时间适应为持久全局校正和敏捷上下文特定专化，实现高效鲁棒的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测研究通常假设固定传感器图，忽略了真实交通网络的持续演化（如道路建设、人类移动模式变化），导致传统预测模型性能显著下降。需要测试时自适应（TTA）来高效适应部署中的模型，但拓扑扩展和时间偏移的多尺度特性带来了新挑战。

Method: 提出A2TTA框架：1）将拓扑变化引发的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题；2）将时间适应分解为持久的全局校正和敏捷的上下文特定专化，分别应对长期/短期时间偏移；3）联合解决拓扑演化与多尺度时间偏移。

Result: 在10个真实交通网络上的大量实验表明，A2TTA在不同骨干网络、数据集和预测范围上均持续提升了预测性能。

Conclusion: A2TTA通过联合处理拓扑演化和多尺度时间偏移，实现了对持续演化交通环境的高效鲁棒适应，验证了其在动态交通预测场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is important for efficient traffic management and route planning in smart cities. Existing traffic forecasting studies typically assume fixed sensor graphs, overlooking the continuous evolution of real-world traffic networks, e.g., ongoing road network construction and evolving human mobility patterns. These dynamic changes can substantially degrade conventional forecasting models, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to efficiently adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, applying TTA to evolving traffic sensor networks remains challenging in two aspects. First, topology expansion introduces new sensors and connections, continuously reshaping the sensor graph. Second, tem- poral shifts vary in time scale and stability, requiring differentiated adaptation to long-term and short-term shifts. In this study, we address these challenges by proposing A2TTA, an Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation framework for evolving traffic sensor networks, which transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem and separates tem- poral adaptation into persistent global correction and agile context-specific specialization. By jointly addressing topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts, A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments. Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks demonstrate that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons. Our code is available in https://github.com/lixus7/A2TTA.

</details>


### [181] [Minimizing Targeted Activations: Input-Only Suppression of Evaluation-Awareness Latents in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25907)
*Deepanshu Mody,Samarth Agarwal,Utkarsh Mittal,Dipesh Mahato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导的对偶方法——输入侧优化，通过优化流畅提示词使内部潜变量归零，实现无需推理时模型访问的控制。实验发现CAA方向存在安慰剂效应，随机方向同样可被压制并改变行为；且压制评估意识潜变量无法降低模型的行为评估判断。激活可读性不等于行为可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"评估意识"潜变量的输入侧控制方法，该潜变量在先前工作中可线性读取和引导，若模型在检测到被测试时表现出不同行为，将威胁安全评估的有效性。

Method: 采用Fluent Dreaming/EPO方法，结合GCG式token优化与自交叉熵流畅性正则化器，通过扫描流畅性权重，在五种目标构造（CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单个MLP神经元、行为logit）下抑制潜变量，使用Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B进行实验。

Result: 潜变量可被稳健抑制（z≈-7）；因果验证的Llama Scope SAE特征可被完全选择性关闭；CAA方向存在安慰剂效应，随机方向同样可被压制并改变行为；在上下文保留真实评估段落仅优化前缀时，压制评估方向未能降低反而略微提升了模型的行为评估判断；单个MLP神经元相关但不因果；真实Pile扫描可为内部方向提供与优化器竞争的自然文本基线。

Conclusion: 激活可读性不等于行为可控性；肯定性控制验证了擦除检测器，解决了先前遗留的擦除与旋转问题。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by editing internal activations at inference time. We study its input-side dual: optimizing a fluent prompt so that a chosen internal latent is driven toward zero, with no inference-time model access. Our target is an "evaluation-awareness" latent-linearly readable and steerable in recent work-whose control would threaten the validity of safety evaluations if models behave differently when they detect being tested. Adapting Fluent Dreaming / EPO with a negated feature term (GCG-style token optimization plus a self-cross-entropy fluency regularizer, swept over a fluency weight), we suppress the latent under five target constructions-a CAA direction, a subspace norm, an SAE feature, a single MLP neuron, and a behavioral logit-on Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B. The latent is robustly suppressible ($z\approx-7$), and a causally-validated Llama Scope SAE feature can be fully and selectively turned off. But our controls tell a cautionary story about the CAA direction: a placebo random direction is suppressed just as hard and shifts behavior just as far, and when we hold a real eval passage in context and optimize only a prefix, suppressing the eval-direction fails to reduce-and slightly increases-the model's behavioral eval judgment. Activation-readability, in short, is not behavioral controllability. We further find that a single MLP neuron is eval-correlated but not causal at both scales, and that scanning the real Pile yields a natural-text baseline competitive with the optimizer for the internal direction. A positive control validates our erasure detector, bounding an erasure-vs-rotation question earlier left open.

</details>


### [182] [Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25970)
*Pierre Chambon,Kunhao Zheng,Juliette Decugis,Benoit Sagot,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过三阶段方法（优化测试、奖励设计、GRPO适配）实现代码执行时间可学习的RL框架，在代码优化任务上显著提升了正确率与速度表现，最高提升125%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然RL用于代码正确性已成熟，但扩展到代码优化时，执行时间作为奖励信号面临测量噪声、奖励稀疏和GRPO不稳定等问题，导致RL失效。本文旨在解决这些挑战，使模型能通过RL学习生成更快的正确代码。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) 构建DMC-Optim基准，包含大型优化测试和校准的沙箱环境；(2) 在RL环境中组合正确性与速度奖励，使用离线模拟器预测最优配置；(3) 适配GRPO和评估策略以适应更稀疏、噪声更大的定时执行场景。

Result: 在DMC-Optim上，top-50% pass@1从18.0%提升至31.3%（Qwen 2.5 7B），从30.7%提升至50.4%（CWM 32B）；top-30%上CWM 32B相对提升125%；在降级沙箱下比标准RLVR提升100%-200%；LCB上CWM 32B赢83%中位数速度对比；达到人类复杂度改进速率的一半（14% vs 28%）。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地解决定时执行RL中的噪声、稀疏性和稳定性问题，代码优化RL可以显著提升生成代码的速度和正确性，且不牺牲纯正确性指标。

Abstract: RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches 100% to 200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

</details>


### [183] [Generator-Aligned Representation Interfaces for Diagnostic Soft Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25988)
*Weitao Li,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GARI（生成器对齐表示接口）是一种基于表示级别的设计原则，通过对齐规范视图和生成器诱导视图，将选定变换生成器暴露给通用序列骨干网络，实现了跨模态（基因组、图像、三维点云）的变换一致性，而无需针对特定群组重新设计骨干架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确等变架构通常通过专门算子编码预定义的群作用，这限制了其在通用骨干网络和跨数据模态间的可复用性。作者希望提出一种更灵活、可移植的设计原则，使得通用序列骨干能够感知并利用生成器结构，而不必为每个群组重新设计专用算子。

Method: 提出Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI)设计原则：构建生成器索引流，转换为公共交互帧，用共享参数处理，修复排序引起的上下文不匹配，实现跨流信息交换，并通过流间差异聚合。引入Direct Equivariance Error (DEE)作为冻结检查点的诊断指标，量化预定义的表示关系。

Result: 在基因组序列、图像（平面旋转、反射）和三维点云（受控轴向传输）上验证。同一接口原则无需对序列骨干进行群特异性重新设计，即可支持任务相关的变换一致性和泛化性。

Conclusion: GARI为硬等变架构提供了可移植的诊断补充：使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可测量，同时有限探针证据仍与连续群上的精确等变认证相区分。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [184] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了9种表格基础模型（TFM）在分布偏移下的OOD性能，发现所有TFM均出现性能退化，偏移差距在0.003-0.060之间，且高性能模型存在可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TFM主要针对独立同分布数据设计，但在现实场景中分布偏移普遍存在，而目前对TFM在分布偏移下的鲁棒性研究有限。

Method: 选取9种不同预训练策略和架构的TFM（TabPFNv2/v2.5/v2.6/v3、TabICL/v2、Mitra、LimiX、TabFM），在TableShift研究中3个真实数据集（HELOC、Voting、Childhood Lead）上评估标签偏移、社会经济偏移和地理偏移三种分布偏移类型下的OOD性能。

Result: 所有TFM在分布偏移下均出现系统性性能退化，偏移差距因偏移类型而异（0.003-0.060）；ID与OOD性能之间的关系与传统表格模型一致；高性能模型存在显著的可扩展性差距，所需内存和计算资源超出标准部署基础设施。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了现有表格数据OOD基准，为在高风险领域采用TFM提供了证据支持，并揭示了TFM在分布偏移下的鲁棒性挑战和部署瓶颈。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [185] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种矩阵感知的Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)变体，在内部扰动阶段对矩阵值隐藏层参数使用谱内扰动，在外部更新阶段结合Muon优化器，在ImageNet-1K上的ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50上取得了最佳验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAM变体虽然探索了多种几何选择，但哪种几何在实际中最有效仍不清晰；同时，矩阵感知优化（如Muon）展示了尊重隐藏层权重矩阵结构的优势，因此本文旨在将矩阵感知几何引入SAM的两个阶段以提升性能。

Method: 针对矩阵值隐藏层参数引入逐层的谱内扰动（spectral inner perturbation），将其作为SAM内部扰动阶段的几何约束；外部更新阶段分别结合AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器，构成完整的矩阵感知SAM框架。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50进行实验，谱内扰动与Muon外更新的组合在所有评估方法中表现 consistently strong，在两个模型上均取得最佳验证准确率。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（特别是谱内扰动结合Muon外更新）在SAM框架中具有显著优势，为设计更有效的几何感知优化方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [186] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reinformed Dreamer算法，通过潜空间引导的异步表示学习目标，改进了异步强化学习中特权信息表示的学习，在多个基准上比Dreamer和Informed Dreamer表现更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步模型强化学习算法（如Informed Dreamer）在学习特权信息表示时存在局限性，导致表示质量不佳，影响算法性能。

Method: 提出一种新的异步表示学习目标——潜空间引导（latent guidance），在此目标下设计了Reinformed Dreamer算法。

Result: 在多个基准实验上，Reinformed Dreamer相比Dreamer表现出更一致的性能提升，优于先前的异步方法（如Informed Dreamer）。

Conclusion: 潜空间引导的异步表示学习能有效改善特权信息表示，提升基于模型的异步强化学习算法的性能稳定性。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [187] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据集感知迁移学习框架(DITL)，通过自适应难度加权交叉熵(A-DWCE)和自适应邻域表示三元组损失(A-NR-Triplet)统一优化目标，在乳腺X线摄影分类任务上实现了对大型临床队列和小型ROI数据集的一致性能提升，无需超参数调优且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习忽略数据集特定特征，而现有邻域信息方法局限于窄任务且刚性公式化，难以扩展到大规模人群数据集，乳腺癌X线摄影分类在小型数据集和大型临床队列上均面临性能瓶颈。

Method: DITL框架包含两个自适应组件：(1) A-DWCE：基于自监督特征空间中的k近邻标签纯度分配每样本权重；(2) A-NR-Triplet：使用可学习边界强制类内紧凑性和类间分离性。统一目标整合数据集导出的难度信号与邻域三元组监督，无需超参数调优和启发式加权。

Result: 在VinDR-Mammo大规模数据集上，全图乳腺密度分类达到SOTA性能，准确率、F1分数和AUC均有显著提升(p<0.0001)；在小型ROI数据集上也获得一致且统计显著的增益(p<0.0001)。

Conclusion: DITL建立了一个临床相关、可扩展且可泛化的乳腺X线摄影分类框架，桥接了小型病变分析与大规模密度估计，覆盖了乳腺癌筛查到诊断的完整谱系。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [188] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于置信度自适应的专家路由方法，通过核采样方式动态调整激活的专家数量，在匹配计算量的情况下优于固定k值的MoE-LoRA，并能用相同信号提升分布外检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-LoRA方法对所有token使用固定数量的专家k，但不同token的不确定性不同，固定k导致简单token过度计算而困难token计算不足。作者观察到路由器输出分布本身就是逐token的不确定性信号。

Method: CARE采用核采样方式激活专家：按路由器权重降序激活，直到累积质量达到阈值；当已激活专家意见不一致时做少量扩展。同时引入预算恒温器校准阈值，使平均激活专家数匹配目标。该方法无需额外参数，可单次前向传播直接替换。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的8个常识推理基准、数学、代码和知识任务上，CARE在匹配计算量下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，且能以更少专家匹配固定k=4基线。相同的置信度和分歧信号在分布外检测上优于MSP、熵和多趟代理方法。

Conclusion: CARE通过核采样和预算控制实现了自适应专家路由，实验验证了其有效性，并提供了核保真度、预算最优性和分歧的认知解读等理论支持。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


### [189] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR-GM是一个面向小样本工业异常检测的预测框架，利用大语言模型从系统文档中提取物理耦合关系构建领域先验邻接矩阵作为结构门控，再结合正常数据的皮尔逊相关系数进行调制，无需额外可学习参数即可在SKAB基准上超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的多元传感器时序异常检测方法在小规模物理系统中表现不佳：标注异常稀缺、正常数据有限，图模型容易捕获虚假相关并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: （1）利用LLM从系统文档中提取传感器间有向物理耦合关系，编码为二元领域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；（2）用正常训练数据的皮尔逊相关系数调制该门控；（3）基于变异系数计算传感器级可靠性权重，加权得到异常分数；（4）所有图结构和权重组件在训练前固定，不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计学和深度学习的三类基线方法。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺的CPS场景中，领域结构化的图先验是比完全学习的拓扑结构更实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Beyond "What to Retrieve": Uncertainty in Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24884)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Xiaoli Lian,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenCoder提出了一种不确定性感知框架，通过估计来源级不确定性来过滤和排序异构证据，并在仓库级代码生成中指导生成、验证和修复。在RepoExec-inline基准上，将GPT的选择输出正确率从56.25%提升至78.13%，但提升效果与验证-修复基线相当，且Gemini上的改进无统计显著性，表明收益依赖于后端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强方法主要优化检索相关性，未显式建模检索证据中的不确定性（噪声、冗余、冲突）对下游代码生成的影响。

Method: 提出不确定性感知框架OpenCoder：①估计每个证据来源（API知识、仓库上下文、相似代码）的不确定性；②利用不确定性过滤和排序异构证据；③将不确定性作为控制信号指导生成、验证与修复过程；④通过因子分析研究不同证据来源之间的交互作用。

Result: 在32任务的RepoExec-inline评估中，GPT选择输出正确率从56.25%提升至78.13%；但该结果与验证-修复基线无显著差异，且Gemini上的改进未通过统计显著性检验。目标感知的API精炼显著提升了API集检索效果。

Conclusion: 不确定性可作为仓库级代码检索、验证和修复的可操作控制信号；但收益具有后端依赖性，不存在普适的证据来源排序，证据间存在显著的交叉交互作用。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation relies on heterogeneous evidence whose relevance, compatibility, and completeness are inherently uncertain. Similar-code examples, repository context, and project-specific APIs may provide complementary information, but can also introduce noisy, redundant, or conflicting signals. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches primarily optimize retrieval relevance without explicitly modeling how uncertainty in retrieved evidence affects downstream generation. We introduce OpenCoder, an uncertainty-aware framework that estimates source-specific uncertainty, uses it to filter and rank heterogeneous evidence, and guides generation, verification, and repair. A factorial analysis over API knowledge, repository context, and similar-code evidence reveals no universal additive source ranking; instead, significant cross-source interactions depend on the accompanying evidence and LLM backend. On an expanded 32-task RepoExec-inline evaluation, OpenCoder improves GPT selected-output correctness over Baseline RAG from 56.25\% to 78.13\%. However, it matches a verification-and-repair control, and the corresponding Gemini improvement is not statistically supported, indicating backend-dependent benefits. Target-aware API refinement also substantially improves API-set retrieval. These findings support treating uncertainty as an actionable control signal for repository-level retrieval, verification, and repair.

</details>


### [191] [Characterizing Structural Testability in JavaScript: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24965)
*Shahrzad Mirzaei,Saba Alimadadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对30个开源JavaScript项目进行大规模实证研究，提出包含可控性、可观测性、分支复杂度、异步协调、事件驱动行为、封装性和副作用强度七个维度的结构化可测试性框架，并通过复合可测试性评分(CTS)进行分析，发现结构难测函数集中在少数文件中，且由多种重复结构配置而非单一模式导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究主要面向面向对象和并发软件，对JavaScript系统的结构化可测试性认识不足。JavaScript依赖异步执行、事件驱动控制流、闭包和动态交互等特性，现有可测试性框架无法有效捕捉这些结构特征。

Method: 将结构化可测试性操作化为七个维度（可控性、可观测性、分支复杂度、异步协调、事件驱动行为、封装性、副作用强度），基于AST静态分析提取各维度指标，聚合为复合可测试性评分(CTS)，对30个开源JavaScript项目进行跨函数、文件和项目的比较分析。

Result: 结构难测函数集中在少数文件中；低CTS函数存在多种重复结构配置而非单一主导模式；项目特征与可测试性之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 揭示了JavaScript结构化可测试性的分布特征和典型结构原型，为测试工作量估计、自动化测试生成、可测试性感知重构和软件质量评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Software testability has long been recognized as a software quality attribute that influences testing effort and effectiveness. While prior work has extensively studied testability in object-oriented and concurrent software, comparatively little is known about structural testability in modern JavaScript systems. JavaScript applications rely on asynchronous execution, event-driven control flow, closures, and dynamic interactions that are not explicitly captured by existing testability frameworks. This paper presents a large-scale empirical study of structural testability in JavaScript. We operationalize structural testability as a seven-dimensional construct capturing controllability, observability, branching complexity, asynchronous coordination, event-driven behaviour, encapsulation, and side-effect intensity. These dimensions are derived from AST-based static analysis and aggregated into a Composite Testability Score (CTS) for comparative analysis across functions, files, and projects. We apply this framework to 30 open-source JavaScript projects spanning diverse domains and sizes. Our analysis characterizes the distribution of structural testability, identifies recurring structural archetypes among low-CTS functions, and examines associations between project characteristics and testability. We find that structurally-challenging functions are concentrated within a relatively small subset of files and arise through multiple recurring structural configurations rather than a single dominant pattern. These findings provide new insight into structural testability in JavaScript and establish a foundation for future research on testing effort, automated test generation, testability-aware refactoring, and software quality assessment.

</details>


### [192] [Preliminary Guidelines for Using and Evaluating GenAI Tools to Support Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24991)
*Barbara Kitchenham,Sebastián Pizard,Lech Madeyski,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Martin Shepperd,David Budgen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GUEST建议，指导软件工程研究者在使用生成式AI和大语言模型进行系统文献综述时，确保方法的严谨性、可靠性和透明度，强调人类监督的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和LLM越来越多地被用于系统文献综述等学术任务，但它们能否满足SLR所需的严谨性、可靠性和透明度尚未有保障，因此需要为研究者提供使用和评估GenAI的指导建议。

Method: 首先进行快速综述以识别已有的GenAI支持SLR的评估和使用指南；其次结合思想实验、文献相关指导以及自身经验，制定在SLR中使用和评估GenAI的建议。

Result: 识别了研究者评估GenAI用于SLR时面临的问题；阐明在规划、实施和报告使用GenAI的SLR及评估GenAI工具时需考虑的过程问题；最终总结为一组名为GUEST的过程建议。

Conclusion: GenAI需要人类监督，目前无法独立完成无监督的系统性研究，但可为某些重复性任务提供经济高效的辅助，并为复杂任务提供额外验证。GUEST建议有助于研究者进行可信的SLR并开展严格的独立评估研究。

Abstract: Context: Generative AI (GenAI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for academic tasks in software engineering and beyond, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). However, while capable of summarizing text, there is no guarantee they can meet the rigour, reliability, and transparency that SLRs require. Objectives: To support researchers intending to conduct SLRs using GenAI or those conducting empirical studies evaluating how well GenAI supports SLR tasks. Methods: First, we conducted a rapid review to identify studies that propose guidelines for evaluating and using GenAI and LLMs to support SLRs. Second, we drew on thought experiments, relevant guidance from the literature, and our own experience conducting SLRs and evaluating tools to develop recommendations for how to use and assess GenAI in the context of SLRs. Results: We discuss the problems researchers face when evaluating GenAI for SLRs. We identify and explain process issues to consider when planning, conducting, and reporting both SLRs using GenAI and evaluations of GenAI tools. Finally, we summarize our results as a set of process recommendations, which we name GUEST (GenAI Use and Evaluation in SLR Tasks). Conclusion: We argue that GenAI requires human oversight and is not currently capable of unsupervised systematic studies. However, it offers the prospect of cost-effective assistance for some repetitive tasks and for additional validation of some complex tasks. Our GUEST recommendations should help software engineering researchers both to conduct and report trustworthy SLRs using GenAI and to provide rigorous independent evaluation studies.

</details>


### [193] [Authoring Agent Skills: A Software-Engineering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25032)
*Giuseppe Destefanis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证了Agent技能是一种软件制品，应遵循软件工程原则（单一职责、接口与实现分离、低耦合、共享令牌预算经济性及行为评估替代确定性测试）。以Claude Code为参考实现，详细阐述了技能的结构、分阶段加载机制、描述编写方法，并将其与项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子代理、外部工具连接和钩子等机制进行了比较，给出了基于"谁决定执行"和"提供何种保证"的选择规则。此外，还提出了评估驱动的创作流程、常见模式与故障以及第三方技能的可信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型代理中可复用过程性知识（Agent技能）的兴起，如何规范地构建、选择和加载这些技能成为一个关键问题。本文旨在将软件工程原则引入技能构建，建立系统化的创作与评估方法。

Method: 采用论证与案例分析相结合的方法：以Claude Code为参考实现，通过UML类图风格对比、加载模型分析、技能解剖、机制相对位置比较以及会话中的技能与钩子作用点分析，系统阐述技能的结构化构建方法。

Result: 提出了技能构建的软件工程原则框架，明确了技能的分阶段加载模型和描述编写规范，建立了不同行为塑造机制（技能、项目记忆文件、斜杠命令、子代理、外部工具、钩子）的选择规则，并总结了评估驱动的创作流程及常见模式与故障。

Conclusion: Agent技能应作为软件制品进行工程化构建，遵循适应性软件工程原则。技能与其他行为塑造机制各有适用场景，选择依据是"谁决定执行"和"提供何种保证"。评估驱动的创作流程可有效提升技能质量，但第三方技能的可信问题仍需关注。

Abstract: Agent Skills are an emerging way to extend large language model agents with reusable procedural knowledge that the agent loads on demand. Anthropic introduced Agent Skills and published the format as an open specification supported across several agent tools. This note argues that a skill is a software artefact and that its construction should follow software-engineering principles, with qualifications: single responsibility, separation of interface from implementation, low coupling, and economy in a shared token budget, together with behavioural evaluation in place of deterministic testing. Using Claude Code as the reference implementation, it describes how a skill is structured, how its contents are loaded in stages, and how to write the description on which selection depends. It places skills against the other mechanisms a developer can use to shape agent behaviour, like project memory files, slash commands, subagents, external tool connections, and hooks, and gives a rule for choosing between them based on who decides that a mechanism runs and what guarantee it provides. It then sets out an evaluation-driven authoring process, a set of patterns and faults commonly encountered in authoring, and the trust question raised by using skills from third parties. We illustrate the comparison drawn in UML class style, the loading model, the anatomy of a skill, the relative position of each mechanism, and the points at which skills and hooks act during a session.

</details>


### [194] [What Motivates Whom? A Survey of Newcomers to OSS and Experienced OSS Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25126)
*Shashiwadana Nirmani,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Mojtaba Shahin,Xiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过208名从业者的在线调查，揭示了OSS贡献者在选择项目时的偏好如何与其动机和人口统计背景相关，并比较了新人和经验丰富从业者的不同需求，为改进贡献者入职、参与和留存以及推荐系统提供了见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件从业者在选择合适的开源项目时面临困难，导致低效的入职和参与度下降。理解贡献者如何选择OSS项目对于支持其入职、参与和长期贡献至关重要。

Method: 通过在线调查收集208名从业者的数据，分析人口统计因素（年龄、性别、OSS角色）与动机的相关性，以及项目特性偏好（项目年龄、开发阶段、文档质量）与具体动机的关系，并分别比较新人和经验从业者的反应。

Result: 人口统计因素（年龄、性别、OSS角色）与动机显著相关；项目特性偏好（项目年龄、开发阶段、文档质量）因具体动机而异；新人与经验从业者具有不同的偏好模式。

Conclusion: 通过区分新人和经验从业者的独特需求，研究结果为研究人员、OSS项目拥有者和软件从业者提供了改进贡献者入职、参与和留存的见解，并为未来项目推荐系统和OSS生态系统的改善提供了依据。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) development continues to expand, yet software practitioners often struggle to select suitable projects, leading to inefficient onboarding and disengagement. Understanding how contributors select OSS projects is important for supporting contributors onboarding, engagement, and long-term participation within OSS communities. This study investigates contributors' project-selection preferences in OSS projects and examines how these preferences correlate with contributors' motivations and demographic backgrounds. Through an online survey of 208 practitioners, we found that demographic factors, such as age, gender, and the OSS role they held, significantly correlate with their motivations. Additionally, preferences for project characteristics such as project age, development stage, and documentation quality vary based on specific motivations. Importantly, our findings are presented through a comparative lens, analyzing the responses of newcomers to OSS and experienced OSS practitioners separately to uncover their distinct preferences. Lastly, we explore software practitioners' perspectives on how existing recommendation systems could better support project selection and align with their motivations. By disentangling the unique needs of newcomers to OSS and OSS practitioners, our findings provide insights for researchers, OSS project owners, and software practitioners to improve contributor onboarding, engagement, and retention, while also informing future project recommendation systems and improving the OSS ecosystem.

</details>


### [195] [Learning from 53.6K Real-World Developer Edits of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25130)
*Jenny T. Liang,Mihika Bairathi,Wayne Chi,Ameet Talwalkar,Nishant Subramani,Valerie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DECODE是一个包含53.6K个真实世界IDE内代码编辑操作的数据集，来源于1000+开发者对AI生成代码的修改。研究发现大多数编辑发生在接受AI补全后的15分钟内，31%的编辑轨迹涉及完全删除AI补全内容。在该数据集上微调的3B开源模型在代码编辑预测任务上显著优于前沿大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM主要使用公开的Git提交数据进行训练，但Git提交只包含最终成功的代码片段，缺乏真实的、细粒度的代码编辑过程信息。为了填补这一高质量真实代码编辑数据的空白，研究者构建了DECODE数据集。

Method: 收集了来自1000+开发者在IDE中对AI生成代码（Python、TypeScript、JavaScript）进行的53.6K次真实编辑操作，形成DECODE数据集。在此基础上进行数据分析洞察编辑行为，并使用该数据集对LLM进行微调以评估其代码编辑预测能力。

Result: 1) 大多数编辑在接受AI补全后15分钟内发生，31%的编辑轨迹中完全删除了AI补全内容；2) 在DECODE上微调的3B开源模型在代码编辑预测任务上显著优于前沿大语言模型。

Conclusion: 强调了未来AI编程助手需要采用以开发者为中心的机器学习方法，即关注开发者实际编辑行为而非仅关注最终代码结果。

Abstract: Imperfections in AI-generated code require that software developers modify the generated code manually, or by re-prompting an AI programming assistant. Manual code edits provide more realistic and granular information on editing behavior than Git commits, which only contain final successful code snippets. Yet, due to a lack of high-quality, realistic code editing data, LLMs are mostly trained on publicly available Git data (e.g., commits). To address this gap, we introduce DECODE (Developer Edits of Code Dataset), a dataset of 53.6K real-world in-IDE code edits of AI-generated code in Python, TypeScript, and JavaScript, sourced from 1K+ developers. First, we demonstrate the utility of DECODE for data analysis, obtaining insights on when, why, and how AI-generated code is edited. We find that most edits occur within the first 15 minutes after accepting an AI completion, resulting in the removal of AI completions in 31% of edit trajectories. Second, we use DECODE to benchmark the ability of LLMs to predict code edits. We find that finetuning on DECODE enables open-source 3B models to perform code edit prediction tasks significantly better than frontier LLMs. We then discuss implications of this work, emphasizing the necessity of developer-centric machine learning approaches for future AI programming assistants.

</details>


### [196] [Specification-Driven DevOps for Multi-Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25141)
*Oleg Grynets,Kyrylo Fursov,Vasyl Lyashkevych,Volodymyr Veres*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了前沿LLM仅基于仓库内容生成Docker化多服务应用部署配置的能力。所有三个生成环境在功能上均可运行，但模型在架构安全性、生产意图等方面存在系统性缺陷，包括遗漏网络分段、多阶段构建、缓存优化等关键部署模式。据此，论文形式化了"功能正确性"与"部署意图保真度"的区别，并推导出无法从仓库工件可靠推断的最小显式部署规约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注LLM生成可执行软件环境的能力，但功能可执行性并不保证符合架构、安全、工作流和生产意图。本研究旨在探究LLM能否仅凭仓库工件生成符合开发者部署意图的Docker化配置，并量化其与真实部署的差距。

Method: 选取三个异构仓库（涵盖Python、Node.js、.NET、React、Rust、Java、Redis、PostgreSQL、MySQL等），使用前沿LLM生成Dockerfile和Docker Compose配置，通过确定性端到端HTTP测试进行功能验证，并结合人工结构化比对分析部署完整度。

Result: 三个生成环境均可功能运行（一处需手动修正Rust基础镜像版本）。模型正确推断了服务拓扑、端口、基础设施依赖、服务主机名、后台工作者、隐藏代理配置和基于文件的Docker密钥机制。但系统性地遗漏了网络分段、多阶段构建、依赖层缓存、热重载卷、生产前端服务、限制性后端端口策略和跨平台构建逻辑。

Conclusion: 论文形式化了"功能正确性"与"部署意图保真度"之间的区分，并推导出无法从仓库工件可靠推断的最小显式部署规约，明确了当前LLM在生成生产级部署配置方面的根本性局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate executable software environments from repository artifacts. However, functional executability does not necessarily imply conformity with architectural, security, workflow, and production intent. This study investigates whether a frontier LLM can generate Dockerfiles and Docker Compose configurations for multi-service applications using repository contents without access to developer-authored deployment artifacts. Three heterogeneous repositories combining Python, Node.js, .NET, React, Rust, Java, Redis, PostgreSQL, and MySQL-compatible infrastructure were evaluated using deterministic end-to-end HTTP oracles and manual structural comparison. All three generated environments became functionally operational, although one required a Rust base-image update from version 1.85 to 1.88. The model correctly reconstructed service topology, application ports, infrastructure dependencies, service hostnames, a background worker, hidden proxy configurations, and a file-based Docker secrets mechanism. However, it consistently omitted network segmentation, multi-stage builds, dependency-layer caching, live-reload volumes, production frontend serving, restrictive backend-port policies, and cross-platform build logic. Based on these observations, the study formalizes the distinction between functional correctness and deployment-intent fidelity and analytically derives a minimal explicit deployment specification for information that cannot be reliably inferred from repository artifacts.

</details>


### [197] [NFR-to-Code Traceability in a Blockchain-IoT System: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25325)
*Yifei Wang,Jacky Keung,Xiaoxue Ma,Shijie Zhang,Yishu Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究非功能性需求（NFR）到代码的可追溯性问题，基于真实区块链-IoT项目构建了包含FR和NFR链接的标注数据集，发现FR到代码的追溯比NFR容易，安全相关NFR最难，且NFR追溯的主要挑战在于实现证据在代码中分散且不局部化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求到代码追溯研究主要聚焦功能性需求（FR），非功能性需求（NFR）的追溯因难度更大且研究不足而被忽视，这阻碍了安全、可靠性等关键质量属性的验证，因此需要系统研究NFR到代码的追溯特性与挑战。

Method: 基于真实区块链-IoT项目，设计跨异构工件的标注协议构建追溯链接，人工构建包含FR和NFR链接的子集；使用该子集分析NFR追溯特征，并评估四种检索基线方法：TF-IDF、BM25、LSI和WMD。

Result: FR到代码的追溯一致地比NFR到代码的追溯更容易；安全相关的NFR是最难追溯的子集；NFR追溯的主要挑战不在于需求本身的可用性，而在于实现证据在代码中分布分散、缺乏明确的局部化。

Conclusion: NFR到代码的追溯比FR更困难，尤其是安全类NFR；其核心瓶颈在于NFR的实现代码分散且不集中，而非需求信息不足，这为未来NFR追溯方法的设计提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Requirement-to-Code traceability has been widely studied, yet existing research and public benchmarks remain largely centered on functional requirements (FRs). In contrast, traceability for non-functional requirements (NFRs) remains more difficult and underexplored, which hinders the verification of critical quality concerns such as security and reliability.This paper studies NFR-to-Code traceability based on a real-world blockchain-IoT project. We design an annotation protocol for constructing trace links across heterogeneous artifacts and build a manually curated subset containing both FR and NFR links. Using this subset, we examine the characteristics of NFR traceability and further evaluate four representative retrieval baselines: TF-IDF, BM25, LSI, and WMD. The results show that FR-to-Code tracing is consistently easier than NFR-to-Code tracing, while security-related NFRs are the most difficult subset. They further indicate that the main challenge of NFR traceability lies not in requirement availability, but in implementation evidence that is distributed and not clearly localized in code.

</details>


### [198] [Specula: Scaling formal specifications for autonomous model checking of system code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25333)
*Qian Cheng,Saad Mohammad Rafid Pial,Ruize Tang,Yiming Su,Emilie Ma,Finn Hackett,Ivan Beschastnikh,Yu Huang,Tianyin Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Specula是一个基于LLM的自动化形式化规约生成与模型检测系统，能够自主为大型系统代码生成TLA+规约，并通过自进化循环迭代提升规约质量，最终在48个开源项目中发现了249个深层漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形式化方法需要大量人工编写规约，门槛高，难以应用于真实世界系统代码；而纯LLM驱动的技术存在奖励黑客和幻觉等局限性。Specula旨在消除这些障碍，实现形式化方法的自动化应用。

Method: Specula利用基于大语言模型的编码智能体自主开发TLA+规约（包括描述正确性属性的不变量和描述系统实现的抽象形式化模型），并通过自进化循环迭代提升规约质量，使智能体能够加深对系统代码及其行为的理解。

Result: 在48个开源系统项目中，Specula发现了249个漏洞，其中包括许多现有方法难以发现的深层漏洞，并已被多家公司投入使用。

Conclusion: Specula成功将形式化方法应用于真实世界系统代码，实现了全自动化的规约生成与模型检测，显著降低了形式化方法的应用门槛，并展现出强大的漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: Specula is a push-button agentic system that generates high-quality formal specifications for large, complex system code and uses the specifications for highly effective model checking and bug finding. Specula employs large language model (LLM) based coding agents to autonomously develop TLA+ specifications, including invariants that describe correctness properties of the target system and formal models that describe the system implementation with the right level of abstractions. Specula is fully autonomous and thus eliminates the barrier of applying formal methods to real-world system code (as in traditional human-centric approaches). Meanwhile, Specula addresses limitations of LLM-driven techniques like reward hacking and hallucinations through self-evolving loops that iteratively improve specification quality by enabling the agents to deepen their understanding of system code and its behaviors. We have used Specula to check 48 open-source system projects; Specula found 249 bugs including many deep bugs that are hard to find by existing approaches. Specula has been used by several companies and is maintained at https://github.com/specula-org/Specula.

</details>


### [199] [CodeNib: A Multi-View Data System for Serving Repository Context to Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25431)
*Zhongming Yu,Hengjia Yu,Boqin Yuan,Shuting Zhao,Yizhao Chen,Aryan Dokania,Mihir Jagtap,Jiayu Chang,Yitong Ma,Yash Jayswal,Wentao Ni,Hejia Zhang,Zhaoling Chen,Gangda Deng,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeNib构建了每个仓库提交的可复用词法、稠密和结构视图，通过单一运行时提供排序搜索、符号导航和有界上下文。在100个快照上，图形和向量更新速度中位数提升8.7倍和25.4倍，静态导航延迟比实时服务器低4.7倍，所选上下文策略可减少50-87%的轨迹token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理在演化仓库中反复搜索、导航和保留上下文，但断连的索引、语言服务器和任务局部历史导致重复发现并掩盖生命周期成本。

Method: CodeNib为每个仓库提交构建可复用的词法、稠密和结构视图，将输出映射到仓库相对源范围，跨编辑维护选定视图，并通过单一运行时提供排序搜索、符号导航和有界上下文。

Result: 在100个快照上，当输出与独立重建匹配时，图形和向量更新中位数分别快8.7倍和25.4倍；在静态导航子集（占1000个请求的63%）上，每请求实时/静态延迟中位数比为4.7倍；跨五个模型，所选上下文策略在保持定位能力的同时减少50-87%的轨迹token。

Conclusion: 结果支持多视角仓库上下文的显式、操作特定有效性边界服务。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly search, navigate, and retain context from evolving repositories, but disconnected indexes, language servers, and task-local histories force repeated discovery and obscure lifecycle costs. CodeNib builds reusable lexical, dense, and structural views per repository commit, maps outputs to repository-relative source ranges, maintains selected views across edits, and serves ranked search, symbol navigation, and bounded context through one runtime.
  Across 100 snapshots, we map quality-cost frontiers across the repository-context lifecycle. When outputs match an independent rebuild, graph and vector updates are $8.7\times$ and $25.4\times$ faster at the median. On the static-navigation subset matching normalized live-server locations (63% of 1,000 requests), the median per-request live/static latency ratio is $4.7\times$. Across five models, selected context policies preserve localization with 50--87% fewer trajectory tokens than paired grep/read. Together, these results support multi-view repository-context serving with explicit, operation-specific validity boundaries.

</details>


### [200] [Repositories, Contributors, and Continuity: An Empirical Study of Foundational Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25437)
*Vincent Gierisch,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对量子软件生态系统中的基础仓库进行了实证分析，结合跨仓库活动和贡献者关系，研究了社区演化、贡献者迁移模式及跨项目知识转移，揭示了概念和影响力如何在仓库边界间持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态系统快速成长，但许多仓库的开发活动仅持续有限时间，其概念是否能在单个仓库之外持续存在尚不清楚，因此需要系统性地研究社区演化和知识迁移模式。

Method: 采用成熟的实证软件工程技术，对一组基础量子软件仓库进行分析，结合跨仓库活动与贡献者关系，研究社区的演化路径。

Result: 提供了贡献者迁移模式和跨项目知识转移的实证证据；观察到多种发展路径：项目可能演化为持续社区、贡献者可能将概念整合到已有生态系统中、或通过新的和后续软件制品继续活动。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于量子软件生态系统中概念和影响力如何跨仓库边界持续存在的初步实证视角。

Abstract: Driven by contributions from academia, industry, and open-source communities, the quantum software ecosystem is rapidly growing. Across this ecosystem, new concepts often emerge through software artefacts accompanying scientific publications as well as through sustained development in larger communities. However, many repositories receive development efforts only over a limited period of time, raising the question whether their concepts persist beyond individual repositories. In this paper, we apply established empirical software engineering techniques to analyse a set of foundational quantum software repositories. We combine cross-repository activity with contributor relationships to study the evolution of communities. Our analysis provides empirical evidence of contributor migration patterns and indications of cross-project knowledge transfer. We observe multiple development paths: projects may evolve into sustained communities, contributors may integrate concepts into established ecosystems, or activity may continue through new and follow-up software artefacts. Our observations provide an initial empirical perspective on how concepts and influence persist across repository boundaries in quantum software ecosystems.

</details>


### [201] [Input Relation Prompting for Metamorphic Testing on Query-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25603)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Shin-Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对查询系统缺乏验证基准的测试难题，提出一种辅助测试者识别蜕变关系的蜕变测试方法，无需预定义测试用例和基准真值，可结合组合测试和模糊测试，案例验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询系统缺乏基准真值验证，手动测试耗时费力，现有方法过度依赖领域知识。

Method: 提出辅助测试者识别蜕变关系的方法，描述输入输出间的变换规则，通过与预期关系比对检测故障，不依赖预定义测试用例或基准真值。

Result: 真实Web应用案例研究证明了方法的适用性和潜力，可与其他测试方法（组合测试、模糊测试）结合。

Conclusion: 推进了蜕变测试领域发展，为查询系统测试者提供了提升测试效率的实用工具。

Abstract: Testing query-based systems (QBSs) presents significant challenges due to the absence of ground truth for validation and the extensive time and effort required for manual testing. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing an approach that assists testers in identifying metamorphic relations (MRs) for metamorphic testing (MT) instead of solely and exhaustively relying on prerequisite domain knowledge. MT is an approach rising in popularity employed to alleviate the oracle problem by applying input transformation rules (MRs) to a program. The proposed approach helps the tester by prompting MRs that describe the relationships between inputs and outputs, enabling fault detection when the expected relationship is not met. Unlike traditional testing approaches, this approach does not rely on pre-defined test cases or concrete ground truth, making it suitable for the testing of real-world QBSs. Furthermore, the proposed approach can be combined with other testing methods such as combinatorial testing and fuzz testing, expanding the possibilities for QBS testing. A conducted case study of a real-world web application demonstrates the applicability and potential of the proposed approach. Overall, this research contributes to advancing the field of metamorphic testing and provides a valuable tool for QBS testers to enhance their testing efficiency.

</details>


### [202] [SkillGate: Cost Efficient Runtime Malicious Skill File Detection in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25619)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文提出SkillGate，一种针对AI编程助手技能包（skill files）供应链攻击的安全网关。该工具通过混合正则预过滤+LLM评判器管道，在保持低误报率（FPR=1.13%）的同时，将LLM输入token减少77%，并在SkillsBench基准上达到F1=0.817，比现有工具AUPRC提升5-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程团队广泛部署AI编码助手（如Cursor、Claude Code、GitHub Copilot），并通过安装领域特定技能文件（Markdown格式）来定制代理行为。这些技能文件可通过简单的npx skills add命令从公共注册中心下载，且缺乏安全审查，构成了一种新型供应链攻击面：恶意技能文件可静默重编程代理行为，窃取凭证、向生成代码注入后门或将代理行为重定向至攻击者控制的端点。已有报告显示公共注册中心存在数百个恶意技能包，包括有组织的凭证窃取活动。

Method: SkillGate采用混合正则预过滤+LLM评判器管道（hybrid regex-prefilter + LLM-judge pipeline）：安全信号文件直接绕过LLM（跳过节省）；被标记的文件仅将匹配的片段窗口发送给评判器，而非完整内容（片段节省）。该方法在降低计算成本的同时保持检测有效性。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准（n=1,650，其中9.1%为恶意样本）上，SkillGate达到F1=0.817，FPR=1.13%，LLM输入token减少77%（相比全文件筛查），AUPRC（0.830）比现有工具（0.144/0.162）提升5-6倍。

Conclusion: SkillGate作为一种可部署的安全网关，有效填补了AI编程助手技能包供应链攻击防御的系统性空白，通过高效的混合检测管道实现了高检测率与低计算成本的平衡。

Abstract: Software engineering teams now deploy AI coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, GitHub Copilot) as first-class productivity tools, installing domain-specific skill files to tailor agent behavior to project APIs, framework conventions, and organizational workflows. These complex Markdown files are easily downloaded from public registries with a single npx skills add command and no real security screening, representing a novel supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill file can silently reprogram agent behavior, exfiltrating credentials, injecting backdoors into generated code, or redirecting agent actions to attacker-controlled endpoints. The threat is not hypothetical: recent reports document hundreds of malicious skill packages in public registries, including organized campaigns that distributed credential-stealing infostealers via fake productivity skills. No systematic toolchain defense exists for this attack surface. We present SkillGate, a deployable security gateway that screens AI skill packages before coding agent installation. SkillGate uses a hybrid regex-prefilter + LLM-judge pipeline: safe-signal files bypass the LLM entirely (skip savings); flagged files have only their matched snippet windows sent to the judge, not the full content (snippet savings). We answer four research questions covering detection effectiveness, screening cost, runtime overhead, and false positive behavior on the SkillsBench benchmark against two existing tools. On SkillsBench (n=1,650, 9.1% malicious), SkillGate achieves F1=0.817, FPR=1.13% while reducing LLM input tokens by 77% vs. full-file screening, and outperforming existing tools by 5-6x on threshold-independent AUPRC (0.830 vs. 0.144/0.162).

</details>


### [203] [An Empirical Study of Model Context Protocol Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25635)
*Muhammad Hamza Arshad Majeed,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对1,723个MCP应用（MCPApps）的大规模实证研究发现：85.2%使用文件配置服务器，81.1%使用官方SDK通信，但配置文件命名无统一规范；人机交互方面，90.8%记录日志、77.2%支持启用/禁用控制，但仅37.2%实现了工具执行的审批拦截机制，导致大多数应用中LLM可无条件调用任何已启用的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议标准化了LLM应用与外部工具的通信，但应用侧缺乏约定（配置、通信、人工监督），且现有研究集中在MCP服务器而非消费这些服务器的应用（MCPApps），该领域尚缺乏系统性实证研究。

Method: 从GitHub挖掘1,723个MCPApps，首先从代表性样本中归纳出MCPAppTax分类体系，然后利用LLM辅助的pipeline将该分类应用于全量数据集，从配置方式、SDK使用、人机交互机制三个维度进行特征分析。

Result: 1）配置方面：85.2%的MCPApps使用文件配置服务器，但无统一命名规范；2）SDK使用：81.1%采用官方SDK通信；3）人机交互方面：日志记录（90.8%）和启用/禁用控制（77.2%）普及，但仅37.2%的应用在工具执行前设置审批拦截，大多数应用中LLM可无条件调用任何已启用的工具。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统在某些实践上已形成共识（配置文件、官方SDK），但在配置命名规范和关键的人机交互安全机制（工具执行审批）上仍存在显著分歧和不足，缺乏审批步骤可能导致LLM不受约束地调用工具，构成安全隐患。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how large language model applications communicate with external tools, but leaves the application side unspecified: unlike traditional dependencies resolved through package managers, developers integrating MCP servers face no conventions for configuration, communication, or human oversight. This ecosystem is also under-researched, with existing work focused on servers rather than the applications consuming them. We conduct a large-scale study of 1,723 MCPApps mined from GitHub. We first derive MCPAppTax from a representative sample, then use an LLM-assisted pipeline to apply it across the full dataset, characterizing server integration across configuration, SDK use, and human-in-the-loop mechanisms. Our results show that the ecosystem has converged on some practices but not others: most MCPApps configure servers using files (85.2%) and use an official SDK (81.1%) to communicate with servers, yet no naming convention has emerged for configuration files. Human oversight diverges most, logging (90.8%) and enable/disable controls (77.2%) are common, but only 37.2% gate tool execution behind a blocking approval step, leaving the LLM able to invoke any enabled tool unconditionally in most MCPApps.

</details>


### [204] [KQFuzz: Knowledge-Guided Fuzzing for Quantum Libraries via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25647)
*Fuyuan Xia,Qixin Zhang,Chenhao Ying,Haojin Zhu,Shuai Wang,Yuan Luo,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KQFuzz是一种基于知识引导的量子库模糊测试工具，通过结合代码库知识、LLM测试生成、适应性引导评估和两级变异策略，在Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个量子库上实现了高达18.44%的覆盖率提升，并发现了13个已确认的bug（12个已修复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的量子库模糊测试方法存在灵活性不足和效率低下的问题，限制了量子计算领域的发展，需要一种更高效、更灵活的测试方法。

Method: KQFuzz提出了一种针对量子程序的新颖提示方案，策略性地融入代码库知识以高效生成高质量量子种子程序；同时开发了适应性引导评估和两级变异策略，用于处理生成的种子程序，促进高效的模糊测试执行并丰富测试用例的多样性。

Result: 在Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三个流行量子库上的实验表明，KQFuzz显著优于现有方法，覆盖率提升高达18.44%；发现了13个bug，全部已被确认，其中12个已被开发者修复。

Conclusion: KQFuzz通过知识引导的LLM测试生成与适应性变异相结合的方法，有效提升了量子库模糊测试的覆盖率和bug检测能力。

Abstract: As quantum computing continually improves, ensuring the reliability and correctness of quantum libraries has become increasingly critical. To this end, many LLM-based fuzzing approaches towards quantum libraries have been proposed to uncover potential bugs. However, these methods still suffer from limitations such as insufficient flexibility and low efficiency, which hinder the progress of the quantum computing field. To address these challenges, we propose KQFuzz, a novel knowledge-guided fuzzer for quantum libraries. It leverages comprehensive codebase knowledge to ground LLM-based test generation, synergizing this with fitness-guided evaluation and two-level mutations to explore complex execution paths and trigger potential bugs. Firstly, KQFuzz introduces a novel prompting scheme tailored to quantum programs, which strategically incorporates knowledge of the codebase to efficiently generate high-quality quantum seed programs. Moreover, we develop evaluation and mutation strategies to handle the generated seed programs, facilitating efficient fuzzing execution while further enriching the diversity of the resulting test cases. We implement KQFuzz and conduct fuzzing on three popular quantum libraries, including Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, with coverage improved by up to 18.44%. During the development of KQFuzz, we discovered 13 bugs, all of which have been confirmed and 12 have already been fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [205] [Delta Debugging for Cyber-Physical Systems with Flaky Test Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25695)
*Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出三种适用于随机CPS系统的delta调试算法，通过结合统计失败分析、重复执行和环境感知缩减，在工业电梯调度系统和自主移动机器人上验证，不仅有效隔离最小失败诱发输入，还提高了失败的可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CPS模拟器常表现出非确定性（易变）行为，导致故障难以复现和调试。传统delta调试方法在确定性系统中有效，但其基本假设在随机环境中不成立。

Method: 提出三种delta调试算法，结合统计失败分析、重复执行和环境感知缩减技术，用于在随机CPS中隔离最小化的失败诱发测试输入。

Result: 在工业电梯调度系统和自主移动机器人上的实验表明，所提方法显著减少了调试时间，同时保留了原始失败行为。更重要的是，最小化失败诱发输入后，失败的可复现性相比原始执行有所提高。

Conclusion: Delta调试不仅简化了失败分析，还能缓解执行的易变性问题，为CPS调试提供了实用基础。通过消除引入偶然随机效应的执行片段，缩减后的测试输入能以更高概率一致地复现失败。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is widely used to validate Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet modern CPS simulators frequently exhibit non-deterministic (flaky) behavior, making failures difficult to reproduce and debug. Although delta debugging has proven effective for deterministic systems, its underlying assumptions do not hold in stochastic environments. This paper presents three delta debugging algorithms that combine statistical failure analysis, repeated executions, and environment-aware reduction to isolate minimal failure-inducing test inputs for stochastic CPSs. We evaluate the proposed techniques on two complementary case study systems: an industrial elevator dispatching system employing stochastic optimization and an autonomous mobile robot exhibiting simulator-induced non-determinism. The results show that the proposed approaches substantially reduce debugging time while preserving the original failure behavior. More importantly, we observe that minimizing failure-inducing test inputs frequently increases failure reproducibility compared with the original executions. By eliminating execution segments that introduce incidental stochastic effects, the reduced test inputs isolate the causal conditions of the failure and consistently reproduce it with higher probability. These findings suggest that delta debugging not only simplifies failure analysis but also mitigates execution flakiness, providing a practical foundation for debugging CPSs.

</details>


### [206] [WarmTuner: Program-Specific Warm Starts for Compiler Autotuning via Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25831)
*Tianlu Qiao,Mingxuan Zhu,Zeyu Sun,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WarmTuner是一个离线到在线的强化学习框架，通过学习历史数据中的程序条件策略来预测编译器优化标志设置，并在目标程序上通过实际编译运行反馈进行在线优化，平均加速比达1.732倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编译器自动调优方法在搜索开始后其从历史数据中提取的知识便冻结，运行时反馈仅引导搜索本身而无法更新先验知识，导致先验与目标程序不匹配时浪费大量在线预算。

Method: 提出WarmTuner框架：离线阶段从历史优秀配置学习基于程序条件的全标志空间策略；在线阶段使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)在目标程序上基于实际编译运行反馈更新策略，无需单独的价值模型。

Result: 在GCC 15.2.0的cBench和PolyBench上，WarmTuner相比GCC -O3平均加速1.732x，在14/30的程序上取得最佳结果，显著优于对比方法。

Conclusion: WarmTuner通过将离线知识转化为可在在线阶段持续适应的策略，有效解决了先验知识冻结问题，提升了编译器自动调优的性能。

Abstract: Compilers are fundamental software tools that translate high-level programs into machine code. Modern compilers expose hundreds of optimizations, each turned on or off through an optimization flag, to improve the performance of the generated code. However, the number of possible flag combinations grows exponentially, making it difficult to find a flag configuration well suited to a given target program. Existing compiler auto-tuning techniques reduce tuning cost by pruning the search space, injecting search biases, or predicting configuration performance. Although some exploit program features, the knowledge they extract from historical data is frozen once search begins; runtime feedback then guides only the search itself, never the prior. As a result, when this prior mismatches the target program, these methods waste much of the limited online budget before the search reaches good configurations. We propose WarmTuner, an offline-to-online reinforcement learning framework that instead turns historical records into a program-conditioned policy that predicts each flag's setting over the full flag space and remains adaptable on the target program. Offline, WarmTuner learns this program-conditioned policy over the full flag space from historical good configurations. Online, it refines the same policy on the target program using real compile-run feedback, so that the policy is driven by measured speedups rather than limited to the historical data. We instantiate the online update with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which compares candidates in the same round and avoids a separate value model. We evaluate WarmTuner on GCC 15.2.0 with cBench and PolyBench. The results show that WarmTuner achieves an average speedup of 1.732x over GCC -O3 and obtains the best result on 14/30 programs, significantly outperforming the compared techniques.

</details>


### [207] [Rethinking Training Data for Generating Code Review Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25851)
*Leonardo Centellas-Claros,Estefania Pakarati-Cofre,Juan Pablo Sandoval Alcocer,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现代码评审评论生成任务中训练数据存在非对齐问题（语义模糊、缺乏可操作性、上下文依赖），并通过实证研究建立其分类体系。研究表明，基于LLM的过滤方法虽能检测部分问题，但提升评论生成质量不仅需要数据清洗，更需要重新审视任务定义、引入显式有效性标准、丰富上下文输入并与评审意图对齐的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的代码评论生成方法产生的结果往往过于泛化、缺乏依据或不可操作；同时，评审评论数据集存在噪声或不适合训练的问题，促使研究者采用基于LLM的数据清洗方法。然而，本文认为问题训练实例并非同质的，其局限性根源在于任务定义本身存在更深层次的问题。

Method: 对广泛使用的评审评论数据集进行实证检验，识别出"不对齐的训练对"（即代码变更与评审评论之间的关系无法为生成可从局部输入出发的可操作反馈提供可靠学习信号），并归纳出三类不对齐来源（语义模糊、缺乏可操作性、上下文依赖）的分类体系；进一步探讨将该分类体系融入基于LLM的过滤方法能否提升问题训练实例的识别效果。

Result: 识别并分类了评审评论数据集中存在的三类"不对齐"训练实例；发现即使融入所提出的分类体系，基于LLM的过滤方法在检测不对齐训练对方面仍面临挑战。

Conclusion: 改进代码评审评论生成不能仅依赖数据集清洗，还需要建立显式的有效性标准、提供更丰富的上下文输入，以及采用与评审意图和可操作性对齐的评估实践。
