Abstract: Generating code review comments has become a prominent research direction in automated code review, commonly formulated as a text generation task over diff-comment pairs. Despite advances in learning-based approaches, generated review comments are often generic, weakly grounded, or non-actionable. Recent studies have also shown that review comment datasets contain noisy or unsuitable training instances, motivating LLM-based dataset cleaning approaches. In this paper, we argue that problematic training instances are not homogeneous and that some limitations stem from deeper issues in the task formulation itself. Through an empirical inspection of a widely used review comment dataset, we identify misaligned training pairs: instances where the relationship between the code change and the review comment does not provide a reliable learning signal for generating actionable review feedback from localized inputs. We derive a taxonomy of misalignment capturing three recurring sources: semantic ambiguity, lack of actionability, and context dependence. We further explore whether incorporating this taxonomy into LLM-based filtering improves the identification of problematic training instances, observing that detecting misaligned training pairs remains challenging. Based on these observations, we argue that improving review comment generation requires more than dataset cleaning alone, motivating explicit validity criteria, richer contextual inputs, and evaluation practices aligned with review intent and actionability.

</details>


### [208] [How Do LLMs Read Bug Reports? An Empirical Study of Attention in LLMs for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25873)
*Ramtin Ehsani,Irene Manotas,Saurabh Pujar,Luca Buratti,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于LLM的自动程序修复中的注意力模式进行实证研究，通过对319个真实世界bug的分析，发现成功修复的特征是注意力分散在多个诊断组件上，而失败修复往往过度聚焦于元数据，且模型注意力与开发者关注区域的对齐程度与修复成功率正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的自动程序修复系统发展迅速，但性能不稳定——相同上下文下LLM能修复一个bug却对另一个相关bug失败，其内在原因尚不明确，尤其是模型如何优先处理bug报告中的多样信息以及注意力是否影响修复成功。

Method: 对SWE-bench Verified和Multi-SWE-bench中319个真实世界Python和Java bug进行分析，通过三个研究问题考察：(RQ1)模型注意力在bug报告各部分的分布情况；(RQ2)成功与失败修复的注意力模式差异；(RQ3)这些模式与开发者认为重要的信息之间的对比。

Result: 成功修复的特征是注意力分散在bug描述、堆栈跟踪和测试用例等多个诊断组件上；失败修复则表现出注意力过度集中于版本信息等元数据。模型注意力与开发者识别的关键部分和短语的对齐程度越高，修复成功率越高。

Conclusion: 提供了首个实证证据表明注意力分配不当是基于LLM的APR失败的关键因素，为设计更可解释、更可靠的未来APR系统提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair systems are advancing rapidly, yet their performance remains inconsistent. Even when provided with the same contextual information, an LLM may generate a correct patch for one bug but fail on another closely related bug. Why this happens remains poorly understood, and it is unclear how LLMs prioritize the diverse information in bug reports and whether model attention affects repair success. In this paper, we present the first empirical study of attention patterns in LLM-based program repair, providing interpretable insights into how models process bug reports and where their attention is concentrated during repair. We analyze 319 real-world Python and Java bugs from SWE-bench Verified and Multi-SWE-bench to study (RQ1) how model attention is distributed across bug report sections, (RQ2) how attention patterns within each section differ between successful and unsuccessful repairs, and (RQ3) how these patterns compare to information developers consider important for bug fixing. We find that successful repairs are characterized by diffused attention across multiple diagnostic components such as bug descriptions, stacktraces, and test cases, while failures often exhibit over-localized attention toward metadata such as version information. We further observe that stronger alignment between model attention and developer-identified key sections and phrases is associated with higher repair success. Our results provide the first empirical evidence that attention misallocation is a key factor in LLM-based APR failures, and offer actionable insights for designing more interpretable and reliable future APR systems.

</details>


### [209] [CONQuER: Hardware-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantisation with Online-Calibrated Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25884)
*Aidan Dakhama,Ajitha Rajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CONQuER是一个统一编译器集成的硬件感知混合精度量化框架，将量化过程移至编译器管道中的TOSA级别，结合NSGA-II进化算法与双代理预筛选引擎，在资源受限硬件上发现帕累托最优配置，实现最高12.19倍加速，top-1准确率仅下降1.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合精度量化流程严重碎片化：量化作为前端框架中与硬件无关的预处理步骤，与下游编译器分离，导致位宽分配与目标硬件的异构执行单元（如张量核心、可变宽度向量单元）不匹配，造成严重的运行时执行惩罚；且通过穷举硬件在环测试评估配置因搜索空间指数级增长而不可行。

Method: 将量化移至编译器管道中的TOSA级别；耦合NSGA-II进化算法与双代理预筛选引擎（评估理论缓存内存边界和特征空间各向同性以丢弃不可行配置）；仅将最强候选策略在IREE硬件上执行，通过在线校准器将执行指标反馈给代理模型。

Result: 在移动CPU、笔记本电脑CPU和服务器GPU上的评估表明，最优量化策略具有硬件依赖性。CONQuER发现的帕累托最优配置实现了最高12.19倍推理加速，top-1准确率在未量化基线的1.44%以内。

Conclusion: 将量化与编译器降级和物理执行耦合能够发现硬件依赖的最优量化策略，有效解决当前量化流程碎片化问题。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained hardware relies on mixed-precision quantisation (MPQ). current deployment toolchains severely fragment this process. Quantisation typically occurs as a hardware-agnostic preprocessing step in front-end frameworks, disconnected from the downstream compilers that generate the physical machine code. This separation leads to suboptimal configurations where assigned bit-widths map poorly to the target machine's heterogeneous hardware execution blocks such as tensor cores and variable-width vector units, incurring severe runtime execution penalties. Furthermore, evaluating these configurations via exhaustive hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testing is intractable due to the exponentially large search space. We present CONQuER, a unified compiler-integrated infrastructure for hardware-aware MPQ. CONQuER shifts quantisation into the compiler pipeline at the TOSA level, enabling intelligent configuration handling based on compiler support. To evaluate this combinatorial search space of different of model layers within practical compilation budgets, CONQuER couples an NSGA-II evolutionary algorithm with a dual-surrogate prescreening engine. This engine evaluates theoretical cache memory bounds and feature space isotropy to discard non-viable configurations. CONQuER then executes only the strongest candidate policies on hardware via IREE, feeding the execution metrics into an online calibrator. This calibrator aligns the surrogate models with the true hardware behaviour during an NSGA-II evolutionary search. Evaluation across mobile and laptop CPUs, and server GPUs demonstrates that optimal quantisation policies are hardware-dependent. By coupling quantisation with compiler lowering and physical execution, CONQuER discovers Pareto-optimal configurations up to 12.19x faster inference with top-1 accuracy within 1.44% of the unquantised baseline.

</details>


### [210] [RSIBench-Data: Benchmarking Data-Centric Research for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25886)
*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Haocheng Lu,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RSIBench-Data是一个可控的基准测试，用于评估LLM agent作为数据驱动研究者的能力。在固定后训练栈下，agent迭代修正训练数据策略。测试表明，agent在58.33%的设置中能通过反馈改进策略，但改进不稳定：78.26%的持续搜索以更低分数结束。四种强运行模式包括准确假设、验证驱动监督、行为对齐数据和强检查点保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进需要将模型失败证据转化为更好的模型，但现有基准将研究决策与优化、服务、评估和系统实现混在一起，模糊了agent的研究能力。因此需要一个可控的基准来专门评估LLM agent作为数据驱动研究者的能力。

Method: 设计RSIBench-Data基准，使用固定的后训练栈（Tinker用于训练和服务，Harbor和E2B沙箱用于评估），让agent迭代修正训练数据策略。在软件工程、终端使用、科学问答和数学六个基准上评估四个前沿agent。

Result: agent在58.33%的设置中能从反馈中改进策略；但在持续搜索中，78.26%最终尝试分数低于最佳观察分数。识别出四种强运行模式：准确假设、验证驱动监督、行为对齐数据和强检查点保留。

Conclusion: 当前agent能做出有用的数据驱动发现，但无法将反馈转化为一致的改进。RSIBench-Data为递归自我改进所需的研究能力提供了可测量、可审计的测试平台。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires turning evidence of model failures into better models. Data-centric post-training research entails diagnosing capability gaps, designing and validating training-data strategies, and learning from checkpoint feedback. Can LLM agents automate this loop? Existing benchmarks entangle research decisions with optimization, serving, evaluation, and systems implementation, obscuring agents' research capability. We introduce RSIBench-Data, a controlled benchmark of LLM agents as data-centric researchers with a fixed post-training stack. Agents iteratively revise training-data strategies for a fixed target model; training and serving use Tinker-backed services, official evaluation runs through Harbor and E2B sandboxes, and budgets are fixed across agents. We evaluate four frontier agents on six benchmarks across software engineering, terminal use, scientific question answering, and mathematics. Agents demonstrate core data-centric research capabilities: in 58.33\% of settings, they improve upon the first valid attempt by refining strategies from feedback. However, improvement is inconsistent. Among searches continuing after the best observed score, 78.26\% end with a lower-scoring final attempt, while the rest only recover the same peak. A strong candidate may therefore appear early or midway through a run even as later revisions fail. Trajectory analysis identifies four patterns in stronger runs: accurate hypotheses, validation-grounded supervision, behavior-aligned data, and preservation of strong checkpoints. These findings suggest that current agents can make useful data-centric discoveries but cannot yet translate feedback into consistent improvements. RSIBench-Data provides a measurable, auditable testbed for the research capabilities required for recursive self-improvement. We open-source our code at https://github.com/evolvent-ai/RSIBench-Data.

</details>


### [211] [A Low-Cost Human-in-the-Loop Investigation of Toxicity on GitHub at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25946)
*Rahat Rizvi Rahman,Mia Mohammad Imran,Kostadin Damevski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协同的毒性标注方法（HITL），通过小型本地LLM生成毒性预测和可解释的事件类别分数，再由轻量级随机森林验证器筛选出最可能误标的样本进行人工复审。该方法应用于超过12.4万个GitHub讨论，验证并修正了先前小规模研究的部分结论，提供了关于开源项目中毒性互动的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub上的毒性互动会削弱开源社区的贡献意愿和项目可持续性，但先前关于GitHub毒性的实证研究规模有限，难以推广。由于GitHub上的毒性常常是隐式和依赖上下文的，完全人工标注和基于LLM的标注都不可靠，因此需要一种可大规模扩展且领域校准的毒性标注方法。

Method: 提出人机协同（HITL）标注流程：1) 使用小型本地LLM生成毒性预测和一组可解释的事件类别分数；2) 轻量级随机森林验证器基于这些分数识别最可能被误标的小部分对话；3) 仅对这部分样本进行人工审查，从而在控制成本的同时提高标注质量。该验证器在性能上优于基于置信度和多LLM的基线方法。

Result: 将该流程应用于超过12.4万个GitHub issue和pull request对话。基于生成的数据集，评估了先前小规模研究的关键发现，确认了部分结论，也修正了另一些结论。

Conclusion: 提供了关于开源项目中毒性互动的普遍性、特征和动态的新见解，证明了所提出的HITL方法能够在大规模、领域校准的毒性标注中高效实用。

Abstract: Toxic interactions in open source discussions can alienate contributors and threaten project sustainability, yet prior empirical studies of GitHub toxicity have been limited in scale, raising questions about their generalizability. Scaling up is difficult because toxicity on GitHub is often implicit and context-dependent, making both fully manual annotation and LLM-based labeling unreliable.
  We present a human-in-the-loop (HITL) annotation methodology that makes large-scale, domain-calibrated toxicity labeling practical. A single call to a small, local LLM produces both a toxicity prediction and a set of interpretable event category scores. A lightweight Random Forest validator then uses those scores to flag the small subset of conversations most likely to be mislabeled, directing human review only where it is needed. The validator outperforms confidence-based and multi-LLM baselines while adding low annotation cost.
  We apply this pipeline to over 124,000 GitHub issue and pull request conversations. Using the resulting dataset, we evaluate key findings from prior small-scale research, confirming some and qualifying others, and present new insights into the prevalence, characteristics, and dynamics of toxicity across diverse open source projects.

</details>


### [212] [RepoReasoner: Evaluating Repository-Level Code Reasoning Ability of Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25996)
*Yanlin Wang,Suiquan Wang,Yanli Wang,Bowen Zhang,Daya Guo,Jiachi Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了RepoReasoner基准，用于评估大语言模型在仓库级别的代码推理能力，包含输出预测和调用链预测两个任务。实验表明，即使最优模型在输出预测上仅达69.1% Pass@1，且模型存在依赖记忆而非真正推理、长上下文引入噪声等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多局限于函数级别的代码推理评估，无法反映真实开发中跨文件、跨依赖的复杂场景，因此需要构建仓库级别的代码推理基准。

Method: 通过多阶段pipeline构建RepoReasoner基准：利用pytest动态追踪获取真实调用链，结合LLM-based I/O重写减少记忆效应。包含输出预测（细粒度、跨文件执行推理）和调用链预测（高层架构依赖理解）两个互补任务。

Result: 7个先进LLM中，最优模型在输出预测上仅达69.1% Pass@1；调用链预测中模型高精度低召回；重写数据上性能下降表明存在记忆依赖；长上下文因噪声未能稳定提升效果。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在仓库级别代码推理上存在根本性局限，未来工作应关注结构化架构理解和跨文件推理。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on software engineering tasks, yet most existing benchmarks evaluate code reasoning at the function level, where all relevant information is localized. This setting fails to reflect real-world development, which requires reasoning across multiple files and complex dependency structures. We introduce RepoReasoner, a benchmark for evaluating repository-level code reasoning. It assesses two complementary abilities: Output Prediction, which measures fine-grained, stateful execution reasoning across files, and Call Chain Prediction, which evaluates high-level architectural dependency understanding under noisy context. Our benchmark is constructed through a multi-stage pipeline that leverages dynamic tracing of pytest executions to obtain ground-truth call chains, along with LLM-based I/O rewriting to reduce memorization effects. We evaluate seven state-of-the-art LLMs. Even under oracle context, the best-performing model achieves only 69.1% Pass@1 on Output Prediction, indicating that cross-file reasoning remains a major challenge. In Call Chain Prediction, models exhibit high precision but low recall, suggesting limited multi-hop dependency understanding. Furthermore, performance drops on rewritten data reveal partial reliance on memorization, and longer contexts do not consistently improve results due to noise. These findings highlight fundamental limitations in current LLMs' repository-level reasoning and motivate future work on structured architectural understanding and cross-file inference.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [DocAnnot -- Accelerating the Creation of Key Information Extraction Datasets with GenAI-Powered Auto-annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24745)
*Siddartha Reddy,Harikrishnan P M,Goutham Vignesh,Varun V,Vishal Vaddina*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DocAnnot是一种利用LVLM、OCR和SICM算法自动生成KIE数据集标注的框架，在CORD和SROIE上分别达到0.679和0.846的F1分数，能显著减少人工标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KIE数据集创建需要大量人工标注，耗时耗力，亟需一种自动化方法加速数据集生成过程，降低对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出DocAnnot框架，结合大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)进行标签值提取、OCR进行文本和边界框检测、以及新颖的空间信息上下文匹配(SICM)算法——通过结合空间关系、邻近度分析和文本匹配来改进标签-值关联。

Result: 在CORD和SROIE基准上自动生成标注的F1分数分别为0.679和0.846。使用自动标注数据微调下游KIE模型，LayoutLMv3在CORD上达到0.6765的F1分数。

Conclusion: DocAnnot能显著减少人工标注工作量，但尚不能完全替代人工干预；通过将过程自动化到审阅者可以有效精炼输出的程度，相比从零开始手动标注，能以更高效率实现近乎完美的标注，在资源受限场景和快速模型原型开发中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Key Information Extraction (KIE) is vital for many document applications, but creating training datasets is traditionally a time-consuming manual process. We introduce DocAnnot, a framework that significantly accelerates KIE dataset generation. DocAnnot leverages a Large Vision Language Model (LVLM) for label value extraction, OCR for text/bounding box detection, and a novel Spatially Informed Contextual Matching (SICM) algorithm. SICM improves label-value association by combining spatial relationships and proximity analysis with textual matching. We evaluate our framework on the CORD and SROIE benchmarks, demonstrating its ability to auto-generate annotations with F1-scores of 0.679 and 0.846, respectively. Furthermore, we investigate the effectiveness of using auto-annotated data for fine-tuning downstream KIE models. While human-annotated data remains superior, models trained exclusively on DocAnnot's outputs attain respectable performance (e.g., LayoutLMv3 achieving an F1-score of 0.6765 on CORD). These results show that while our framework significantly reduces reliance on manual effort, it does not yet fully eliminate the need for human intervention. However, by automating the process to a point where reviewers can efficiently refine outputs, our system enables near-perfect annotations with much greater efficiency than manual annotation from scratch. This approach offers substantial time and cost savings, making it valuable for resource-constrained settings and rapid model prototyping.

</details>


### [214] [VLD-RAG: Agentic Vision-Language Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long, Visually-Rich Multi-Page Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24748)
*Seonok Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VLD-RAG是一个面向视觉丰富型长文档的多模态RAG框架，通过混合检索（关键词+密集语义）和验证器驱动的智能体协作（检索、回答、验证三个智能体），实现跨页面的证据检索与推理，在LongDocURL和MMLongBench-Doc基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富型文档（如报告、幻灯片、手册）中，答案所需的证据往往分散在多个页面，且混合了文本、布局、表格、图表等多种模态信息。现有RAG方法难以有效检索跨页面的多模态证据并进行跨页推理，因此需要一种能够同时处理文本和视觉信号、支持多页证据检索和跨页推理的框架。

Method: VLD-RAG包含三个核心组件：(1) 页面级多模态索引——存储解析文本、页面元数据和密集视觉表示；(2) 混合检索策略——结合关键词稀疏搜索与密集语义查询，识别候选源和证据页面；(3) 验证器引导的智能体工作流——协调检索智能体、回答智能体和验证智能体，扩大证据覆盖、检测缺失引用并在必要时优化检索请求。

Result: 在LongDocURL和MMLongBench-Doc基准上，VLD-RAG在Top-1和Top-5证据页面准确率以及最终问答的广义准确率上均优于现有的基于视觉的检索基线方法。

Conclusion: 协调的智能体验证与多模态混合检索对于视觉丰富型长文档的可靠证据检索和回答至关重要，尤其当正确答案依赖于分散在多个页面上的证据时。

Abstract: Visually-rich documents such as reports, slides, and manuals often distribute the evidence needed to answer a question across multiple pages, mixing text with layout cues, tables, charts, and figures. This work studies multimodal retrieval-augmented generation for question answering over such visually-rich long documents, where retrieval must select evidence pages that include both textual and visual signals. We present VLD-RAG, an agentic multimodal RAG framework for multi-page evidence retrieval and cross-page reasoning over long documents. VLD-RAG builds a page-preserving multimodal index that stores parsed text, page-level metadata, and dense visual representations, and uses a hybrid retrieval strategy that combines keyword-based sparse search with dense semantic queries to identify candidate sources and evidence pages. A verifier-guided agent workflow coordinates a Retrieval Agent, Answer Agent, and Validation Agent to broaden evidence coverage, detect missing citations, and refine retrieval requests when needed. We evaluate retrieval with Top-1 and Top-5 evidence-page accuracy and generation with generalized accuracy, and show that VLD-RAG improves both evidence-page retrieval and end-task question answering on visually-rich long-document benchmarks, including LongDocURL and MMLongBench-Doc, outperforming previous vision-based retrieval baselines. These findings highlight that coordinated agent verification and multimodal hybrid retrieval are crucial for reliable grounding when correct answers depend on evidence scattered across pages.

</details>


### [215] [CHaystack: Benchmarking Chinese Document Retrieval and VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24760)
*Hanxi Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHaystack，一个面向中文大规模文档检索与问答的基准数据集，涵盖四类文档；同时提出CDocRAG系统，利用VLM相关性过滤器优化检索结果。实验表明Qwen系列模型在文本密集型文档上表现最优，中文文本编码是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档VQA基准主要面向英文，缺乏专门针对中文大规模文档检索与问答的基准，难以全面评估中文文档理解系统。

Method: 构建CHaystack基准数据集（含学术论文、广告、网页、实拍文档四类），提出CDocRAG系统（在检索后使用VLM-based相关性过滤器验证文档图像相关性再生成答案）。

Result: Qwen系列在文本密集型（网页、论文）上最优，Qwen3-VL Recall@1达71.91，最佳非Qwen模型仅14.40；其他模型仅在视觉密集型（广告）上有竞争力，在文本密集型文档上性能剧降。

Conclusion: CHaystack的核心挑战在于中文文本编码，中文大规模文档VQA仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has made substantial progress in extending the memory of large language models (LLMs), and recent advances have further pushed RAG from pure text settings toward multimodal scenarios. In the document understanding domain, document visual question answering (DocumentVQA) has evolved from question answering over a single document to retrieval-and-generation pipelines over large-scale document collections. However, a benchmark specifically designed for Chinese large-scale document retrieval and question answering is still lacking. To bridge this gap, we introduce CHaystack, a new Chinese DocumentVQA benchmark that covers four document categories, namely academic papers, advertisements, web pages, and real-world photographed documents, enabling a more comprehensive evaluation of DocumentVQA systems. In addition, we present CDocRAG, a Chinese DocumentVQA system that uses a VLM-based relevance filter to verify retrieved document images before answer generation. We evaluate representative open-source embedding and generation models on CHaystack. The results reveal a clear contrast in category-wise strengths: Qwen-family models perform best on text-rich documents such as webpages and papers, whereas other models only achieve competitive results on visually rich categories such as advertisements and degrade sharply on text-dense documents. For retrieval, Qwen3-VL reaches 71.91 Recall@1 while the best non-Qwen model achieves only 14.40. These results indicate that the core challenge of CHaystack lies in Chinese textual encoding, and that Chinese large-scale DocumentVQA still leaves substantial room for improvement. Our code and dateset is available at https://github.com/hanxi19/CHaystack.

</details>


### [216] [The Effect of Text Chunk Size on Retrieval-Augmented Generation Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24767)
*German Garrido-Lestache Belinchon,Hugo Garrido-Lestache Belinchon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了RAG系统中文档分块粒度（块大小）和检索数量对生成质量与检索效率的影响，发现较小的块（如句子）可提升检索精度但可能损害连贯性，较大的块（如章节）能提供更全面信息但引入噪声和计算开销，并指出块大小与检索数量需协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中文档分块粒度（chunk size）对生成质量、上下文正确性、检索精度和计算效率有显著影响，但该因素目前缺乏系统性研究，常被随意选择而未评估其影响。

Method: 通过对比不同块大小（从小粒度句子到大粒度章节）及不同检索数量配置，评估对生成质量和检索有效性的影响。

Result: 小块（如句子）可提升检索精度但可能限制生成连贯性；大块（如章节）提供更多信息但引入噪声和计算成本，且需考虑检索返回的块数量。

Conclusion: 文档分块的粒度是影响RAG系统性能与效率的关键因素，块大小与检索数量需协同优化以达到最佳效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems have emerged as a powerful process for allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve relevant information to use as source material during text generation. A critical yet under-explored component of these systems is the granularity at which source documents are segmented into retrievable chunks. The size of these chunks has the potential to significantly influence generation quality, contextual correctness, retrieval precision, and computational efficiency. Despite its importance, chunk size is often selected without proper evaluation of its impact on generation quality. Smaller chunks, such as individual sentences, may allow for precise retrieval by narrowing the focus of each chunk. However, they contain less information, which may limit the model's ability to generate coherent responses. Larger chunks, such as entire chapters, contain lots of broad information that may improve correctness, but also introduce additional noise and increase computational cost. Because larger chunks contain more information, the number of chunks returned to the model must also be considered. This paper evaluates how chunk size, along with the number of retrieved segments, influences generation quality and retrieval effectiveness. By comparing these configurations, this study seeks to better understand how document segmentation affects the performance and efficiency of Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems. segmentation affects the performance and efficiency of Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems.

</details>


### [217] [JKO-RAG: Distributional Retrieval as Wasserstein Free-Energy Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24776)
*Levi Segal,Murari Ambati*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出JKO重排序方法，将RAG中的重排序问题建模为Wasserstein-2梯度流下的自由能最小化，通过线性响应理论解释了Wasserstein几何相对于KL散度的优势，并在BEIR基准上显著超越交叉编码器，尤其在鲁棒性方面提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG流水线返回排序列表，但下游语言模型实际以集合为条件，这种不匹配导致排序问题本质上具有几何特性，现有方法忽略了嵌入空间的语义几何结构。

Method: 将重排序建模为在Wasserstein-2梯度流下最小化自由能泛函 F(p)=relevance+entropy+redundancy，通过Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto近端方案求解。核心贡献是线性响应理论，证明Wasserstein与KL检索映射的唯一区别在于近端Hessian：W²是稠密且几何感知的，KL是对角线且几何盲的，这种差异抑制了查询改写引起的质量传输。还提出了四个扩展：学习地面度量的NMJKO、W²-KL插值的BWJKO、2倍加速的SAMJKO、以及基于OT对偶势的置信度信号DualRank。

Result: 在5个BEIR基准上，JKO全面超越交叉编码器；决定性优势是鲁棒性——在改写场景下稳定性提升22-38%，泄露的干扰项减少2倍。通过自由能下降、频率分辨的扰动响应、预测的步长h依赖性以及认证半径分析验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 将重排序重新定义为几何最优传输问题而非列表排序问题，通过线性响应理论揭示了Wasserstein几何的核心优势，为RAG管道提供了更鲁棒的检索机制。

Abstract: RAG pipelines return a \emph{ranked list} of passages. We argue this is a mismatch: the downstream language model conditions on a \emph{set}, and the selection problem is fundamentally geometric. We propose \jko, which frames reranking as minimising a free-energy functional $F(p)=\text{relevance}+\text{entropy}+\text{redundancy}$ under Wasserstein-2 gradient flow via the Jordan--Kinderlehrer--Otto proximal scheme. The ground metric $C_{ij}=(1-\cos\langle z_i,z_j\rangle)^2$ encodes the semantic geometry of the embedding manifold. Our central contribution is a \emph{linear-response theory} explaining \emph{why} the Wasserstein geometry helps: the Wasserstein and KL retrieval maps differ only in their proximal Hessian -- dense and geometry-aware for $W^2$, diagonal and geometry-blind for KL -- and this difference damps the mass transport that query paraphrase induces. The theory yields a falsifiable prediction: the stability advantage is monotonically decreasing in step size $h$. We verify this empirically via free-energy descent, frequency-resolved perturbation response, the predicted $h$-dependence, and a certified-radius analysis. Four extensions are introduced: \textbf{\nmjko} (learned ground metric), \textbf{\bwjko} ($W^2$--KL interpolation), \textbf{\samjko} ($2\times$ speedup), and \textbf{\dualrank} (OT dual potentials as confidence signals). Across five BEIR benchmarks, \jko\ outperforms the cross-encoder on all five; the decisive advantage is robustness -- 22--38\% more stable under paraphrase, $2\times$ fewer leaked distractors.

</details>


### [218] [Three Sides of Retrieval: Factorial Evidence for Document-Side, Query-Side, and Answer-Side Complementarity in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24781)
*Ng S. T. Chong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种零LLM成本的目录引导页面检索方法（ToC-guided page retrieval），通过视觉格式推断标题、构建并行索引并加载整页内容。在8份企业文档的1280个条件下实验发现：目录检索对答案质量有显著主效应（d=+0.41），与答案端验证结合优于查询端分解+验证，且与文档长度正相关。贡献在于方法论和实证层面——首次证明文档端、查询端和答案端增强之间存在三方互补交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的分块方式破坏文档结构信息，而基于标题的检索需要多次调用LLM且返回子块，效率低下。作者希望设计一种无需LLM调用的、保留文档结构信息的检索方法。

Method: 提出ToC-guided page retrieval：从视觉格式推断标题，构建目录作为并行索引，直接加载整页内容。在8份企业文档（5-195页）上进行1280个条件的受控实验，采用析因设计同时操纵文档端、查询端和答案端增强策略，并进行480个条件的敏感性分析。

Result: (1) ToC检索对答案质量主效应显著（d=+0.41, p=0.031），完整性（+0.40）和有用性（+0.40）提升最大；(2) 与答案端验证结合优于查询端分解+验证（d=+0.32, p=0.036）；(3) 仅增加2.9页/查询，却贡献20%的引用；(4) 在更长文档上效果更优（118页文档上d=+1.50）；(5) 敏感性分析确认默认参数接近最优。

Conclusion: 方法论上贡献了一种零LLM成本的检索算法；实证上通过析因实验首次发现文档端、查询端和答案端增强三方互补，三者交互作用此前未被研究过。

Abstract: RAG systems rely on chunking, which destroys structural information in documents. Existing heading-based retrieval (Jeong et al., 2025) requires multiple LLM calls per document and returns sub-chunks within matched sections. We introduce ToC-guided page retrieval, which infers headings from visual formatting without LLM calls, embeds them as a parallel index, and loads full page sections. Across 1,280 conditions on 8 enterprise documents (5 to 195 pages), we find: (1) ToC is a significant main effect on answer quality (d = +0.41, p = 0.031), with the largest gains in completeness (+0.40) and usefulness (+0.40); (2) combined with answer-side verification, it outperforms query-side decomposition + verification (d = +0.32, p = 0.036); (3) ToC contributes 20% of citations despite adding only 2.9 pages per query; (4) gains are directionally larger on longer documents (up to +1.50 on 118 pages), though the trend does not reach significance with 8 documents; and (5) a 480-condition sensitivity analysis finds no significant parameter effects (all p > 0.38), confirming defaults are near-optimal. The contribution is both methodological (a new zero-LLM-cost retrieval algorithm) and empirical: factorial evidence that document-side, query-side, and answer-side enhancements are complementary, a three-way interaction not previously studied.

</details>


### [219] [Unlocking Spatial Grounding in Large Audio-Visual Retrieval models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24786)
*Hugo Malard,Michel Olvera,Sanjeel Parekh,Gaël Richard,Slim Essid,Stéphane Lathuilière*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LAIP框架，通过轻量级音频引导空间池化（AiSP）模块替换全局聚合模块，从预训练的音频-视觉检索模型中恢复中间视觉token的细粒度空间信息，在不进行像素级监督的情况下实现高精度音源定位，在AVSBench和AVATAR上达到SOTA，后者性能近乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督音源定位中密集空间标注成本高昂，而现有大规模检索模型的全局对齐优化导致空间细节在上层丢失，亟需一种方法从预训练表征中恢复细粒度空间信息。

Method: 提出LAIP框架，核心为音频引导空间池化（AiSP）模块：利用帧对齐的音频特征作为查询，从预训练检索模型中间层的视觉token中恢复被全局池化丢弃的局部空间信息，替代标准的全局聚合模块。

Result: 在AVSBench和AVATAR数据集上达到SOTA，其中AVATAR上性能近乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 精确的音源定位无需从头学习，可直接从现有检索表征中解锁，为检索与定位任务提供统一路径。

Abstract: Weak supervision sets a practical regime for audio-visual sound source localization as dense spatial annotations are costly to obtain at scale. The task, however, remains challenging, as models must locate sound sources from temporally aligned audio-visual data without pixel-level supervision. Recent large-scale audio-visual retrieval models, trained at unprecedented scale, encode rich multimodal structure. We show their latent representations, though optimized for global alignment, can nonetheless enable fine-grained spatial grounding. While spatial detail is progressively lost in the upper layers of retrieval backbones due to global pooling, intermediate visual tokens retain highly structured spatial information. To exploit this, we introduce LAIP (\emph{Localization via Audio-Informed Pooling}), a framework that employs a lightweight \emph{Audio-informed Spatial Pooling} (AiSP) to replace the standard global aggregation module. By using frame-aligned audio to query intermediate visual tokens, LAIP recovers localized spatial information that is otherwise discarded by the frozen retrieval pipeline. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on AVSBench and AVATAR, nearly doubling previous results on the latter. These findings prove that accurate localization does not need to be learned from scratch; instead, it can be unlocked from existing retrieval representations, providing a unified path for both retrieval and localization tasks.

</details>


### [220] [NEXT: Reasoning-Driven Video Recommendation via a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24789)
*Yuming Liu,Hongye Yang,Harrison Zhao,Ellie Zhu,Bokai Cao,Lei Huang,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NEXT是一个基于推理驱动的视频推荐框架，通过"视频→意图→视频"的范式实现用户下一兴趣探索。其核心模型NEXT-8B（8B参数视觉语言模型）经三阶段训练（感知增强强化学习、分布对齐微调、组相对策略优化），在DocVQA任务上达到单模型最佳，部署于大规模社交媒体推荐系统后显著提升观看时长（+0.53%）和视频曝光多样性（+0.51%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推荐系统多基于共现关联或语义相似性，缺乏对用户观看视频后下一意图的显式推理能力。作者希望构建一个能够理解用户当前观看内容、推理其下一兴趣方向并检索匹配视频的推荐框架，实现从"物品共现"到"意图推理"的范式转变。

Method: 提出Item-to-Intent-to-Item框架：对显性续集（如剧集）直接链接，对隐性意图则生成意图查询并搜索匹配候选项。核心模型NEXT-8B采用三阶段训练：1）感知增强强化学习进行查询无关的证据提取；2）分布对齐监督微调融合真实与合成视觉QA数据；3）组相对策略优化进行最后一公里对齐。训练后模型用于生成意图查询和候选检索。

Result: NEXT-8B在DocVQA任务上达到单模型最佳性能，整体排名第二（仅次于多智能体系统），超越200B+参数的大规模模型。在下一意图逻辑质量评估中相对基础模型提升3.3%。部署于大规模社交媒体推荐系统后，观看时长提升0.53%，不同视频曝光量提升0.51%，均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: NEXT证明了经过精心训练的紧凑型视觉语言模型（8B参数）可以作为生产级规模下下一兴趣探索的实用推理引擎，在推荐系统中实现超越传统协同过滤和语义相似性范式的有向推荐能力。

Abstract: We present NEXT (Next-interest EXploration Transformer), a reasoning-driven video recommendation framework that reasons over the video a user has just watched, infers the viewer's next intent, and retrieves concrete follow-up videos. Explicit continuations such as episodes are linked directly; implicit cases are handled by generating intent queries and searching for matching candidates. This Item-to-Intent-to-Item formulation produces directed recommendations beyond co-engagement correlation or semantic similarity.
  To make this framework reliable at scale, we train NEXT-8B, a purpose-trained 8B vision-language model with a three-stage recipe: Perception-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for query-agnostic evidence extraction, Distribution-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning over real and synthetic visual QA mixtures, and Group Relative Policy Optimization for last-mile alignment. NEXT-8B achieves the best single-model DocVQA performance, ranking second overall only behind a multi-agent system while surpassing a substantially larger 200B+ scale model, and improves next-intent logic-wise quality by 3.3% over the base model in a task-specific LLM-as-a-judge evaluation.
  We deploy NEXT as an additional retrieval path in a large-scale social media recommendation system and observe statistically significant production gains, including +0.53% watch time and +0.51% distinct video exposure. Overall, NEXT shows that a carefully trained compact vision-language model can serve as a practical reasoning engine for next-interest exploration at production scale.

</details>


### [221] [From Naive RAG to Deep Agentic Retrieval: An Evolving Context Engineering Pipeline for Regulatory Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24791)
*Mishca de Costa,Muhammad Saleh Anwar,Dave Mercier,Issam Hammad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文追踪了安大略发电公司(OPG)生产级检索管道的演化过程，从朴素RAG到深度多智能体架构，提出了渐进式证据获取与成本感知升级(PEA-CAE)形式化架构，证明上下文工程比领域微调更经济可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语料库规模和查询复杂度的增长，朴素RAG实现面临硬性限制，需要更先进的检索架构来满足监管合规和费率分析场景的需求。

Method: 追踪了OPG生产管道的四个演化阶段：朴素RAG、混合检索重排序、智能体函数调用检索、深度多智能体架构（含代码工具合成与显式规划），并形式化为PEA-CAE架构。

Result: 上下文工程比领域微调在经济上更可行；深度智能体检索引入了自适应查询重构、渐进式文档发现和层级子智能体摘要等实用原语。

Conclusion: 现代企业级问答系统正从单次检索转向迭代证据获取与自适应规划，搜索性质日益显著。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is the dominant paradigm for applying large language models (LLMs) to enterprise document corpora, yet naive implementations encounter hard limits as corpus scale and query complexity grow. This paper traces the evolution of a production retrieval pipeline at Ontario Power Generation (OPG) for regulatory compliance and rate case analysis under Ontario Energy Board (OEB) reporting requirements. We examine successive stages: naive RAG, hybrid retrieval with re-ranking, agentic function-calling retrieval, and a deep multi-agent architecture with code-based tool synthesis and explicit planning, and identify the failure modes and tradeoffs that motivated each transition. We formalize the mature architecture as Progressive Evidence Acquisition with Cost-Aware Escalation (PEA-CAE): begin with low-cost, high-precision retrieval and escalate to full-document reads only when the expected evidence gain justifies latency and cost. Our findings show that context engineering is a more tractable and economically viable path than domain-specific fine-tuning for large, evolving regulatory corpora. More broadly, the progression toward deep agentic retrieval mirrors classical information retrieval ideas, introducing adaptive query reformulation, progressive document discovery, and hierarchical subagent summarization as practical system primitives. Operational traces further support the search-based nature of modern retrieval systems, where iterative evidence acquisition and adaptive planning increasingly replace single-pass retrieval as the foundation for enterprise-scale question answering.

</details>


### [222] [AI-Assisted Knowledge Access for Legacy Enterprise Asset Management in Energy Operations: A Practical Retrieval System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24792)
*Dave Mercier,Mishca de Costa,Muhammad Anwar,Mark Randall,Issam Hammad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检索助手系统，通过意图理解、查询重写、混合检索、上下文工程、生成式回答和超链接转换等方法，在遗留的企业资产管理平台上提升三类操作模式（供应商文档问答、ODS模式问答、UI使用问答）的知识检索效率。实验结果表明，Precision@5从0.56提升至0.72，MRR从0.43提升至0.58，nDCG@5从0.51提升至0.66，任务完成中位时间从14.2分钟降至8.3分钟，用户有用性和置信度均达到4.0/5.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源公用事业公司仍在使用寿命较长的企业资产管理平台，但替换这些平台成本高昂且操作上具有破坏性，因此需要实用的改进层来提升日常知识访问效率。

Method: 运行时方法包括意图理解、查询重写、混合语义与向量检索、令牌限制下的上下文工程、生成式答案生成以及面板标识符和引用文档的确定性超链接转换。数据准备管道侧重于语义丰富化，包括添加表和字段描述、跨源规范化缩写、索引代表性行级上下文等。

Result: Precision@5从0.56提升至0.72，MRR从0.43提升至0.58，nDCG@5从0.51提升至0.66。任务完成中位时间从14.2分钟降至8.3分钟，有用性和置信度均提升至4.0/5.0。

Conclusion: 意图理解和语义丰富化可以在遗留环境中提供有意义的操作价值，同时为未来的分析和自动化工具建立可复用的基础。

Abstract: Energy utilities still run engineering work management, engineering procurement, and inventory processes on long-lived enterprise asset management platforms. Replacing these platforms is often cost prohibitive and operationally disruptive, so practical improvement layers are required. This paper presents a retrieval assistant that improves day-to-day knowledge access across three operational modes: vendor documentation question answering, operational data store (ODS) schema question answering, and user interface usage and how-to question answering. The runtime method combines intent understanding, query rewriting, hybrid semantic and vector retrieval, context engineering under token limits, grounded answer generation, and deterministic hyperlink conversion for panel identifiers and cited documentation. The data preparation pipeline emphasizes semantic enrichment as the primary quality lever by adding table and field descriptions, normalizing acronyms across sources, and indexing representative row-level context when useful. A measured pilot shows consistent gains in retrieval quality and user outcomes. Precision at five improved from 0.56 to 0.72, mean reciprocal rank from 0.43 to 0.58, and normalized discounted cumulative gain (nDCG) at five from 0.51 to 0.66. Median task completion time dropped from 14.2 to 8.3 minutes, while usefulness and confidence both increased to 4.0 on a five-point scale. Results are based on a small sample and are reported as pilot findings, but they indicate that intent understanding and semantic enrichment can deliver meaningful operational value in legacy environments while also establishing reusable foundations for future analytics and automation tools.

</details>


### [223] [Retrieval, not hallucinations, will be the limiting factor for LLM-based clinical AI tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24793)
*Kirk Roberts,Steven Bedrick,Kurt Miller,William R. Hersh,Hongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出应关注临床AI中LLM的召回错误（而非仅关注精度错误如幻觉），讨论了错误类型、缓解策略、检索研究方向及评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前讨论多聚焦于LLM的精度错误（如幻觉），而召回错误（特别是患者级数据检索中的遗漏）同样重要但被忽视，因此需要转变关注视角。

Method: 通过综述视角，分类错误类型、提出缓解策略、梳理LLM与检索领域的研究方向，并概述检索评估方法。

Result: 系统性地指出了召回错误的潜在危害（如遗漏关键患者数据可能导致诊疗失误），并提出了相应的研究方向与评估框架。

Conclusion: 临床AI领域应平衡关注精度与召回错误，尤其需重视检索环节的召回问题，以提升LLM在临床场景中的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Discussions around large language model (LLM) errors in clinical artificial intelligence (AI) generally center around precision errors like hallucinations. This perspective, targeting both clinicians and AI researchers, seeks to shift that discussion to recall errors, particularly in retrieval of patient-level data needed for many clinical AI tools. The perspective outlines types of errors and mitigation strategies, describes research directions in LLMs and retrieval, and provides an overview of retrieval evaluation.

</details>


### [224] [Prediction Is Not Memory: Dual-Timescale Gated Profile Writing for Persistent User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24798)
*Ziyide Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性轮廓写入控制（SPW-Gate），解决推荐系统中用户持久轮廓的盲目更新问题。通过近/远离线评估协议，SPW-Gate利用长短期及候选轮廓漂移特征判断是否将交互写入持久记忆，在MicroLens-100K上减少22.45%的远未来对齐损害，同时保持77%写入覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统将每次交互都视为更新持久用户状态的证据，但许多交互（如临时上下文、探索行为、曝光事件）并不反映稳定的偏好形成，盲目写入会损害长期轮廓质量。

Method: 提出选择性轮廓写入控制框架，包含：(1) 近/远离线评估协议，用近期未来数据提供弱监督信号，远期未来数据用于评估；(2) SPW-Gate轻量级门控模型，利用长期、短期和候选轮廓漂移特征作为输入，判断是否将交互写入持久轮廓。

Result: 在MicroLens-100K数据集上，全写策略导致22.45%测试用例远未来轮廓对齐受损；SPW-Gate将损害降至约14.5%，同时保持约77%写入覆盖率。匹配覆盖率对照和预测置信度基线表明，收益不仅是写得更少的结果，且下一项置信度不足以作为持久写入有效性的代理指标。

Conclusion: 推荐系统需要将交互预测与持久化决策解耦，选择性轮廓写入控制能有效减少有害更新，且简单的置信度阈值无法替代专门的门控设计。

Abstract: Persistent user profiles increasingly serve as reusable memory in recommender systems, but common update pipelines conflate two decisions: predicting an interaction and deciding whether it should persist in the profile. After an interaction is observed, many systems treat it as evidence for updating durable user state. This assumption can be harmful when the event reflects transient context, exploration, exposure, or short-term satisfaction rather than stable preference formation. We formulate this boundary as selective profile-write control: after an observed interaction, a system should decide whether, and how strongly, to write it into the persistent profile. We introduce a chronological near/far offline protocol in which near-future evidence provides weak write-risk supervision and far-future evidence is reserved for evaluation. We instantiate the controller as SPW-Gate, a lightweight write-risk gate using long-term, short-term, and candidate-profile drift features. On MicroLens-100K, write-all updating hurts far-future profile alignment in 22.45% of test cases under the main protocol. SPW-Gate reduces far hurt to about 14.5% while preserving about 77% write coverage. Matched-coverage controls and prediction-confidence baselines show that the gain is not merely a by-product of writing less, and that next-item confidence is not a sufficient proxy for persistent write validity.

</details>


### [225] [Multimodal Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Scientific Document Understanding using Open-Source SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24799)
*Alexandru-Andrei Saucă,Ana-Luiza Rusnac*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高级多模态RAG系统，通过多模态摄入管道（Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct生成图表文本摘要）、HNSW语义搜索与GIN词汇搜索的混合检索、交叉编码器重排以及查询压缩器，在MMLongBench等基准上实现了比Naive-RAG提升157%的检索质量，仅增加50ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在回答领域特定科学文档问题时存在幻觉问题，现有RAG方法面临上下文知识有限、稀疏与稠密检索差异、检索噪声等挑战。

Method: 提出高级多模态RAG架构：(1) 多模态摄入管道，使用开源视觉语言模型Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct生成表格和图片的文本摘要；(2) 检索阶段融合HNSW语义搜索与GIN词汇搜索，通过RRF统一排序并利用交叉编码器重排降噪；(3) 引入查询压缩器保证多轮对话连贯性。

Result: 检索质量比Naive-RAG基线提升157%，仅增加50ms延迟；Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct在BERTScore上达到与云端模型可比的结果。

Conclusion: 开源优化的SLM结合高级检索策略，能在不依赖云端模型的情况下，为文档理解提供有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models tend to hallucinate when answering domain-specific ques tions from scientific documents without prior fine-tuning. Currently, methods such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation partially solve this problem but face different challenges: limited context knowledge, difference between sparse and dense retrieval, and retrieval noise. This paper presents an Advanced Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation system that aims to solve those challenges and im prove the accuracy of information extraction. The proposed architecture introduces a multimodal ingestion pipeline that leverages an open-source Vision-Language Model (Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct) to generate textual summaries of tables and fig ures. The retrieval phase integrates HNSW-based semantic search with GIN-based lexical search, unified through Reciprocal Rank Fusion and refined using Cross Encoder reranking to minimize retrieval noise. To ensure conversational coherence across multi-turn interactions, a Query Condenser module is employed. Evaluation is conducted by independently assessing the ingestion, retrieval and generation stages using the MMLongBench benchmark, a BeIR-format synthetic dataset and the DeepEval framework. Moreover, results demonstrate a 157% improvement in retrieval quality over a Naive-RAG baseline, with only 50 ms additional la tency, while Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct achieved results comparable to cloud-based models in BERTScore. These findings validate that open-source optimized SLMs, paired with advanced retrieval strategies, can provide competitive performance for document understanding without relying on cloud-based models.

</details>


### [226] [When Thinking Before Retrieval Hurts: TraceBound Diagnostics for Adaptive Knowledge-Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24800)
*Partha Sarathi Purkayastha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceBound诊断协议，研究自适应检索在知识图谱问答中的表现，发现增加条件控制虽提升可检查性但降低检索质量，诊断出重复调用、零结果调用和探索预算分配不当等失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应检索有望通过控制器搜索、检查邻域、修订动作和适时停止来提升知识图谱问答的鲁棒性，但该前提尚未得到充分验证，需要系统性的诊断方法。

Method: 提出TraceBound协议，一种轻量级的基于profile和trace条件的诊断方法，在检索前暴露紧凑查询profile，在观察失败症状后发出简短trace提示，并记录轨迹计数器，同时固定图数据、工具、金标签和排序指标。

Result: 在STaRK验证集和保留子集上，条件控制提升了可检查性但持续降低开放权重控制器的检索质量；成对轨迹分析将退化定位到重复调用、零结果调用和探索预算分配不当；更严格的交互预算缩短轨迹但未修复策略。

Conclusion: "检索前思考"必须被评估为动作选择上的控制问题，而非提示格式变更。

Abstract: Adaptive retrieval promises to make knowledge-graph question answering more robust by letting a controller search, inspect neighborhoods, revise actions, and stop when evidence is sufficient. We study this premise by introducing TraceBound, a lightweight profile- and trace-conditioned diagnostic protocol for an ARK-style retriever on text-rich knowledge graphs. TraceBound exposes a compact query profile before retrieval, issues short trace hints after observable failure symptoms, and logs trajectory counters, while keeping graph data, tools, gold labels, and ranking metrics fixed. Across STaRK validation and held-out subsets, the added conditioning improves inspectability but consistently reduces retrieval quality under open-weight controllers. Paired trajectory analysis localizes the degradation to repeated calls, zero-result calls, and misallocated exploration budget, while stricter interaction budgets shorten trajectories without repairing the policy. The result diagnoses the common failure mode in that "thinking before retrieval'' must be evaluated as a control problem over action selection, not as a prompt-format change.

</details>


### [227] [Influence of Prompt Engineering on Small Language Models for Guarded Query Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24801)
*Richard Šléher,William Brach,Kristián Košťál,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究受保护查询路由问题，评估22个小语言模型在延迟约束下联合处理分布内查询路由与分布外查询拒绝的能力。结果表明，中等规模SLM能以更低延迟接近前沿模型性能；提示优化技术可在不改变权重的情况下显著提升模型表现，其中Qwen3.5 9B经优化后得分达95.74，接近未优化的最强大型模型Gemma 3 27B的96.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询路由系统通常由大模型或专用分类器处理，但在延迟敏感场景下，小型语言模型能否同时胜任分布内查询路由和分布外查询拒绝两个任务尚不明确，且许多紧凑模型因输出格式不遵循而失败，需要探索无需权重调整的优化方案。

Method: 在GQR-Bench基准上评估22个开放权重小语言模型，使用分布内准确率和分布外准确率的调和平均数作为评分指标；采用多种提示优化技术（包括DSPy签名、少样本学习等）提升模型性能，不改变模型权重。

Result: 少样本提示优化使Mistral 7B从81.79提升至90.87 GQR-Score；Qwen3.5 9B经优化后达到95.74，接近未优化的最强大型模型Gemma 3 27B的96.01；DSPy签名对Granite 4 Tiny最有效，从54.29提升至83.05。

Conclusion: 提示优化是受保护查询路由任务的有效第一步，但较弱模型可能仍需权重级适配或模式感知训练才能进一步突破性能瓶颈。

Abstract: We study the problem of guarded query routing, where we assume that a user query first meets a router that either determines the ideal endpoint for in-distribution queries or rejects out-of-distribution queries that are potentially unsafe or out of the system's scope. We investigate whether compact open-weight Small Language Models (SLMs) can jointly handle both tasks under latency constraints. We evaluate 22 models on GQR-Bench and score them with the harmonic mean of in-distribution and out-of-distribution accuracy. We find that mid-scale SLMs come close to frontier model routing quality at much lower latency. Still, many compact models fail because they do not reliably follow the required output format. However, our results show that prompt optimization techniques enable SLMs to handle such cases gracefully, without changing the models' weights. Moreover, few-shot prompt optimization raises Mistral 7B from 81.79 to 90.87 GQR-Score and lifts Qwen3.5 9B to 95.74, the best optimized score in our study and within 0.3 points of the strongest unoptimized larger model: Gemma 3 27B at 96.01. The bare DSPy signature, without in-context exemplars, is the most effective strategy for Granite 4 Tiny, raising its score from 54.29 to 83.05. These results show that prompt optimization is a useful first step for guarded query routing, while weaker models may still need weight-level adaptation or schema-aware training

</details>


### [228] [SourceMinds at CheckThat! 2026: NLI-Grounded Citation Auditing in a Multi-Agent Pipeline for Full Fact-Checking Article Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24802)
*Farhan Sharukh Hasan,Anirban Saha Anik,Eric Liu,Xiaoying Song,Mohotarema Rashid,Lingzi Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab任务3的多智能体流水线，通过证据检索、结构化事实规划、文章生成、门控自我批评和NLI引文审计，生成带有引文支持的完整事实核查文章。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事实核查文章生成方法在证据选择和引文准确性方面存在不足，难以保证生成内容在事实上忠实于原始证据源。

Method: 采用多智能体流水线，包含五个核心模块：1) 基于稠密检索、重排序和源平衡选择的证据检索；2) 结构化事实规划；3) 文章生成；4) 门控自我批评阶段用于修正弱支撑的草稿；5) 基于NLI的引文审计器用于修复缺失引文、删除无支撑或冗余引文。

Result: 该方法在CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab任务3上取得了良好效果，验证了证据选择、结构化生成和后生成引文验证相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合证据检索、结构化生成和事后引文验证是提升源基事实核查文章生成质量的有效路径。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Lab, which focuses on generating full fact-checking articles from claims, veracity labels, and evidence documents. We propose a multi-agent pipeline that combines evidence retrieval, structured fact planning, article generation, gated self-critique, and NLI-based citation auditing. The system retrieves claim-relevant evidence using dense retrieval, reranking, and source-balanced selection, then generates a citation-supported article from a structured plan. A gated self-critique stage revises weakly grounded drafts, while the NLI citation auditor repairs missing citations and removes unsupported or redundant ones. The approach highlights the importance of combining evidence selection, structured generation, and post-generation citation validation for source-grounded fact-checking article generation.

</details>


### [229] [SciClaimSeekers at CheckThat! 2026: Retrieving Scientific Sources for Social Media Claims with LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24803)
*Mohotarema Rashid,Nansu Baniya,Anirban Saha Anik,Xiaoying Song,Lingzi Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SciClaimSeekers系统，结合BM25与零样本多语言E5检索及Reciprocal Rank Fusion（k=60），再经Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct进行逐点重排序，在CLEF-2026 CheckThat!任务1上达到64.39% MRR@5，相比BM25提升13.67个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学主张在社交媒体上的传播速度快于验证速度，且帖子很少链接到原始学术来源，因此需要一种能高效检索和排序相关科学论文的框架来辅助事实核查。

Method: 采用BM25与零样本多语言E5检索器进行初步检索，通过Reciprocal Rank Fusion（k=60）融合结果，再使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct进行逐点重排序，形成完整的检索-重排序流水线。

Result: 在英语开发集上达到64.36% MRR@5，比BM25提升13.67个点，比未排序的混合检索提升10.17个点；在官方测试集上达到64.39% MRR@5。

Conclusion: 精心组合的大型预训练模型流水线可以在该任务上与微调方法竞争。

Abstract: Scientific claims often spread on social media faster than they can be verified, while posts rarely link to the original scholarly sources. To tackle this problem this paper presents system called SciClaimSeekers, a retrieval and reranking framework by combining BM25 and zero-shot multilingual E5 retrieval with Reciprocal Rank Fusion (k=60), followed by Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct pointwise reranking. The pipeline reaches 64.36% MRR@5 on the English development set a 13.67-point jump over BM25 and 10.17 points over the unranked hybrid and 64.39% on the official test set, in the CLEF-2026 CheckThat! Task 1 evaluation. Our experiment suggests that large pre-trained models, when combined into a careful pipeline, can be competitive with fine-tuned approaches on this task.

</details>


### [230] [Bumblebee: Interleaved Mixed-Layer Building Blocks for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24804)
*David Bauer,Cancan Zhang,Wenshun Liu,Xiaoyi Zhang,Weijia Liu,Wanli Ma,Yue Weng,Wei Li,Rui Li,Jing Qian,Huayu Li,Xiaoyi Liu,Linhong Zhu,Jerry Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Bumblebee是一种新型推荐架构，通过交错堆叠的模块化设计（每个模块包含序列个性化、注意力编码和特征交叉的子管道），实现序列建模与特征交互的深度融合，在工业级数据上显著优于传统基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统在序列建模和特征交互两个方向上各自独立发展，缺乏两者之间的有效交互机制，限制了推荐性能的进一步提升。

Method: 提出Bumblebee架构，采用可堆叠的交错块设计，每个块内部实现序列个性化、注意力编码和特征交叉的微管道，输出联合表示传递给下一块；块间使用残差连接形成跨模态信息通路，且支持选择性丢弃组件以实现质量与吞吐量的灵活权衡。

Result: 在大型工业数据集上，Bumblebee在多个分类和回归任务中持续优于基线模型；消融实验确认交错组合本身是性能提升的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 交错异构功能单元而非单纯堆叠深度层，是未来推荐架构设计的 promising 范式。

Abstract: Recommendation systems have undergone significant transformations in the past years. The transition from traditional feature interaction modules to generative next-action prediction has pushed the boundaries of personalized content. Developments have largely evolved along two separate tracks. Sequence modeling approaches on the one hand and feature interaction methods on the other. In this paper, we introduce Bumblebee, a recommendation architecture that addresses the lack of interaction between the two directions through an interleaved, stackable block design. Each block implements a micro-pipeline of layers combining sequence personalization, attention-based encoding, and feature crossing into a self-contained unit. Every block produces a joint representation of both feature modalities which is consumed by the next block in the sequence. This mechanism encourages early and repeated mixture of modalities and enriches downstream features with additional contextual information. Residual connections between blocks create cross-modal information pathways and yield additional predictive performance without adding additional parameters. Blocks can be specialized by selectively dropping components, enabling flexible trade-offs between quality and throughput. We evaluate our approach on large-scale industrial data and show consistent improvements over comparable baseline models across several classification and regression tasks. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to confirm that the interleaved composition itself is the primary driver of these improvements. Our results suggest that interleaving heterogeneous functional units, rather than composing deep stacks, is a promising paradigm for future-generation recommendation architectures.

</details>


### [231] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation in LLMs for Mental Health: Quantifying the Incremental Contribution of Retrieval Within a Layered Safety Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24817)
*Anand Gupta,Akshat Surolia,Shubham Mishra,Shakil Imtiaz,Chaitali Sinha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究在Wysa数字心理健康干预系统中，对比评估了六种LLM模型在启用与禁用RAG模式下的意图检测效果。结果显示，RAG虽导致误报增加，但提升了检测灵敏度，符合安全关键系统的设计原则，可作为提升LLM驱动的DMHI准确性、一致性和安全性的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字心理健康干预（DMHI）需在用户情绪波动等不稳定情境下准确检测用户意图，而纯参数化的大语言模型缺乏安全关键架构，可能出现漏检或幻觉问题，影响可靠性。现有DMHI通常组合多种安全层，但各层的增量贡献尚未量化。

Method: 在Wysa平台中，对六种LLM模型进行RAG启用与禁用的对照实验。使用经临床团队标注的匿名真实及合成用户-聊天机器人对话数据，针对多类意图（如自伤、虐待、恐慌）计算分类准确率、召回率、精确率和F1分数，并进行统计显著性检验及风险类别和模型间一致性分析。

Result: RAG启用导致误报率上升，但这一权衡符合安全关键设计原则——优先保证灵敏度，被标记的案例会被转至人工复查而非直接执行。整体上，RAG提升了DMHI中LLM驱动的意图检测表现。

Conclusion: RAG是提升LLM驱动的数字心理健康干预系统准确性、一致性和安全性的有前景的方法，其误报增加在安全关键系统中可接受。

Abstract: Digital mental health interventions (DMHIs) offer scalable support, but ensuring they accurately detect users' intent during volatile situations can be challenging. Pure parametric Large Language models (LLMs) do not contain specific safety critical architecture, and can miss critical cues, or hallucinate, undermining reliability. Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), which supplements an LLM with retrieved context, could enhance intent detection during volatile situations. Commercially available DMHIs typically combine multiple independent safety layers like rule-based filters, symbolic escalation protocols, and neural classification. The incremental contribution of any single layer, however, remains unquantified. This paper evaluates six LLM models within a DMHI called Wysa, via a controlled comparison of RAG-enabled versus RAG-disabled modes. Anonymized real and synthetic user-chatbot exchanges were annotated by a qualified clinical team against multi-class intent categories (e.g. self-harm, abuse, panic). The study computed classification accuracy, recall, precision and F1 scores against ground truth labels and tested differences for statistical significance. Performance was also examined by risk category and inter-model agreement. While RAG caused a rise in false alarms, the trade-off is consistent with safety-critical design principles that prioritize sensitivity, where flagged cases are routed to additional review rather than acted on directly. Overall, these findings support RAG as a promising approach to improve the accuracy, consistency and safety of LLM-driven DMHIs.
  Keywords: Digital Mental Health Intervention, Large Language Model, Retrieval Augmented Generation, Accuracy, Recall, Precision

</details>


### [232] [Dual-Level Atomic and Coordination Geometry Learning for Crystal Property Prediction Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24818)
*Sanjay Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CPGN，一种多尺度图神经网络，通过显式建模配位多面体（原子、键、配位多面体三级图结构），在Materials Project、JARVIS-DFT和QM9基准数据集上实现了优于现有GNN（如CGCNN、MEGNet、ALIGNN、SchNet）的晶体性质预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN主要表征原子级别信息并通过消息传递隐式学习局部化学环境，但许多材料性质由配位多面体（原子及其邻居形成的基本结构单元）决定，因此需要显式建模配位多面体以提升预测性能和物理可解释性。

Method: CPGN构建三个耦合图：原子图（编码元素和键信息）、线图（捕捉角相互作用）和配位多面体图（通过Voronoi导出局部环境，描述角共享、边共享和面共享关系）。为每个多面体引入物理意义的几何描述符，并采用交错消息传递机制与双向交叉注意力实现跨结构层级的信息交换。

Result: 在Materials Project上形成能MAE达0.060 eV/atom、带隙MAE达0.292 eV；在JARVIS-DFT上实现竞争性多性质预测；在QM9上取得优越的HOMO预测性能，整体超越现有SOTA GNN模型。

Conclusion: 显式建模配位多面体能够改善晶体表示学习，实现准确且物理可解释的材料性质预测。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of crystal properties remains a key challenge in computational materials science. While graph neural networks (GNNs) such as CGCNN, MEGNet, ALIGNN, and SchNet have shown strong performance, they primarily represent crystals at the atomic level and implicitly learn local chemical environments through message passing. However, many material properties are governed by coordination polyhedra, the fundamental structural units formed by atoms and their neighboring atoms. To address this limitation, we propose the Coordination Polyhedron Graph Network (CPGN), a multi-scale GNN that jointly learns atomic, bond, and coordination-polyhedron representations. CPGN constructs three coupled graphs: an atom graph encoding elemental and bonding information, a line graph capturing angular interactions, and a coordination polyhedron graph describing Voronoi-derived local environments through corner-, edge-, and face-sharing relationships. Physically meaningful geometric descriptors are incorporated for each polyhedron, while an interleaved message-passing mechanism with bidirectional cross-attention enables effective information exchange across structural levels. Extensive evaluations on the Materials Project, JARVIS-DFT, and QM9 benchmark datasets demonstrate that CPGN outperforms existing state-of-the-art GNN models. It achieves a formation-energy MAE of 0.060 eV/atom and a band-gap MAE of 0.292 eV on the Materials Project, while providing competitive multi-property prediction on JARVIS-DFT and superior HOMO prediction on QM9. The results highlight that explicit modeling of coordination polyhedra improves crystal representation learning and enables accurate, physically interpretable prediction of material properties.

</details>


### [233] [A Position Paper on Recommender Systems in the Era of Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24822)
*Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 随着自主智能体的快速发展，推荐系统的消费者将从纯人类用户转变为人类与授权智能体的混合体。本文探讨了这一范式转变下的人-智能体-平台三方交互关系，并分析了评估、对齐和系统设计方面的新挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的推荐系统研究以人类用户为中心进行优化，但自主智能体的快速部署正在改变推荐系统的消费者构成。作者意识到未来推荐系统的使用者将是人类和代表人类行事的授权智能体的混合体，现有研究框架无法适应这一变化，因此需要重新审视推荐系统的研究范式。

Method: 本文采用立场论文（position paper）的形式，首先回顾了以往以人为中心的推荐系统研究（human-centric RecSys）的关键见解，在此基础上提出向人-智能体混合环境过渡的必要性和趋势。作者将智能体视为独立的、与用户目标一致的助手（而非平台内置组件），并以此构建了人类、智能体与平台之间的三方交互（tripartite interactions）分析框架，讨论这三者之间的动态关系。

Result: 文章揭示了混合推荐环境中的新动态关系特征，包括人类、智能体和平台之间可能出现的利益冲突与协同效应。同时指出了该范式转变带来的三个核心研究方向：评估方法（如何评价同时服务人类和智能体的推荐系统）、对齐问题（如何确保智能体行为真正符合用户意图）以及系统设计（如何构建适应混合用户的推荐架构）。

Conclusion: 推荐系统正面临从纯人类用户向人类与智能体混合用户的根本性转变，这带来了新的研究机遇与挑战。未来推荐系统研究需要重新思考评估框架、对齐机制和系统设计原则，以适应人-智能体-平台三方交互的新范式。

Abstract: For decades, recommender systems have been optimized to serve human users. However, the rapid deployment of autonomous agents introduces a paradigm shift: recommendation consumers are predicted to be increasingly a mixture of humans and authorized agents acting on their behalf. This position paper reviews insights from prior human-centric RecSys research and outlines the transition to this hybrid environment. In our discussion, we treat agents as independent, user-aligned assistants that act as end-users of recommender systems, rather than platform-built components. We characterize the resulting tripartite interactions among humans, agents, and platforms, highlighting the dynamics that can arise across these relationships. This shift presents new opportunities for RecSys research, while introducing unique challenges for evaluation, alignment, and system design.

</details>


### [234] [Retrieval-based and Fine-tuned LLM Approaches for Industrial Asset Health Monitoring and Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24824)
*Seshu Kumar Damarla,Xiuli Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索方法和开源大语言模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）在工业故障-传感器诊断推理任务中的表现，使用IBM Research提出的FailureSensorIQ基准。结果显示语义搜索和混合搜索优于纯关键词匹配，微调模型显著优于零样本和少样本提示，且所有方法对选项数量、选项顺序、标签变化、释义和额外干扰项均敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备故障诊断依赖工程师的经验，但将这种推理能力迁移到计算机系统中仍然困难。本文旨在探索检索方法和LLM在工业故障-传感器诊断推理中的可行性。

Method: 使用FailureSensorIQ多项选择问答基准，对比两类方法：1）检索方法（TF-IDF、BM25、语义搜索、混合搜索），通过将每个答案选项转化为选项级查询并与训练数据中的正确/错误记录进行评分；2）LLM方法（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct），采用零样本提示、少样本提示和QLoRA微调。

Result: 语义搜索和混合搜索优于纯关键词匹配；微调模型性能最佳，显著优于零样本和少样本提示；所有方法在选项增多时性能下降；所有方法对选项顺序打乱、标签改变、释义和额外干扰项敏感。

Conclusion: 基于语义的相似性对于工业故障-传感器推理比关键词匹配更重要；微调是提升LLM在此任务上性能的有效方式；当前方法在鲁棒性方面仍存在不足，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Industrial plants run many important machines such as pumps, turbines, and compressors. Although engineers can use their experience to identify and diagnose machine problems, transferring this reasoning ability to computer systems remains difficult. This work studies how well a retrieval-only method and an open-source large language model (LLM) perform failure-sensor diagnostic reasoning using the FailureSensorIQ benchmark, a multiple-choice question-answering task introduced by IBM Research. In the retrieval-only approach, each answer option is converted into an option-level query and scored using similar correct and incorrect records from the training data. TF-IDF, BM25, semantic search, and hybrid search are tested and compared. In the LLM-based approach, the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model is evaluated using zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, and QLoRA fine-tuning. The results show that semantic search and hybrid search perform better than pure keyword-matching techniques, indicating that meaning-based similarity is more important for industrial failure-sensor reasoning. Among the LLM-based methods, the fine-tuned model achieves the best performance and substantially improves over zero-shot and few-shot prompting. Error analysis shows that performance decreases as the number of answer options increases. Robustness analysis also shows that all methods are sensitive to option shuffling, changed labels, paraphrasing, and additional distractors.

</details>


### [235] [Aethel: A Reproducible Graph-Retrieval Framework for Multi-Hop Financial Diligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24826)
*Krish Sapru*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Aethel是一个结合二分图Personalized PageRank检索、共指感知二分图共指传送层和专家智能体架构的可复现框架，用于处理二级私募股权交易中分散、非结构化金融披露文档的多跳推理检索。在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上HR@5达88.5%和100.0%，但图检索的优势高度依赖语料规模和实体索引质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二级私募股权交易需要快速综合分散、非结构化的金融披露信息，关键指标及其实体锚点分布在缺乏词汇重叠的多个文档中，现有检索方法难以支持这种多跳金融尽职调查需求。

Method: Aethel框架由三部分组成：(1) 二分图Personalized PageRank图检索，将语料建模为实体-段落图，通过显式关系路径传播相关性；(2) 共指感知的二分图共指传送层，处理跨文档共指关系；(3) 编排式专家智能体架构。检索层在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上对比了稀疏词汇检索、密集双编码器、普通图检索和共指感知图检索。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA上HR@5达100.0%，MuSiQue上达88.5%，相比普通图检索提升了覆盖率但牺牲了顶部精度。在4,123块金融披露语料上，图检索在多跳召回上优于密集检索，但未超越强BM25基线。图检索提供了可解释的多跳证据路径，且随语料规模增长性能下降比密集检索更平缓。

Conclusion: 基于图的检索可提供可解释的多跳证据路径，且在大规模语料下退化更平缓，但其优势高度依赖语料规模和实体索引质量。代码和评估工件已开源以供复现。

Abstract: Secondary private equity transactions require rapid synthesis of fragmented, unstructured financial disclosures, where critical metrics and their entity anchors are distributed across disjoint documents with limited lexical overlap. We present Aethel, a reproducible framework that combines bipartite Personalized PageRank graph retrieval with a coreference-aware Bipartite Coreference Teleportation layer and an orchestrated specialist-agent architecture. Aethel models corpora as entity-passage graphs and propagates relevance through explicit relational paths to support multi-hop financial diligence. We evaluate the retrieval layer on 200-question samples from the MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA validation sets, comparing sparse lexical, dense bi-encoder, vanilla graph, and coreference-aware graph retrieval. Aethel achieves HR@5 of 100.0% on 2WikiMultiHopQA and 88.5% on MuSiQue, improving coverage over vanilla graph retrieval while trading off top-rank precision. We also evaluate retrieval over a 4,123-chunk corpus of financial disclosures and find that graph retrieval outperforms dense retrieval on multi-hop recall but does not surpass a strong BM25 baseline at open-corpus scale. The results show that graph-based retrieval offers interpretable multi-hop evidence paths and degrades more gracefully than dense retrieval as corpus size grows, while also demonstrating that its advantage depends strongly on corpus scale and entity-index quality. Code and evaluation artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [236] [Improving Rare Medication Recommendation with Counterfactual Data Augmentation and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24829)
*Shinhwan Kang,Soo Yong Lee,Jaewon Kim,Kijung Shin,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenRxR框架，利用大语言模型生成反事实医疗数据并建模共推荐药物关系，解决了罕见药物推荐中数据稀缺和协同药物建模不足的问题，在罕见药物预测性能上比最强基线提升高达30.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI药物推荐系统对罕见药物的预测性能显著较低，原因是：(a) 罕见药物本身数据稀缺，(b) 缺乏对共推荐药物关系的充分考虑。

Method: 提出GenRxR框架：(1) 利用LLM的医学知识和临床推理能力生成反事实医疗数据，缓解罕见药物数据稀缺；(2) 将LLM集成到药物推荐过程中，建模共推荐药物间的关系；(3) 引入指令微调步骤，对齐LLM能力与推荐任务，增强对临床上下文（包括罕见药物案例）的处理能力。

Result: GenRxR在大多数情况下优于14个基线（包括5个基于LLM的），在罕见药物预测性能上比最强基线提升高达30.9%。

Conclusion: GenRxR通过LLM生成反事实数据和建模药物共推荐关系，有效解决了罕见药物推荐的低性能问题，显著提升了预测准确性。

Abstract: AI-based medication recommendation systems have attracted substantial attention due to their potential to enhance patient safety and therapeutic outcomes. Despite the clinical importance of accurately recommending rarely prescribed medications (rare-meds), we observe that most existing methods show significantly lower predictive performance for rare-meds. We attribute this issue to two intrinsic limitations: (a) the inherent scarcity of data for rare-meds and (b) limited consideration of co-recommended medications. To address these limitations, we propose GenRxR, a novel framework based on large language models (LLMs). GenRxR leverages the medical knowledge and clinical reasoning capability of LLMs to generate counterfactual medical data, mitigating the data scarcity issue for rare-meds. It also integrates an LLM into the medication recommendation process to model relationships among co-recommended medications. To further enhance the clinical reasoning, we introduce an instruction tuning step that aligns the LLM's capability with the recommendation task, enabling better handling of clinical context, including rare-meds cases. In our experiments, we show that GenRxR outperforms 14 (including 5 LLM-based) baselines in most cases. Specifically, it achieves up to 30.9% higher predictive performance for rare-meds than the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [237] [MedJudgeRAG: Option-Wise Evidence Judgment with Dynamic Knowledge Graphs for Medical MCQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24838)
*Seongwon Seo,Seung Hwan Cho,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MedJudgeRAG提出利用动态知识图谱增强检索文档的表示，通过结构化推理轨迹进行监督微调，让模型对每个选项进行证据判断并选择知识利用策略，从而在医学多项选择问答中优于传统RAG和参数化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG在医学多项选择问答中不加区分地使用所有检索文档，可能引入噪声信息从而降低语言模型性能，需要更智能的知识利用机制。

Method: 将检索文档构建为动态知识图谱（实体和关系），模型对每个选项从检索文档和知识图谱中判断证据裁决，基于裁决组合确定知识利用策略以推理出最终答案。通过教师语言模型生成的结构化推理轨迹进行监督微调，使用加权交叉熵损失对知识图谱和推理片段进行差异化加权。

Result: 在两个医学MCQA基准上，MedJudgeRAG一致优于vanilla RAG和纯参数基线方法；消融分析表明动态知识图谱在训练时作为图条件监督比在推理时作为显式输出更有效。

Conclusion: 动态知识图谱与结构化推理轨迹相结合的方法能有效提升医学问答中检索增强生成的质量，且训练阶段的图条件监督是更优的知识融合方式。

Abstract: In medical multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can supplement the domain knowledge of language models (LMs). However, since vanilla RAG indiscriminately utilizes retrieved documents, it can degrade LM performance. To address this, we propose MedJudgeRAG. Our framework represents retrieved documents as a dynamic knowledge graph (KG) composed of entities and relations. For each option, the model judges an evidence verdict from the retrieved documents and the KG. Based on the verdict combination, the model determines a knowledge utilization strategy to reason toward the final answer. These capabilities are trained via supervised fine-tuning using structured reasoning traces generated by a teacher LM. The training employs a weighted cross-entropy loss that differentially weights the KG and reasoning segments. Experiments on two medical MCQA benchmarks demonstrate that MedJudgeRAG consistently outperforms both vanilla RAG and parametric baselines. Furthermore, ablation analysis reveals that the dynamic KG is more effective as graph-conditioned supervision at training time than as an explicit output at inference time. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyu-amllab/medjudgerag, and the generated reasoning traces are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/youarethewon/medjudgerag.

</details>


### [238] [REPREC: Representation Driven Parameter-Efficient Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24845)
*Harshini Kavuru,Dwipam Katariya,Giri Iyengar,Pranab Mohanty,Kalanand Mishra,Kalanand Mishra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: REPREC提出一种轻量级框架，通过固定大小的用户嵌入映射为少量软token，仅训练一个MLP注入器即可冻结LLM和序列编码器，在无需微调或额外模块的情况下，在顺序推荐中超越LoRA，并实现1.51倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的顺序推荐方法依赖LLM微调、额外架构模块、表征蒸馏或长历史逐项条件化，导致训练复杂度和部署成本过高。

Method: 将冻结的序列编码器输出的固定大小用户嵌入，通过轻量级MLP注入器映射为一组可学习的软token，作为冻结LLM的输入条件。仅训练MLP注入器，不修改预训练骨干网络。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上一致优于LoRA，对普通用户和核心用户提升显著；在短历史训练、长历史评估场景下保持LoRA 85-100%的性能，且每轮训练时间平均减少1.51倍。

Conclusion: REPREC实现了模块化、生产友好的推荐流水线，无需修改预训练组件即可在推荐质量与计算效率之间取得有效平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been applied to sequential recommendation by formulating it as a natural language task. Previous work has improved personalization by incorporating collaborative and sequential signals through input conditioning or LLM fine-tuning. However, existing approaches often rely on one or more of the following: LLM fine-tuning, additional architectural modules, representation distillation, or item-level conditioning over long interaction histories, increasing training complexity and deployment cost. We propose REPREC, a lightweight framework that reformulates LLM-based sequential recommendation through lightweight user representation alignment. REPREC maps a fixed-size user embedding from a frozen sequential encoder into a small set of learned soft tokens through a lightweight MLP injector that conditions a frozen LLM, leaving both pretrained backbones unchanged while training only the injector. We conducted exhaustive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets and demonstrate that REPREC consistently outperforms LoRA while remaining compatible with different pretrained sequential encoders and LLM backbones, enabling a modular and production-friendly recommendation pipeline without modifying either pretrained component. The gains are particularly pronounced for casual and core users across all datasets, highlighting REPREC's effectiveness in low-data regimes. Finally, when trained on short prompt histories and evaluated with longer contexts, REPREC maintains 85-100% of LoRA's performance while reducing per-epoch training time by an average of 1.51X, demonstrating an effective balance between recommendation quality and computational efficiency for production deployment. The code is available at https://github.com/phdbotcode/REPREC

</details>


### [239] [Two Views, One Voice: Evidence-Grounded Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24846)
*Sungwook Yoo,Sewook Yoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出将检索与响应生成解耦的流水线架构，通过混合检索（词法-密集+任务自适应）、LightGBM校准和PAS证据框架，在ACM RecSys Challenge 2026中取得第三名，并在解释质量榜单位列第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话式推荐系统将检索和响应生成纠缠在同一文本接口中，导致实体线索随对话意图演变而模糊，降低了解释的可信度。

Method: 解耦为独立流水线：检索阶段采用混合词法-密集池进行精确匹配，结合基于微调Qwen 8B适配器的任务自适应池；通过LightGBM校准候选项目；最后利用基于证据的"提议-分配-选择"（PAS）框架构建结构化响应。

Result: 在ACM RecSys Challenge 2026 Blind-B行业赛道中获得第三名，并在最终盲评的解释质量排行榜上排名第二。

Conclusion: 隔离检索与响应生成能同时保留目录线索和流畅意图；通过显式证据分配来结构化生成过程是实现接近最佳解释可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Traditional conversational recommenders entangle retrieval and response generation within a single text interface, so exact entity cues fade as the dialogue's intent evolves, which compromises explanation credibility. We address this within the ACM RecSys Challenge 2026, which mandates both top-20 ranking and evidence-grounded response generation. This paper presents the third-place solution by team "swyoo" for the Blind-B industry track. We decouple retrieval and response into separate pipelines connected strictly via ranked tracks and metadata. Retrieval combines a hybrid lexical-dense pool for exact matching with a task-adapted pool driven by fine-tuned Qwen 8B adapters. Candidates are calibrated via LightGBM, then routed to an evidence-grounded propose-assign-select (PAS) framework to structure responses. This system also ranked second on the explanation-quality leaderboard in the final blind evaluation. Our findings demonstrate that: (i) isolating retrieval and response preserves both catalog cues and fluid intent; (ii) structuring generation via explicit evidence assignment is key to this near-best-in-class explanation reliability.

</details>


### [240] [SearchArt: Training Long-Horizon Search Agent with Scalable Synthetic and Verified Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24850)
*Lang Mei,Xiaohan Yu,Chong Chen,Liyan Liu,Xiangnan Chen,Jinchao Ma,Chao Feng,Li Huang,Siyu Mo,Sichen Kang,Yunkun Xu,Zhihan Yang,Zhujun Xue,Jingren Zhang,Qing He,Yingdi Huang,Hao Jiang,Ziao Ma,Zewei Pan,Minhao Sun,Zhuo Tao,Jinzhao Xiao,Gangtao Xin,Huanyao Zhang,Wenjian Zhang,Jiangshan Zhang,Guojie Zhu,Jiaxin Mao,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchArt提出了一种可扩展的框架，通过验证驱动的任务合成和多阶段后训练流水线，训练具备长程搜索与推理能力的搜索智能体。仅用27B参数模型，在BrowseComp、Deepresearch-bench等基准上达到或超越前沿闭源智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的搜索智能体在长程、复杂任务中缺乏可扩展的训练数据，且难以评估和纠正中间推理与工具使用行为。

Method: 1) 从网络文档和自动生成的证据图中合成多样化的信息寻求QA对及搜索轨迹；2) 设计验证流水线联合评估QA一致性、轨迹质量和检索证据相关性；3) 多阶段训练：监督微调 + 基于强化学习的策略优化。

Result: Qwen3.5-27B参数模型在BrowseComp-ZH上74.39分，BrowseComp上70.06分，Deepresearch-bench上52.55分，匹配或超越前沿闭源智能体。

Conclusion: 验证驱动的合成数据与多阶段训练能有效提升搜索智能体的自适应搜索规划、迭代证据聚合和复杂推理能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled search agents to autonomously tackle complex tasks across extended search and reasoning horizons. However, training effective search agents remains challenging due to the lack of scalable and long-horizon tasks, and the difficulty of evaluating and correcting intermediate reasoning and tool-use behaviors. We introduce SearchArt, a scalable framework for training long-horizon search agents through verification-driven task synthesis and a multi-stage post-training pipeline. SearchArt constructs large-scale datasets for complex search-, research- and user-oriented tasks by synthesizing diverse information-seeking QA pairs and corresponding search trajectories from web documents and automatically generated evidence graphs. To ensure the reliability of the synthesized data, we design a verification pipeline that jointly evaluates QA consistency, trajectory quality, and the relevance of retrieved evidence. The verified trajectories are subsequently used in a multi-stage training process comprising supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning-based policy optimization. Search agents trained with SearchArt exhibit adaptive search planning, iterative evidence aggregation, and complex reasoning over extended interaction horizons. Experimental results demonstrate that, with only (Qwen3.5-) 27B parameters, SearchArt scores 74.39 on BrowseComp-ZH, 70.06 on BrowseComp, and 52.55 on Deepresearch-bench, matching or surpassing frontier closed-source agents on both deepsearch and deepresearch benchmarks.

</details>


### [241] [HVM-GraphRAG: Holistic-View Multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Complex Document](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24861)
*Xin He,Yili Wang,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Qinggang Zhang,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HVM-GraphRAG提出了一种整体视图的多模态GraphRAG框架，通过概念级图构建和索引直接访问证据，避免密集实体级图的遍历，并在检索后将多模态块重组以增强异构证据整合，在三个数据集上取得了最佳性能并显著提升了检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在跨模态证据索引上不可靠，且图遍历成本高昂，亟需一种可靠的索引和高效检索的多模态图RAG方法。

Method: 提出HVM-GraphRAG框架：1）利用整体视图指导图构建，减少噪声和冲突，建立概念级图节点与多模态块之间的可靠索引；2）检索时在紧凑的概念级图上搜索，通过索引直接访问证据，避免实体级图的昂贵遍历；3）检索后将多模态块重组为模态特定组，便于模型整合异构证据。

Result: 在三个数据集上，HVM-GraphRAG在大多数评估设置中取得最佳答案性能，并显著提升在线检索效率。

Conclusion: HVM-GraphRAG通过整体视图引导的概念级图构建和索引机制，有效解决了跨模态证据索引不可靠和图遍历成本高的问题，为复杂文档问答提供了高效且有效的新范式。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over complex documents requires models to retrieve and integrate evidence distributed across distant document regions and modalities. Multimodal GraphRAG provides a promising direction by organizing document evidence with graph structures. However, existing methods often suffer from unreliable cross-modal evidence indexing and expensive graph traversal. To address these issues, we propose HVM-GraphRAG, a holistic-view multimodal GraphRAG framework on complex document. HVM-GraphRAG uses a holistic view to guide graph construction, thereby reducing noisy and conflicting graph updates and building reliable indices between concept-level graph nodes and supporting multimodal chunks. During retrieval, HVM-GraphRAG searches over a compact concept-level graph and directly accesses supporting evidence through the constructed index, avoiding costly traversal over dense entity-level graphs. After obtaining the retrieved evidence, HVM-GraphRAG further reorganizes chunks into modality-specific groups, enabling the answering model to better integrate heterogeneous evidence. Experiments on three datasets show that HVM-GraphRAG achieves the best answer performance in most evaluated settings while substantially improving online retrieval efficiency over representative graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [242] [KuaiLive-M3: A Multi-Modal, Multi-Domain, and Multi-Feedback Dataset for Live Streaming Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24862)
*Ke Guo,Changle Qu,Jiayaqi Cheng,Xiao Zhang,Shijun Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Xueliang Wang,Le Zhang,Lantao Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了KuaiLive-M3，一个多模态、多领域、多反馈的直播推荐数据集，包含21938名用户、3500万次直播交互和1.11亿次短视频交互，以及8800万个时间戳级别的分段时间多模态嵌入和25403份问卷反馈，解决了现有直播数据集在时间动态性、跨域交互和显式反馈方面的三大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直播数据集存在三大局限：无法提供时间演化的多模态直播内容、忽略用户跨域（短视频与直播）交互、仅含隐式行为信号缺乏显式反馈，无法真实反映实际直播推荐场景，阻碍了直播推荐研究的全面开展。

Method: 从快手平台收集大规模真实交互数据，构建包含多模态分段嵌入（提取直播内容的时序演化特征）、跨域行为数据（短视频+直播）以及问卷反馈（连接隐式行为与显式偏好）的数据集，并基于这些信号建立了跨域推荐、直播高光预测和问卷增强推荐三个基准任务。
